[[sec-017-smu_axi_in_burst_outstanding_test]]
===== 17 `smu_axi_in_burst_outstanding_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_axi_in_burst_outstanding_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip` master on the flat `ext_in_*` pins; `ocah_jtag_vip` to open the INBOUND0 window; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The inbound SMN port carries several transfers in flight and refuses the burst types the register targets do not support.

*Test Coverage*:

* With the SMC local aperture routed and an INBOUND0 window opened over JTAG2AXI, sixty-four reads of `VERSION_LO` under distinct ARIDs are launched while RREADY is held low. The AR handshakes the port accepts before its first R handshake are counted on the `ext_in_*` pins, and at least two must be accepted: a port that serialises accepts one and fails here. Every read must then return the VERSION_LO RDL reset value with OKAY, so a dropped transfer fails on its bounded wait.
* Sixty-four writes under one AWID are launched against `SCRATCH_COLD` while BREADY is held low; the AW handshakes accepted before the first B handshake are counted the same way and at least two are required. The last value written is what a later read returns, which is the ordering AXI requires of same-ID transfers.
* No specification document states the inbound port's outstanding depth (`doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc` "SMU Default Parameters" sizes no such queue), so the floor of two is a DV-owned rule and the accepted count the port reaches is logged with each depth check rather than compared against a stated depth.
* A four-beat 32-bit INCR burst read from VERSION_LO, covering the four CHIP_CONFIG registers, and one written to the first four SCRATCH_COLD words are OKAY, and a WRAP burst read and a FIXED burst write of the same address, length and size are refused with SLVERR, so the refusal follows the burst type and not the length.
* Spec gap: no SMC chapter states how the register path answers a non-incrementing or a multi-beat INCR burst (`hw/sys/smc/doc/fabric.adoc` names only the decode error of a deny entry); the SLVERR and OKAY expectations are the documentation item.
* S3 takes the same window into the SMC SPM (`SMC_TOP_SPM_MEMORY_BASE_ADDR`/`_SIZE` in `smc_addr.h`): every AxSIZE with each of address bits [2:0] set and cleared between transfers, and a 32-bit write and read at every address bit the SPM leaves free, read back what was written; an eight-beat INCR burst reads back its beats, and a WRAP write followed by an INCR write complete, the INCR one OKAY.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Route the SMC local aperture and open an INBOUND0 window over JTAG2AXI, checking each write's status.
. Arm an AR/R accept counter on the `ext_in_*` pins, hold RREADY low, issue sixty-four reads of `VERSION_LO` with distinct ARIDs; require at least two AR handshakes before the first R handshake, then compare each data word and response.
. Arm an AW/B accept counter, hold BREADY low, issue sixty-four writes to `SCRATCH_COLD` under one AWID; require at least two AW handshakes before the first B handshake, then read the register back and compare against the last value written.
. Issue an INCR burst read and write at the register targets and require OKAY, then a WRAP burst read and a FIXED multi-beat burst write of the same shape and require SLVERR on each.
. Write and read the SPM at every size cell and address-walk cell, write and read an INCR burst, then issue a WRAP write and an INCR write.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-AXIIN-DEPTH` |the inbound port accepts at least two address handshakes before it returns the first response, on the read train and on the write train, and every read returns the VERSION_LO RDL reset value with OKAY
|`CHK-AXIIN-DEPTH-ORDER` |sixty-four inbound writes in flight under one AWID leave the last value written in SCRATCH_COLD
|`CHK-AXIIN-BURST` |an inbound INCR burst read and write at the register targets are OKAY, and a WRAP burst read of the same shape is refused with SLVERR
|`CHK-AXIIN-BURST-FIXED` |an inbound multi-beat FIXED burst write at a register target is refused with SLVERR
|`CHK-AXIIN-SPM-SWEEP` |every SPM size and address cell reads back OKAY with the bytes written, the INCR burst reads back its beats, and the INCR write after the WRAP write is OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `fabric`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-018-smu_xbar_connectivity_matrix_test]]
===== 18 `smu_xbar_connectivity_matrix_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xbar_connectivity_matrix_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip` master on the flat `ext_in_*` pins; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: An `ext_in` access that matches neither programmed aperture is answered DECERR by the crossbar and never reaches `ext_out`.

*Test Coverage*:

* The rule is the integrator guide's (`doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc`, "SMU AXI Crossbar Address Map": unmatched inbound requests return a decode error and do not reach `ext_out`); the SEP and SMC apertures are read live from the DUT to pick an address outside both.
* A read and two writes are issued, the second write with every AWID bit set; the crossbar itself must answer DECERR with the issued IDs, and the SMN egress boundary counters must not move.
* SMU-XBAR-CONN.S5 and S6 (the SEP and SMC self-routes) need SEP or SMC firmware that targets its own aperture and are not claimed here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Read the live SEP and SMC aperture registers and choose a candidate address outside both.
. Sample the `ext_out` egress boundary counters, issue a read and two writes at that address, the second with the all-ones AWID, and require DECERR with the issued IDs.
. Re-sample the egress counters after a settle and require them unchanged.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-XBAR-CONN-S7` |unmatched ext_in read and writes, including the all-ones AWID, DECERR with the issued IDs and nothing reaches ext_out
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-019-smu_periph_ext_window_test]]
===== 19 `smu_periph_ext_window_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_periph_ext_window_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC fabric JTAG2AXI); `ocah_axi_vip` for the out-of-chiplet leg; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The SMC adopter external window is reachable over JTAG2AXI at the addresses the SMC memory map and the register map generated from `smc.rdl` give, answers at the two blocks those sources place, returns DECERR on the first word past the straps pair, which no block claims, and shrinks with the SMC aperture.

*Test Coverage*:

* EFUSE_SHIM_CTRL.EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME, at the EFUSE_SHIM_CTRL block base the generated SMC map gives, is read at its RDL reset value over the eFuse bank-control AXI-Lite port, written, read back and restored, so the shim window is proven both ways.
* The straps pair in the mandatory region answers, and the first word past it, which no block claims, returns DECERR.
* REGION_SIZE shrunk so the local aperture ends exactly at the window sends the same shim read out of the chiplet instead, observed on the outbound boundary rather than inferred from the response alone. REGION_SIZE zero is not driven: `fabric.adoc` forbids firmware from programming it, and the crossbar's address decoder assumes every rule spans at least one byte, so a zero-size aperture is outside the design's contract rather than a state to prove.
* Golden sources: the window base and size from `hw/sys/smc/regs/gen/c/smc_addr.h` (`SMC_TOP_SMC_EXTERNAL_*`) and the mandatory / supplementary split from `hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc` ("AXI-Lite External Window"); the EFUSE_SHIM_CTRL block base, both region bases and the allocated extent from the same `smc_addr.h`; the EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME offset and reset from the PeakRDL headers of `hw/ip/efuse/dv/models/regs/efuse_shim_ctrl.rdl`; the straps addresses transcribed from `hw/sys/smc/bootrom/prod/doc/hardware-initialization.adoc` ("Straps and eFuses"), with `straps.rdl` fixing STRAPS_HI at STRAPS_LO + 4; the REGION_SIZE and LOCAL_BASE resets from `smc_base_config.h`. The sequence refuses to run if these sources disagree about the window.
* Specification gap: `hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc` leaves every placement inside the window to the adopter and `hw/ip/efuse/doc/memmap.adoc` says the SHIM sits at no fixed address, so the shim base rests on the reference placement in `smc_addr.h` (only the block base is taken from it, and the register inside follows `efuse_shim_ctrl.rdl`) and the straps address on the boot-ROM documentation. Neither is an SMC memory-map statement. The map passes the unallocated remainder through to the adopter external port without saying what answers it; `hw/sys/smu/doc/port_table.adoc` ties an unused port to DECERR, and the DV rule is that an address no source allocates must not be answered by a live register. `CHK-PERIPH-EXT-UNMAPPED` is graded against that rule, not against a decode boundary a document states.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Over JTAG2AXI read, write, read back and restore EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME at the EFUSE_SHIM_CTRL base the generated SMC map gives.
. Read STRAPS_LO and STRAPS_HI at the boot-ROM documented address, then the first word past them, and compare the responses.
. Shrink REGION_SIZE so LOCAL_BASE + size ends at the window and repeat the shim read; require the transaction on the outbound boundary.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-PERIPH-EXT-SHIM` |EFUSE_SHIM_CTRL.EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME, at the block base the generated SMC map gives, reads its RDL reset value over the eFuse bank-control AXI-Lite port, takes a written value and is restored
|`CHK-PERIPH-EXT-STRAPS` |STRAPS_LO and STRAPS_HI at the boot-ROM documented mandatory-region address both answer SUCCESS
|`CHK-PERIPH-EXT-UNMAPPED` |the first word past the straps pair, which no block claims, DECERRs (DV rule; see the specification gap above)
|`CHK-PERIPH-EXT-APERTURE` |REGION_SIZE shrunk so the local aperture ends at the window sends the same shim read out of the chiplet instead
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `smc_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-129-smu_alias_cache_reset_test]]
===== 129 `smu_alias_cache_reset_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_alias_cache_reset_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_alias_cache_reset_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC JTAG2AXI bridge, with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); `ocah_axi_vip` master on `smu_axi_in` for the alias and inbound filter registers; the outbound interface `u_axi_out_if` (`awvalid`, `awready`, `awaddr`, `awcache`, `arvalid`, `arready`, `araddr`, `arcache`) sampled on `clk_smu_i`; SMC aperture GLOBAL_BASE and LOCAL_BASE; inbound filter entry 0 start, end and config; alias remap region 0 start, end and attrs; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: with every alias region invalid the SMU passes an outbound access and the local alias window through unchanged, an alias hit carries the programmed cacheable value as AxCACHE on both channels, and the AxCACHE of an access that misses every region is measured.

*Test Coverage*:

* With every alias region invalid, a JTAG2AXI write and read to `0x8000_0000` leave on `smu_axi_out` at the same address, and a read of CHIP_CONFIG VERSION_LO through the local alias returns SUCCESS with the `VERSION_LO` reset value of `hw/sys/smc/regs/blocks/chip_config/chip_config.rdl`; transparent mapping at reset is stated in `hw/sys/smc/doc/fabric.adoc` ("Alias Remapping").
* For each REGION_ATTRS.cacheable value 0 to 15, with alias region 0 valid over a window the leaf programs and its offset set to reach `0x8000_0000`, a JTAG2AXI write and read to the window leave on `smu_axi_out` with AWCACHE and ARCACHE equal to that value; `hw/ip/axi_alias_remap/regs/alias_remap.rdl` states that the field overrides all existing AxCACHE bits.
* The bridge's own AxCACHE is measured on `smu_axi_out` and logged (2 in the kept runs). The sweep shows each programmed cacheable value on both channels and, for the values that clear a bit the bridge sets, that the field replaces the initiator AxCACHE rather than OR-ing into it. The bridge value itself has no documented source, so the miss leg is logged and not compared.
* With region 0 invalid again, a write and read to an address that misses every region are issued and their AxCACHE on `smu_axi_out` is logged beside the bridge's own; the miss leg is measured and not compared, because no document gives the AxCACHE the SMC JTAG2AXI bridge issues (`hw/sys/smc/doc/fabric.adoc` says only that a miss keeps the AxCACHE its initiator issued).
* Every bounded wait (JTAG2AXI status polls, `smu_axi_in` responses, the take-effect poll of the attrs register and the outbound observation) records its last state, and none expires.
* The inbound filter entry 0 the leaf opens over the alias register page is a stimulus path; the filter decision itself is out of scope here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, program SMC GLOBAL_BASE to LOCAL_BASE over JTAG2AXI and read it back, read the IDCODE, require the JTAG2AXI gate open and create the `smu_axi_in` master.
. With every alias region invalid, write and read `0x8000_0000` over JTAG2AXI while capturing `smu_axi_out`, log the bridge's AWCACHE and ARCACHE, read VERSION_LO at the local alias, and compare address, status and data.
. Program inbound filter entry 0 over JTAG2AXI to admit the alias register page for read and write, and read the config back.
. Clear alias region 0, program its start, end and offset, then for cacheable 0 to 15 program the value over `smu_axi_in`, poll the attrs register until it reads back, and capture the AWCACHE and ARCACHE of a JTAG2AXI write and read to the window.
. Compare the sixteen captured pairs with their cacheable values, and log the cacheable 0 pair beside the measured bridge AxCACHE.
. Invalidate region 0, write and read an address that misses every region, log the captured pair beside the measured bridge value, and restore region 0 to its reset values.
. Log the last state of every bounded wait and require none expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ALIAS-RESET-TRANSPARENT` |with every alias region invalid the outbound address equals the issued address for the write and the read and the VERSION_LO read at the local alias returns SUCCESS with its reset value
|`CHK-ALIAS-CACHE-HIT` |for each cacheable value 0 to 15 an alias hit carries that value as AWCACHE and as ARCACHE on `smu_axi_out`
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |none of the bounded JTAG2AXI, `smu_axi_in`, take-effect and outbound waits expires
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-130-smu_filter_cfg_register_map_test]]
===== 130 `smu_filter_cfg_register_map_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_filter_cfg_register_map_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_filter_cfg_register_map_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC JTAG2AXI bridge, with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); `ocah_axi_vip` master on `smu_axi_in` for every filter register access after setup; SMC aperture GLOBAL_BASE and LOCAL_BASE; inbound filter entry 0, which the leaf programs over JTAG2AXI to admit the window from the inbound filter instance base through the end of the outbound instance; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: the FILTER_CONFIG, START_ADDR and END_ADDR registers of all 16 entries of both the inbound and the outbound filter instance reset, store, read back and address as `filter_ctrl.rdl` and the SMC register map specify.

*Test Coverage*:

* Every entry of both instances reads FILTER_CONFIG at its reset value, which is the `data_bus_width` reset of `hw/ip/axi_filter/regs/filter_ctrl.rdl` with every other field 0, and START_ADDR 0x0 and END_ADDR 0x7 as the same RDL gives; inbound entry 0 is read before the leaf programs it for the window.
* On entry 14 of each instance, each single-bit read-write field bit of FILTER_CONFIG (`read_allowed`, `write_allowed`, `entry_enabled`, `allow_ns`, `src_id`, `group_id`, `allow_burst`) written alone reads back set, with `data_bus_width` still at its reset value; the RDL marks `data_bus_width` read-only and an all-ones write to its field still reads the reset value.
* The `locked` bit written through the high half of FILTER_CONFIG of entry 14 reads back 1 on both instances; the RDL makes it write-once. The write responses are logged and not compared, and later writes to the locked entry are not driven.
* On entry 13 of each instance, START_ADDR and END_ADDR store and read back all of bits [55:0] for an all-ones write and for each single-bit write, as the RDL fields `start_addr[55:0]` and `end_addr[55:0]` give. Bits [63:56] of an all-ones write, and the FILTER_CONFIG bits the RDL defines no field for, are logged as read and never compared.
* Entries other than entry 0 of the inbound instance and entry 14 receive a value unique to their instance, register and entry, and each reads back at its own address only; the entry stride is 0x20 and the instance bases are the generated SMC register map's (`hw/sys/smc/regs/smc.rdl`, `hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc`), which the leaf cross-checks against the generated header before use.
* Each 64-bit register read as two 32-bit halves equals the 64-bit value, and a write of one half of START_ADDR on entry 13 leaves the other half unchanged.
* GROUP_ID written 0xA on entry 13 of each instance reads back 0xA.
* The granule rounding and match semantics of the range fields (`hw/ip/axi_filter/doc/index.adoc`, "Address Range Granule") are out of scope: no transaction is driven through a programmed entry here, and the leaf programs ranges that do not match any transaction it sends.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, program SMC GLOBAL_BASE to LOCAL_BASE over JTAG2AXI, read the IDCODE, require the JTAG2AXI gate open, read inbound entry 0 at reset, program inbound entry 0 to admit the filter register window, read its config back and create the `smu_axi_in` master.
. Read FILTER_CONFIG, START_ADDR and END_ADDR of all 16 entries of both instances as two 32-bit halves each and compare them with the reset values.
. On entry 14, program a range that matches nothing, write each read-write FILTER_CONFIG bit alone and read it back, write all ones to the `data_bus_width` field and read it back, clear the entry, then write the `locked` bit through the high half and read the config back.
. On entry 13, write and read back all-ones, then each single bit of START_ADDR and END_ADDR, log the bits above bit 55 and the undefined FILTER_CONFIG bits, and clear the entry.
. Write a unique value into every register of every other entry, read all entries back as halves and compare with the expected stride map, write one half of START_ADDR on entry 13 and read both halves, then restore the entries.
. Write 0xA to `group_id` on entry 13 of each instance, read it back and clear it.
. Log the last state of the JTAG2AXI and AXI waits and require none expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CFG-RESET-CONFIG` |FILTER_CONFIG of all 32 entries reads the reset value
|`CHK-CFG-RESET-RANGE` |START_ADDR reads 0x0 and END_ADDR reads 0x7 on all 32 entries
|`CHK-CFG-RW-FIELDS` |a one-hot write of each read-write FILTER_CONFIG field bit reads back with `data_bus_width` at its reset value, and `locked` reads 1 after it is written
|`CHK-CFG-DBW-RO` |an all-ones write to the `data_bus_width` field of FILTER_CONFIG still reads the reset value on both instances
|`CHK-CFG-ADDR-56` |START_ADDR and END_ADDR bits [55:0] store and read back an all-ones value and a single-bit walk on both instances
|`CHK-CFG-ENTRY-STRIDE` |the value written to each entry reads back at that entry's own address at the 0x20 stride and at no other entry
|`CHK-CFG-HALF-WORD` |the 32-bit halves of every register match its 64-bit value, and a write of one half leaves the other half unchanged
|`CHK-CFG-GROUPID-RB` |`group_id` written 0xA reads back 0xA on both instances
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |none of the bounded JTAG2AXI and `smu_axi_in` waits expires
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-131-smu_filter_lock_test]]
===== 131 `smu_filter_lock_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_filter_lock_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_filter_lock_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC JTAG2AXI bridge, with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); `ocah_axi_vip` master on `smu_axi_in` for the filter registers and the inbound probe; the outbound interface `u_axi_out_if` (`arvalid`, `arready`, `araddr`, `arprot`) sampled on `clk_smu_i`; SMC aperture GLOBAL_BASE and LOCAL_BASE; inbound filter entry 0, which the leaf programs over JTAG2AXI to admit the span of both filter register banks; entries 4, 5 and 6 of both the inbound and the outbound filter instance; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: once `locked` is set in FILTER_CONFIG of an entry of the inbound or the outbound filter, the SMU answers every later write to that entry's FILTER_CONFIG, START_ADDR and END_ADDR with DECERR, keeps the entry's values and its filtering, and leaves the neighbouring entries writable.

*Test Coverage*:

* Before any entry is locked, an unmatched JTAG2AXI read of `0x8000_0000` on the outbound path completes with SUCCESS and an unmatched read of CHIP_CONFIG VERSION_LO on `smu_axi_in` returns DECERR; `hw/ip/axi_filter/doc/index.adoc` ("Match, Then Permit", "Blocked Transactions") gives the block-on-no-match behaviour.
* Entry 5 of each instance is programmed with a one-page window the leaf chooses: the inbound entry admits reads of the page holding VERSION_LO, and the outbound entry blocks reads of the page holding `0x8000_0000`, with `allow_ns` equal to the AxPROT[1] the bridge read is measured to carry on `smu_axi_out`. The measured AxPROT is a stimulus calibration and is logged, not compared.
* After a write of FILTER_CONFIG with `locked` set, a read of FILTER_CONFIG returns OKAY with `locked` 1 and the programmed fields unchanged on both instances (`hw/ip/axi_filter/regs/filter_ctrl.rdl`, `locked`; `hw/ip/axi_filter/doc/index.adoc`, "Locking a Filter Entry"). The write response of the locking write is logged and never compared.
* Writes of FILTER_CONFIG that change a field, and that try to clear `locked`, complete with DECERR on both halves, and FILTER_CONFIG reads back unchanged; the same RDL description and document section state that writes to a locked entry are steered to the error subordinate and that reads still return the locked configuration.
* Writes of new START_ADDR and END_ADDR values to the locked entry complete with DECERR and the registers read back OKAY with the values programmed before the lock.
* Entries 4 and 6 of both instances take their three registers written with values the leaf chooses and read them back with OKAY responses.
* After the lock, an inbound read of the VERSION_LO page returns OKAY with the VERSION_LO reset value of `hw/sys/smc/regs/blocks/chip_config/chip_config.rdl`, and an outbound read of `0x8000_0000` over JTAG2AXI completes with DECERR, so the locked entries still filter.
* The inbound and the outbound instance return the same response and value sequence for the lock, rejected-write, readback and neighbour steps; each element of that sequence is already compared per instance, so this holds as the conjunction of those checks on both instances.
* The RDL makes `locked` impossible to clear once set; the leaf leaves entry 5 locked on both instances and drives no reset.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, program SMC GLOBAL_BASE to LOCAL_BASE over JTAG2AXI, read the IDCODE, require the JTAG2AXI gate open, program inbound entry 0 to admit the filter register banks and create the `smu_axi_in` master.
. Read `0x8000_0000` over JTAG2AXI while sampling `smu_axi_out` to measure the AxPROT, require the read to complete, and read VERSION_LO on `smu_axi_in` and require DECERR.
. On each instance program START_ADDR, END_ADDR and FILTER_CONFIG of entry 5, write FILTER_CONFIG again with `locked` set, and read FILTER_CONFIG back.
. Write FILTER_CONFIG of entry 5 with a changed `entry_enabled` and then with `locked` clear, and read it back.
. Write new START_ADDR and END_ADDR values to entry 5, reading each register back after its write.
. Read the three registers of entry 5.
. Write and read back the three registers of entries 4 and 6.
. Read VERSION_LO on `smu_axi_in` and `0x8000_0000` over JTAG2AXI and compare the responses.
. Compare the recorded response and value sequences of the two instances.
. Log the last state of the JTAG2AXI, `smu_axi_in` and outbound observation waits, compare the access count at every `smu_axi_in` wait site, and require none expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-LOCK-SET` |after the locking write FILTER_CONFIG of entry 5 reads OKAY with `locked` 1 and its programmed fields on both instances
|`CHK-LOCK-CONFIG-DECERR` |FILTER_CONFIG writes to the locked entry, one changing a field and one clearing `locked`, return DECERR on both halves and the value stays
|`CHK-LOCK-ADDR-DECERR` |START_ADDR and END_ADDR writes to the locked entry return DECERR and the registers read back their pre-lock values
|`CHK-LOCK-READBACK` |the three registers of the locked entry read OKAY with the locked configuration and the programmed range
|`CHK-LOCK-ISOLATION` |entries 4 and 6 stay writable and read back what was written on both instances
|`CHK-LOCK-STILL-FILTERS` |the locked inbound entry admits its page with the VERSION_LO reset value and the locked outbound entry blocks its page with DECERR
|`CHK-LOCK-PARITY` |the conjunction of `CHK-LOCK-SET` through `CHK-LOCK-ISOLATION` on both instances: the response and value sequence of the lock, rejected-write, readback and neighbour steps is equal on the inbound and outbound instances
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the `smu_axi_in` write, read and probe wait sites each complete their declared access count, and none of the bounded JTAG2AXI, `smu_axi_in` and outbound observation waits expires
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-132-smu_remap_cfg_register_map_test]]
===== 132 `smu_remap_cfg_register_map_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_remap_cfg_register_map_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_remap_cfg_register_map_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC JTAG2AXI bridge, with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); `ocah_axi_vip` master on `smu_axi_in` for every remap register access after setup; SMC aperture GLOBAL_BASE and LOCAL_BASE; inbound filter entry 0, which the leaf programs over JTAG2AXI to admit the span from the alias remap bank through the Xvisor remap bank; the eight alias remap regions, the eight M-mode output remap registers and the eight Xvisor output remap registers; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: the alias remap and the two output remap register banks reset to zero, sit at the addresses and stride the SMC register map gives, and store and read back exactly the fields their RDL defines.

*Test Coverage*:

* All 24 alias remap registers (REGION_START, REGION_END and REGION_ATTRS of eight regions) and all 16 output remap REGION_ATTRS registers (M-mode and Xvisor, eight each) read 0 before any write; the reset value is 0 in `hw/ip/axi_alias_remap/regs/alias_remap.rdl` and `hw/ip/output_remap/regs/output_remap.rdl`.
* A value unique to each register, written to every register of the three banks, reads back at its own address only, masked to the defined fields; the alias bank base is the aperture LOCAL_BASE plus 0x1_2000 with a 0x20 region stride, the M-mode bank is at plus 0x1_3000 and the Xvisor bank at plus 0x1_4000 with an 0x8 stride (`hw/sys/smc/regs/smc.rdl`), and the leaf cross-checks the generated header against those placements before use.
* On alias region 3, REGION_START and REGION_END `start_addr` and `end_addr` store and read back bits [55:12], and REGION_ATTRS stores `offset` [55:12], `cacheable` [59:56] and `valid` [63]; the leaf walks one bit at a time and also writes all ones, as the fields in `alias_remap.rdl` give.
* On M-mode region 2 and Xvisor region 5, REGION_ATTRS stores `offset` [55:0] and `valid` [63] one bit at a time, as `output_remap.rdl` gives.
* Bits no RDL field defines (alias bits [11:0], alias REGION_ATTRS bits outside the three fields, and output remap bits [62:56]) are written and read, and the read value is logged and never compared.
* The remap action on traffic is out of scope: the leaf programs the alias START above END during its walks so no address is selected, and clears every register it wrote.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, program SMC GLOBAL_BASE to LOCAL_BASE over JTAG2AXI, read the IDCODE, require the JTAG2AXI gate open, program inbound entry 0 to admit the remap banks and create the `smu_axi_in` master.
. Read every alias remap and output remap register as two 32-bit halves and compare with 0.
. Write a unique value to every register of the three banks, read all back, compare each with its own expected value masked to the defined fields, and clear them.
. On alias region 3, with a START above END, write all ones and then a one-bit walk to REGION_START, REGION_END and REGION_ATTRS fields, reading each back, and write and read the bits no field defines.
. On M-mode region 2 and Xvisor region 5, walk `offset` bits [55:0] and `valid`, reading each back, and write and read bits [62:56].
. Log the last state of the JTAG2AXI and `smu_axi_in` waits and the access count at every site, compare each site's access count, and require none expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ALIAS-REGS-RESET` |all 24 alias REGION_START, REGION_END and REGION_ATTRS registers read 0 before any write
|`CHK-OREMAP-REGS-RESET` |all 16 output remap REGION_ATTRS registers read 0 before any write
|`CHK-ALIAS-REGS-PLACEMENT` |each alias register reads back its own unique value, masked to the defined fields, at its own address only
|`CHK-OREMAP-REGS-PLACEMENT` |each output remap offset reads back its own unique value, masked to `offset` and `valid`, at its own address only
|`CHK-ALIAS-REGS-MASKS` |alias `start_addr`, `end_addr`, `offset`, `cacheable` and `valid` bits store and read back
|`CHK-OREMAP-REGS-MASKS` |output remap `offset` bits [55:0] and `valid` store and read back
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |every bounded `smu_axi_in` wait site completes its declared access count, and none of the bounded JTAG2AXI and `smu_axi_in` waits expires
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-133-smu_smc_unmapped_response_test]]
===== 133 `smu_smc_unmapped_response_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_smc_unmapped_response_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_smc_unmapped_response_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC JTAG2AXI bridge, with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); `ocah_axi_vip` master on `smu_axi_in` (`ext_in`) for every probe; the `smc_region_size_o` output polled on `clk_smu_i`; SMC aperture GLOBAL_BASE, LOCAL_BASE and REGION_SIZE; inbound filter entry 0, which the leaf programs over JTAG2AXI to admit the local aperture; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: an `ext_in` access to an SMC address that reaches no unit, to the first word past a unit's extent, or to the M-mode and Xvisor remap windows answers DECERR, with read data `0xBADCAB1E` where the specification gives it.

*Test Coverage*:

* With REGION_SIZE widened to `0x0400_0000` and the aperture opened by the leaf, a read at two addresses between unit apertures answers DECERR with data `0xBADCAB1E` and a write there answers DECERR (`hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc`, "Address Space Organization"). The first address is the end of the last mapped aperture of the DFX control unit and the second is the midpoint of the unmapped range up to the SMC base configuration unit, both taken from the generated SMC address map.
* A read at `BASE + 0x001_7000` and `BASE + 0x001_7FFC` answers DECERR with `0xBADCAB1E` and is compared; the write there is documented as SLVERR (`memmap.adoc`), and its response is logged and not compared. In this run it answers DECERR, a mismatch recorded for the owner.
* A read of the first word past the extent of each of eleven units (reset unit, misc wrap, GPIO interface, base configuration, alias remap, DFX control, mailbox, DMA control, zeroer, CPU control and the core 0 watchdog) answers DECERR with `0xBADCAB1E`, as the past-extent response in `hw/sys/smc/regs/smc.rdl` declares.
* A write past the extent of the DFX control, mailbox, DMA control, zeroer, CPU control and watchdog units answers DECERR, as `smc.rdl` declares; the write past the extent of the reset unit, misc wrap, GPIO interface, base configuration and alias remap units is declared SLVERR there, and its response is logged and not compared. In this run those writes answer DECERR, a mismatch recorded for the owner.
* A read and a write at `BASE + 0x100_0000` (M-mode window) and `BASE + 0x180_0000` (Xvisor window) answer DECERR, because those remap regions forward SMC initiator accesses only (`memmap.adoc`).
* An `ext_in` access to the window beyond the local and global apertures, and the units that lie beyond the reset REGION_SIZE, are out of scope here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, program SMC GLOBAL_BASE to LOCAL_BASE and REGION_SIZE to `0x0400_0000` over JTAG2AXI, wait for `smc_region_size_o` to follow, program inbound entry 0 to admit the local aperture, read it back and create the `smu_axi_in` master.
. Read and write the two between-unit addresses and the two addresses at `BASE + 0x001_7000` and `BASE + 0x001_7FFC`, and compare the read responses and data and the between-unit write responses.
. Read and write the first word past each of the eleven units' extents, and compare the reads and the DECERR-declared writes.
. Read and write the M-mode and Xvisor window addresses and compare the responses.
. Write REGION_SIZE back to its reset value, wait for `smc_region_size_o`, and clear inbound entry 0.
. Log the last state of the JTAG2AXI, REGION_SIZE poll, read and write waits and require none expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-UNMAP-BETWEEN` |between-unit `ext_in` reads answer DECERR with `0xBADCAB1E` and between-unit writes answer DECERR
|`CHK-UNMAP-PAST-EXTENT` |past-extent reads answer DECERR with `0xBADCAB1E` and DECERR-declared writes answer DECERR; the writes the RDL and `memmap.adoc` declare SLVERR are logged and answered DECERR in this run, a mismatch recorded for the owner
|`CHK-UNMAP-MMODE-XVISOR` |reads and writes to the M-mode and Xvisor windows from `ext_in` answer DECERR
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |none of the bounded JTAG2AXI, REGION_SIZE poll, `smu_axi_in` read and write waits expires
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
==== RESET

[[sec-020-smu_boot_stall_jtag_cold_reset_matrix_test]]
===== 20 `smu_boot_stall_jtag_cold_reset_matrix_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_boot_stall_jtag_cold_reset_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: Boot-stall cold-reset sticky + TRST clear matrix.

*Test Coverage*:

* Stall asserted across cold reset (TRST held high) stays sticky; fuse gated
* TRST pulse (reset_tap) clears DEBUG_CONTROL / boot-stall exports
* fuse_reset releases after TRST clear
* Sticky: re-assert stall without primary reset does not re-gate fuse_reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run without `+skip_fuse_sense` (the `testlists/all.toml` entry carries none): the SMC eFuse
  controller senses the `+smc_efuse_hex` image through the wrapper's eFuse model, and
  `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` is that controller's own sense-complete release. The run log
  carries `Not skipping fuse sense` and no shadow-preload load.
. Run the test body `cocotb_wrapper/tests/smu_boot_stall_jtag_cold_reset_matrix_test.py` over the wrapper's product interfaces; it drives the TAP and pins directly and has no separate sequence file.
. Watch `smc_fuse_sense_done_o` 0 -> 1 after every primary reset (bounded at 5000 `clk_smu`; a
  flag already high, or an expiry, fails) before any `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` compare. At
  bring-up the release must then follow within 256 `clk_smu`. With the stall held across the cold
  reset the gate must read `0` on every one of the 64 `clk_smu` after that rise, so the `0` is the
  gate and not a sense still in progress. After the stall clears, with the sense already done, the
  release must arrive within 256 `clk_smu` (the `prim_sync3` + sticky flop + 16-stage pipe path,
  not the sense latency) and stay released for 64 `clk_smu`.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

The scenario has two implementations that share this claim: the cocotb test body
`smu_boot_stall_jtag_cold_reset_matrix_test.py` and the SV-UVM
`smu_boot_stall_jtag_cold_reset_matrix_test_seq.svh` (`--framework uvm`). The SV-UVM
shape runs the same eight legs per pass on the real sense (the testlist entry
carries no `+skip_fuse_sense` in either framework), including the GPIO-pad legs
(pad alone, both sources, override low over the pad), with the sense-done and
fuse-reset observables read through `smu_tb_if` and every bounded wait recorded.
Independently of the sequence, `smu_debug_control_tdr_ref_model` predicts the
capture of every DEBUG_CONTROL scan from the register shadow and the TAP resets it
observes, and `smu_boot_gate_ref_model` requires the boot gate open and the sense
complete at every fuse-reset release `smu_reset_pin_monitor` observes.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-STALL-STICKY` |Evidence token `STALL_COLD_STICKY`: stall survives cold; the sensed `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` stays 0 for 64 `clk_smu` with `smc_fuse_sense_done_o` high
|`CHK-STALL-TRST` |Evidence token `STALL_TRST_CLEAR`: TRST clears stall; with the sense already done, the release follows the clear within the gate path and stays released
|`CHK-STALL-REASSERT` |Evidence token `STALL_REASSERT_STICKY`: re-assert sticky
|`CHK-STALL-BASELINE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): after the TAP reset the fuse reset is released, the sense is done and the stall override idle
|`CHK-DEBUG-CONTROL-READBACK` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the written DEBUG_CONTROL image reads back on its four software-owned bits and on the SMC exports
|`CHK-STALL-STICKY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): `STALL_COLD_STICKY` in the SV-UVM shape: the stall exports survive the cold reset and the fuse reset reads 0 on every hold-window clock after the sense completes
|`CHK-STALL-TRST` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): `STALL_TRST_CLEAR` in the SV-UVM shape: with the sense done and the gate shut, the TRST pulse clears both exports, the release follows within the gate-path bound and holds, and the release latency counted from the instant the clear stimulus began fits inside the hold window the sticky claim rests on
|`CHK-STALL-REASSERT` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): `STALL_REASSERT_STICKY` in the SV-UVM shape: the re-asserted stall does not re-gate the released fuse reset
|`CHK-STALL-PAD-ONLY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the GPIO pad alone gates the fuse reset across a cold reset while the JTAG override is idle
|`CHK-STALL-BOTH-SOURCES` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): pad and JTAG stall asserted together keep the gate shut
|`CHK-STALL-OVRD-MASKS-PAD` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the override forced low over the driving pad opens the gate within the gate-path bound and holds, and the release latency counted from the end of the clearing DR scan fits inside the hold window
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): eight bounded waits (the TAP-state confirmation, the baseline release, two sense completions, two primary releases, two gate releases) each name a finite bound and their last state; none expired
|`CHK-SB-MIN-ACTIVITY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the `debug_control_tdr` feature compares at least the seven scans the pass issues and `boot_gate` at least the two releases it causes, both counts from the always-on scoreboard
|`CHK-SB-DEBUG-CONTROL-TDR` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): always-on scoreboard, feature `debug_control_tdr`: `smu_debug_control_tdr_ref_model` predicts every DEBUG_CONTROL capture from the previous update and zero after any TAP reset (TRST, Test-Logic-Reset, power-on reset), the TCK-domain register untouched by the cold reset
|`CHK-SB-BOOT-GATE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): always-on scoreboard, feature `boot_gate`: `smu_boot_gate_ref_model` requires `ext_boot_seq_done_i` = 1 and `smc_fuse_sense_done_o` = 1 in the SMU clock of every `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` 0 -> 1 the pin monitor observes
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`. Under `--framework uvm` the SV-UVM rows above govern instead, and `+SMU_DEBUG_CONTROL_SCOREBOARD_NEGATIVE` and `+SMU_BOOT_GATE_SCOREBOARD_NEGATIVE` must each fail the run.

'''''

[[sec-021-smu_boot_stall_vs_ic_reset_priority_test]]
===== 21 `smu_boot_stall_vs_ic_reset_priority_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_boot_stall_vs_ic_reset_priority_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: Stall sticky isolation vs IC_RESET; TRST clears both.

*Test Coverage*:

* IC_RESET warm then cold while stall sticky: ovrd asserts; stall survives; the cold override asserts the SMC primary reset
* IC_RESET DEFAULT: domain ovrd clears; the primary reset releases again and the still-sticky stall gates fuse_reset across that reset too
* TRST clears stall AND IC_RESET; fuse_reset releases

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run without `+skip_fuse_sense` (the `testlists/all.toml` entry carries none): the SMC eFuse
  controller senses the `+smc_efuse_hex` image through the wrapper's eFuse model, and
  `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` is that controller's own sense-complete release. The run log
  carries `Not skipping fuse sense` and no shadow-preload load.
. Run the test body `cocotb_wrapper/tests/smu_boot_stall_vs_ic_reset_priority_test.py` over the wrapper's product interfaces; it drives the TAP and pins directly and has no separate sequence file.
. Watch `smc_fuse_sense_done_o` 0 -> 1 after every primary reset (bounded at 5000 `clk_smu`; a
  flag already high, or an expiry, fails) before any `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` compare. At
  bring-up the release must then follow within 256 `clk_smu`. With the stall held across the cold
  reset the gate must read `0` on every one of the 64 `clk_smu` after that rise, so the `0` is the
  gate and not a sense still in progress. After the stall clears, with the sense already done, the
  release must arrive within 256 `clk_smu` (the `prim_sync3` + sticky flop + 16-stage pipe path,
  not the sense latency) and stay released for 64 `clk_smu`.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-STALL-VS-IC` |Evidence token `STALL_VS_IC_RESET`: IC_RESET does not clear stall; the sensed `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` stays gated through the IC_RESET legs (sampled after `smc_fuse_sense_done_o`) and releases within the gate path after TRST
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-024-smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test]]
===== 24 `smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test`

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive only (no external VIP); `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: SMU_006: ext_boot_seq_done_i gates smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o release.

*Test Coverage*:

* Observes smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o — the RTL consumer gated by ext_boot_seq_done_i (port_table: gates reset release).
* rst_primary_smc_clk_no is NOT gated by this pin (negative control CHK-PRIMARY-NOT-GATED).
* With the pin lowered again across a cold reset, the gate holds the fuse reset asserted after the new release, and raising the pin releases it.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive `smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test_seq` over the wrapper's product interfaces.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

The scenario has two implementations that share this claim: the cocotb
`smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test_seq.py` and the SV-UVM `smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test_seq.svh`
(`--framework uvm`). The SV-UVM shape re-runs the cold reset with the gate shut at
the start of every pass (the base test's bring-up releases with the gate open), so
the gated window, the negative control and the ungate are proved once per pass, and
stamps the first primary and fuse rises, and the gate level in the clock of the primary
rise, with a tracker armed while the cold reset holds both low, so the negative control
and the fence are taken from DUT edges rather than from levels found after the settle.
Independently of the sequence,
`smu_boot_gate_ref_model` requires the boot gate open and the sense complete at
every fuse-reset release `smu_reset_pin_monitor` observes, so a release inside the
gated window fails the scoreboard as well as the sequence.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-PRIMARY-NOT-GATED` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-BOOT-SEQ-GATE` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |Both bounded waits (primary release while gated; fuse release after ungate) completed within RELEASE_BOUND clk_smu_i cycles and none expired: the compare is the list of (label, expired, bound) records against the expected list, so a missing, expired or differently bounded wait fails
|`CHK-NONVAC` |Ordered fence from DUT edges, simulation-time stamped: the first rise of `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` precedes the `ext_boot_seq_done_i` drive, and the first rise of `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` follows both the end of the gated sample window and the ungate drive; a fuse release inside the gated or pre-ungate window fails
|`CHK-BOOT-SEQ-REGATE` |with `ext_boot_seq_done_i` low across a second cold reset, `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` stays low for the whole gated sample window after the release and rises within RELEASE_BOUND cycles of raising the pin
|`CHK-BOOT-SEQ-GATE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the sense is done before the gated window, the gate reads 0 and the fuse reset reads 0 on every one of the 64 gated samples, and the fuse reset releases within the gate-path bound after the ungate
|`CHK-PRIMARY-NOT-GATED` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the primary reset is low under the cold reset, its 0 -> 1 is observed by the tracker with the gate reading 0 in that clock, and it reads 1 within the bound while the gate still reads 0
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the three bounded waits (the sense done before the gated window, the primary release while gated, the fuse release after the ungate within the gate-path bound) name a finite bound and their last level; none expired
|`CHK-NONVAC` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the DUT-edge fence, simulation-time stamped: first primary rise before the ungate, first fuse rise after the gated window's end and after the ungate
|`CHK-SB-MIN-ACTIVITY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the `boot_gate` feature compares at least the one release the pass causes, from the always-on scoreboard
|`CHK-SB-BOOT-GATE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): always-on scoreboard, feature `boot_gate`: `smu_boot_gate_ref_model` requires `ext_boot_seq_done_i` = 1 and `smc_fuse_sense_done_o` = 1 in the SMU clock of every `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o` 0 -> 1 the pin monitor observes
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `smc_under_smu`, `smoke`, `hosted`. Under `--framework uvm` the SV-UVM rows above govern instead, and `+SMU_BOOT_GATE_SCOREBOARD_NEGATIVE` must fail the run.

'''''

[[sec-028-smu_jtag_reset_override_test]]
===== 28 `smu_jtag_reset_override_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_jtag_reset_override_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: IC_RESET override/release on EXT and SMC cold-reset slices.

*Test Coverage*:

* The TDR is 155 bits on this wrapper: 68 SMC + 8 SEP + 1 EXT ports, two bits each, plus `reset_hold` (`doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc` "IC_RESET TDR Structure"). The EXT port sits nearest TDO, so a helper geometry short of the TDR lands the EXT fields in the SEP slice and the EXT leg below fails; this is the leaf that catches a wrong slice width.
* Default IC_RESET readback is all-ones over the whole DR, with TDO required resolvable on every readback: the JTAG VIP reads an X/Z TDO bit as 0, so during each IC_RESET readback the leaf samples the bench's `jtag_tdo` on every TCK rising edge while `jtag_tdo_oen` (high in Shift-IR and Shift-DR) is high, and an X/Z bit, an X/Z enable, or fewer driven bits than the 155-bit DR fails the run. Verilator is two-state, so there the guard cannot fire; it bites on a four-state simulator.
* The EXT override is applied and released in two Update-DRs each, `reset_control` first and `reset_enable` second, the order the DTP JTAG chapter (`hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc`) gives so a hazard-free consumer multiplexer never sees its select and data move together. `.ovrd` is active high and `.val` carries `reset_control`, so each staging update is visible on the EXT slice alone.
* EXT control=0 staged under enable=1 drives ctrl_n=0 with ovrd still 0; enable=0 then asserts ovrd=1 with ctrl_n=0 while the whole override half of both the SMC and the SEP slice reads 0; control=1 staged under enable=0 drives ctrl_n=1 with ovrd still 1.
* SMC cold_reset port override updates the hierarchical SMC slice while the EXT override is released: the SMC slice's override half reads exactly the cold-reset bit, the SEP slice's override half reads 0 and the EXT override reads 0.
* Clearing the TDR restores both overrides to 0.
* Every managed-subsystem cold and warm port and every SEP slice port, including `km_jtag_rst_n`, is then walked in the same two-update order: control staged under enable=1, enable cleared to apply the override, control released under the override, then the TDR cleared. After each update, the clearing one included, the TDR reads back as written, `ic_reset_smc_ovrd_o` carries exactly the ovrd bits of the SS ports, and the SMC and SEP slices are read whole: each is a packed struct whose `.ovrd` half sits above its `.val` half, the first field of each half nearest TDI. `.ovrd` carries the inverse of reset_enable, 1 while the override applies, and `.val` carries reset_control as written, the active-low reset value (`doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc`, "IC_RESET TDR Structure"; fuse is the SMC port nearest TDO, the Key Manager the SEP port nearest TDO). Every port's override and value are compared: override on the applied ports only, value equal to the control written, and every other port at its default.
* The leaf loads `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/km_rom.parhex` into the Key Manager ROM (`+km_rom_hex`) before that walk. `SW_RESET_N.km_sw_rst_n` resets to 0, and the release step (`reset_enable=0`, `reset_control=1`) holds the Key Manager out of reset. With the image loaded, `u_km_rom.req_i` stays 0 or 1. An empty ROM returns X; PicoRV32 `mem_la_read` is gated by `resetn`, so once the reset is high the look-ahead read takes the request to X and `prim_rom` `noXOnCsI` fires. That assertion is the only grade of this part: Verilator compiles it out (`+define+ASSERTS_OFF`, stand-in entry in `hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`), so there it cannot fire; it bites on a four-state simulator. The bench holds it off until the SMC primary reset has released (stand-in entry "`noXOnCsI` hold-off window"), well before the walk releases the Key Manager.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run the test body `cocotb_wrapper/tests/smu_jtag_reset_override_test.py` over the wrapper's product interfaces; it drives the TAP and pins directly and has no separate sequence file.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

The scenario has two implementations that share this claim: the cocotb test body
`smu_jtag_reset_override_test.py` and the SV-UVM `smu_jtag_reset_override_test_seq.svh`
(`--framework uvm`). The SV-UVM shape holds the TDR geometry as a DV-owned table
transcribed from the integrator guide (68 SMC ports, 8 SEP ports, one external port,
two bits each plus `reset_hold`), scans the 155-bit register through the wide DR path,
and reads the override exports and the whole SMC slice (`jtag_smc_reset_ctrl.ovrd`
/ `.val`) through `smu_tb_if`; it also confirms the SMC primary reset releases again
once the cold override is cleared, so the next pass starts from the same baseline.
Independently of the sequence, `smu_ic_reset_tdr_ref_model` predicts the capture of
every IC_RESET scan from the register shadow it rebuilds from the updates and the
TAP resets it observes, and the always-on scoreboard pairs the two.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-IC-DEFAULT` |Evidence token `IC_RESET_DEFAULT`: IC_RESET default all-ones, every driven TDO bit of the scan resolvable
|`CHK-IC-EXT-STAGED` |Evidence token `IC_RESET_EXT_STAGED`: EXT control staged low reaches `jtag_ic_reset_ext_o.val` while `.ovrd` stays 0
|`CHK-IC-EXT-RELEASE-STAGED` |Evidence token `IC_RESET_EXT_RELEASE_STAGED`: EXT control staged high reaches `.val` while `.ovrd` stays 1
|`CHK-IC-DOMAIN` |Evidence token `IC_RESET_DOMAIN_EXCL`: with the EXT override applied, the EXT override is 1 and the SMC and SEP slices' whole override halves are 0; with the SMC cold override applied, the SMC override half is exactly the cold-reset bit and the EXT override and the SEP override half are 0
|`CHK-IC-SS-SEP-WALK` |Evidence token `IC_RESET_SS_SEP_WALK`: the SS and SEP ports staged, applied, released and cleared, with every SMC and SEP slice port's override and value following each update
|`CHK-IC-DEFAULT` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the reset image (all ones over the whole 155-bit register) reads back and both override exports are idle
|`CHK-IC-EXT-OVERRIDE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the external port overridden with reset_control 0 asserts `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ovrd` and drives `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ctrl_n` low, within a bounded wait
|`CHK-IC-READBACK` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the written EXT image reads back whole
|`CHK-IC-SMC-COLD-OVERRIDE` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the SMC cold port overridden likewise asserts the SMC cold override, drives it low, and the SMC slice the reset controller receives (`ovrd` and `val` vectors) equals the slice the image encodes; the SMC primary reset is held low while the override stands
|`CHK-IC-DOMAIN` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): while one slice is overridden the other's export stays idle
|`CHK-IC-CLEAR` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the reset image restores both exports and the SMC primary reset releases again
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): seven bounded waits (the TAP-state confirmation, the four export transitions, the primary reset held by the cold override, the primary release) each name a finite bound and their last state; none expired
|`CHK-SB-MIN-ACTIVITY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the `ic_reset_tdr` feature's comparison count, from the always-on scoreboard, is at or above the five scans the pass issues
|`CHK-SB-IC-RESET-TDR` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): always-on scoreboard, feature `ic_reset_tdr`: `smu_ic_reset_tdr_ref_model` predicts every IC_RESET capture from the previous update, the reset image after TRST or power-on reset, and after Test-Logic-Reset the reset image only while `reset_hold` is 1 (PTAP document, IC_RESET fields). A required feature with zero comparisons fails the run
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`. Under `--framework uvm` the SV-UVM rows above govern instead, and `+SMU_IC_RESET_SCOREBOARD_NEGATIVE` must fail the run.

'''''

[[sec-030-smu_reset_release_sync_test]]
===== 30 `smu_reset_release_sync_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_reset_release_sync_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive only (no external VIP); `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: Cold and primary reset deassertions land on a clock edge of the domain each output is synchronised to.

*Test Coverage*:

* Measured against `hw/sys/smu/doc/port_table.adoc`: deassertion of `rst_cold_ni` is synchronous to `clk_ref_i`, and the `rst_cold_stable_ref_clk_no` / `rst_primary_ref_clk_no` / `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` rows name the domain each output is synchronised to.
* `rst_cold_ni` is released at a random phase that lies on no clock edge, and the release is retried until it does; the deassertion of the reference-domain outputs must land on a `clk_ref_i` rising edge and the SMC-domain primary reset on a `clk_smu_i` rising edge, none at the release instant.
* Only alignment is asserted: the synchroniser depth is unstated, so the cycle count is logged, not compared.
* `obs_smc_rst_n_o` and `obs_dtp_rst_n_o` are two taps on one net -- `smu.sv` wires the DTP's `rst_n_i` to the SMC primary reset -- so their agreement is logged as a composition fact and is not a checker.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Assert `rst_cold_ni`, wait for every observed reset to read low, and start edge monitors on `clk_ref_i` and `clk_smu_i`.
. Release `rst_cold_ni` at a random phase into the `clk_ref_i` period; retry until the release instant is on no edge of either clock.
. Record the deassertion time of each observed output and require it after the release and on its own domain's rising edge.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-RST-COLD-S2` |cold-stable deassertion lands on a clk_ref_i edge; the SMC-domain primary reset lands on a clk_smu_i edge
|`CHK-SMU-RST-PRIMARY-S2` |primary reset deassertions land on their own domain's edge
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-031-smu_sram_auto_init_done_test]]
===== 31 `smu_sram_auto_init_done_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sram_auto_init_done_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive only (no external VIP); `elaboration` firmware mode, no `+smc_scratch_ram_hex` image

*Objective*: With SRAM auto-initialisation enabled, a cold reset clears `smc_init_mem_done_o`, the zeroing sweep runs, and the output asserts and holds when it completes.

*Test Coverage*:

* SMU-MEMINIT.S1 against the `smc_disable_sram_auto_init_i` and `smc_init_mem_done_o` rows of `port_table.adoc`.
* The bench drives `smc_disable_sram_auto_init_i` low (its value when no `+smc_scratch_ram_hex` image is staged) and the leaf reads it low at the SMU boundary before and during the sweep; the zeroing is seen running at its consumer (enable high, address counter advancing, writes reaching the scratch RAM), so the done output is tied to observed work.
* The passing value of the enable is the bench default; the positive control is <<sec-032-smu_sram_auto_init_disabled_test>>, which drives the pin high on the same composition and requires the sweep not to start.
* The clear is observed from a flag read high: the leaf waits, under a bound that fails on expiry, for the bring-up sweep to raise `smc_init_mem_done_o` and requires it high before the cold reset asserts, so the low read under reset is a 1 -> 0 transition and not the bring-up value.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Read `smc_disable_sram_auto_init_i` low at the SMU boundary, wait for the bring-up sweep to raise `smc_init_mem_done_o` and require it high, then assert a cold reset; require `smc_init_mem_done_o` to clear.
. Release the reset and observe the scratch-RAM zeroing running at its consumer.
. Require `smc_init_mem_done_o` to assert once the sweep completes and to hold afterwards.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-MEMINIT-S1` |smc_init_mem_done_o is high after the bring-up sweep, clears under cold reset, and rises again after release, after an observed zeroing sweep
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-032-smu_sram_auto_init_disabled_test]]
===== 32 `smu_sram_auto_init_disabled_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sram_auto_init_disabled_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive only (no external VIP); `elaboration` firmware mode with a `+smc_scratch_ram_hex` image, which is what drives `smc_disable_sram_auto_init_i` high until `tb_smc_sram_auto_init_restore` lowers it

*Objective*: With SRAM auto-initialisation held off, no zeroing sweep starts and no zeroing write reaches the scratch RAM; what `smc_init_mem_done_o` does in that state is recorded, not claimed.

*Test Coverage*:

* The complement of <<sec-031-smu_sram_auto_init_done_test>> on the same composition: the same consumer nodes that are required to move there are required to stay idle here; the done output that is required to assert there is only recorded here.
* The cold-reset leg is bounded by the same specification gap. With the sweep held off the output's level before the reset is unspecified, so requiring it high would claim that unspecified behaviour from the other side; this leaf compares only that the output reads low while the reset is asserted, and records what it read beforehand. The clear of an output established high is <<sec-031-smu_sram_auto_init_done_test>>, which runs the sweep to completion and requires the output high before asserting the same reset.
* The preloaded image must survive to first fetch, which is what the hold-off exists for; the leaf reads the enable high at the SMU boundary rather than assuming it.
* EXPECT-SOURCE: the no-sweep and no-write legs follow from the input's stated purpose (`smu.adoc`, `cpu.adoc`), and the output reading low under an asserted cold reset from its being a completion status (`port_table.adoc`). Specification gap, recorded here: neither document states what `smc_init_mem_done_o` does when the sweep is held off, so the leaf logs whether and when it rose and how it held after the release, and that record carries no token. A specification statement would turn it into a claim; the implementation is not consulted for one.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Read `smc_disable_sram_auto_init_i` high at the SMU boundary, record what `smc_init_mem_done_o` reads, then assert a cold reset and require the output to read low while it is asserted.
. Release the reset and watch the zeroing consumer: the enable stays low and no zeroing write reaches the scratch RAM.
. Record what `smc_init_mem_done_o` does over the same window (rose or not, held or not); no compare.
. Lower the input through `tb_smc_sram_auto_init_restore`, run a second cold reset, which must reach the primary SMC reset within the bound, and require the enable to rise and zeroing writes to reach the scratch RAM.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-MEMINIT-DISABLED` |with smc_disable_sram_auto_init_i high the zeroing sweep never starts and no zeroing write reaches the scratch RAM, and smc_init_mem_done_o reads low while the cold reset is asserted; what it read beforehand and how it behaves after the release is recorded, not compared
|`CHK-SMU-MEMINIT-RESTORED` |with smc_disable_sram_auto_init_i lowered again, the next cold reset raises the initialisation enable and zeroing writes reach the scratch RAM
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `smc_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-033-smu_clock_domain_composition_test]]
===== 33 `smu_clock_domain_composition_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_clock_domain_composition_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` for the IC_RESET override; TB pin drive; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The telemetry, SEP-watchdog and peripheral clock and reset domains reach their consumers as their own domains and stay independent of `clk_smu_i`.

*Test Coverage*:

* `port_table.adoc` declares the clock and reset ports read here (`clk_telemetry_i`, `rst_telemetry_ni`, `clk_sep_wdt_i`, the `rst_primary_*` outputs) and names each one's domain; it does not tabulate which logic each domain clocks, so identity and rate are checked at the consumers inside the elaborated design.
* Each secondary clock is read at its consumer for identity with the wrapper pin and for a toggle rate that matches its own period rather than `clk_smu_i`; the leaf requires distinct reference and SMU periods from the timing draw so an equality read can tell the clocks apart.
* The SMC cold reset is asserted through the DTP IC_RESET override; the primary and peripheral resets must fall while `rst_telemetry_ni` stays released and `clk_telemetry_i` keeps running.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Read each secondary clock at its consumer against the wrapper pin, and count its transitions over a fixed window against its configured period.
. Read each domain reset released at its consumer.
. Assert the SMC cold reset through IC_RESET and require the primary and peripheral resets to fall while the telemetry reset and clock stay live; release and require recovery.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-CLK-DOMAINS-S2` |secondary clocks toggle at their own rate
|`CHK-SMU-CLK-DOMAINS-S3` |each domain reset is released at its consumer
|`CHK-SMU-CLK-DOMAINS-S4` |telemetry stays released and clocked while primary/periph fall
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-134-smu_dtp_sysreset_stop_clks_test]]
===== 134 `smu_dtp_sysreset_stop_clks_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_sysreset_stop_clks_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_dtp_sysreset_stop_clks_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` on the DTP TAP (IDCODE read, then DEBUG_CONTROL writes with `jtag_clock_stop` set and cleared); TB pin `tb_cool_reset_pin` to assert and release the primary reset; observation of `dtp_stop_clks_o` and `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` on every `clk_smu_i` edge; `clk_ref_i` for the reset hold timer; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: `dtp_stop_clks_o` reads 0 on every clock edge inside a primary reset even though the JTAG clock-stop request stays programmed, and returns to 1 on the third `clk_smu_i` edge after the reset releases, a latency the leaf takes from the documented request latency by inference.

*Test Coverage*:

* `hw/sys/dtp/doc/clk_rst.adoc` ("Reset Architecture") states that `rst_n_i` resets the clock-stop output to 0, and ("Clock Stop", Signal Flow) that `stop_clks_o` follows the requests three `clk_i` cycles later and is low while `rst_n_i` is asserted; those statements are the source of the 0 inside the reset. The document gives the three-cycle latency for a change of the requests and does not state it for a reset release, so requiring the return on the third edge after the release applies that latency by inference.
* With `jtag_clock_stop` programmed in DEBUG_CONTROL, `dtp_stop_clks_o` must read 1 before the reset; the leaf then asserts the cool reset, waits for `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` to read 0, holds the reset for 64 `clk_ref_i` cycles and requires 0 on every `clk_smu_i` sample taken inside that hold.
* After the release the request is still programmed, so `dtp_stop_clks_o` must read 1, and the leaf requires the first edge on which it reads 1 to be the third `clk_smu_i` edge after `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` rises, the value inferred from the request latency above.
* The number of samples taken inside the reset and the number that read 1 are logged as measured; the proof is the compare of the ones count against 0.
* Clearing `jtag_clock_stop` at the end and waiting for `dtp_stop_clks_o` to fall is a bounded cleanup wait with no compare.
* The TAP is left alone: `TRST_n` stays high through the primary reset, so the TAP-side reset inputs stay out of scope here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for the bench reset, open the TAP, read IDCODE against the DTP default and require `dtp_stop_clks_o` to read 0.
. Write DEBUG_CONTROL with `jtag_clock_stop` set, wait for `dtp_stop_clks_o` to read 1 and require it to read 1.
. Raise `tb_cool_reset_pin`, wait for `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` to read 0, then sample `dtp_stop_clks_o` on every `clk_smu_i` edge for 64 `clk_ref_i` cycles and require none to read 1.
. Release `tb_cool_reset_pin`, wait for `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` to read 1, count the `clk_smu_i` edges until `dtp_stop_clks_o` reads 1 and require the count to be 3.
. Write DEBUG_CONTROL back to 0 and wait for `dtp_stop_clks_o` to read 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DTP-SYSRESET-S1` |`dtp_stop_clks_o` reads 1 with `jtag_clock_stop` set and reads 0 on every `clk_smu_i` edge inside the primary reset.
|`CHK-DTP-SYSRESET-S2` |`dtp_stop_clks_o` returns to 1 on the third `clk_smu_i` edge after the primary reset releases, the documented request latency applied by inference.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-135-smu_isolation_retention_test]]
===== 135 `smu_isolation_retention_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_isolation_retention_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_isolation_retention_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` on the DTP TAP with the SMC JTAG2AXI frontdoor (`tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read low before any access); TB pins `tb_cool_reset_pin`, `rst_cold_ni`, `tb_cfg_flr_pf_active`, and `tb_gpio_drive_en` / `tb_gpio_drive_val` bit 53 for the isolation pin; observation of `obs_smc_rst_n_o` and `rst_cold_n_o`; the SMC reset-unit registers `ISOLATE_REQ_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_SMC_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_SMCEN_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_FLR_COUNTER_VALUE` and `ISOLATE_REQ_FLR_RESET_COUNTER_VALUE`; `clk_ref_i` and `clk_smu_i`; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The isolation registers and both FLR counters keep their programmed values across a cool reset and return to their reset values across a cold reset, with `ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG` clearing only while the isolation pin is low.

*Test Coverage*:

* `hw/sys/smc/doc/clk_rst.adoc` ("Reset Architecture", "Isolation Controls", "Isolation Connections", "Timing and Recovery") is the source of the retention behaviour: a cool reset leaves the isolation state and the FLR counters alone, a cold reset clears them, and the pin-enable register clears on a cold reset only while the isolation pin reads low.
* After the isolation registers are programmed and a 64-cycle cool reset, `ISOLATE_REQ_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG` and `ISOLATE_REQ_SMCEN_REG` must read the values the leaf programs (the register field masks applied to fixed patterns), and `ISOLATE_REQ_SMC_REG` must read its field mask, which the leaf sets by driving an edge on `tb_cfg_flr_pf_active`.
* After the same programming and a 64-cycle cold reset with GPIO 53 low, `ISOLATE_REQ_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_SMC_REG` and `ISOLATE_REQ_SMCEN_REG` must read the reset values in `hw/sys/smc/regs/blocks/reset_unit/reset_unit.rdl`.
* `ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG` must read its RDL reset value after a cold reset with GPIO 53 low and must keep the value the leaf programs after a cold reset with GPIO 53 high.
* The two FLR counters, programmed to 5 and 7, must keep those values across a cool reset and read their RDL reset values after a cold reset.
* The register values read before the cool reset are logged as measured; no compare is made on them.
* Isolation of the subsystem outputs by `isolate_req_o` is not observed on this bench; only the register state is read.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for the bench reset, open the TAP, require the SMC JTAG2AXI path to be enabled and drive GPIO 53 low.
. Write `ISOLATE_REQ_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG` and `ISOLATE_REQ_SMCEN_REG`, pulse `tb_cfg_flr_pf_active` to set `ISOLATE_REQ_SMC_REG` (a write to it only clears it), read the four registers back and log them, run a cool reset and require the values above.
. Repeat that programming, run a cold reset with GPIO 53 low and require the three registers to read their reset values.
. Program `ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG`, run a cold reset with GPIO 53 low and require its reset value; program it again, drive GPIO 53 high, run a cold reset and require the programmed value to remain; drive GPIO 53 low again.
. Program the two FLR counters to 5 and 7, run a cool reset and require 5 and 7, run a cold reset and require both counters to read their reset values.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ISO-RETAIN-S1` |`ISOLATE_REQ_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_SMC_REG` and `ISOLATE_REQ_SMCEN_REG` read their programmed values after a cool reset.
|`CHK-ISO-RETAIN-S2` |`ISOLATE_REQ_REG`, `ISOLATE_REQ_SMC_REG` and `ISOLATE_REQ_SMCEN_REG` read their reset values after a cold reset with GPIO 53 low.
|`CHK-ISO-RETAIN-S3` |`ISOLATE_REQ_PINEN_REG` reads its reset value after a cold reset with GPIO 53 low and keeps its programmed value after a cold reset with GPIO 53 high.
|`CHK-ISO-RETAIN-S4` |Both FLR counters keep 5 and 7 across a cool reset and read their reset values after a cold reset.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-136-smu_ndm_reset_handshake_test]]
===== 136 `smu_ndm_reset_handshake_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_ndm_reset_handshake_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_ndm_reset_handshake_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` on the DTP TAP with the SMC JTAG2AXI frontdoor (`tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read low before any access); TB pins `tb_smc_ndmreset_request` (drives `smc_ndmreset_request_i`), `tb_smc_ndmreset_process` (observes `smc_ndmreset_process_o`) and `rst_cold_ni`; observation of `rst_cold_n_o` and `obs_smc_rst_n_o`; the SMC registers `NDMRESET_REQUEST`, `NDMRESET_PROCESS` and `NDMRESET_CLUSTER_COUNT`; `clk_ref_i` and `clk_smu_i`; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: `NDMRESET_REQUEST` mirrors the four cluster request inputs, `NDMRESET_CLUSTER_COUNT` reads 4, and `NDMRESET_PROCESS` stores 32 bits while only its low four bits reach `smc_ndmreset_process_o`.

*Test Coverage*:
