[[sec-146-smu_xtrig_cold_reset_clears_ctn_test]]
===== 146 `smu_xtrig_cold_reset_clears_ctn_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xtrig_cold_reset_clears_ctn_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_xtrig_cold_reset_clears_ctn_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` primary TAP and the SMC JTAG2AXI bridge to the DTP CSR window (with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); the cold reset input `rst_cold_ni`, which the leaf pulses low for 64 `clk_ref_i` cycles; `obs_dtp_rst_n_o`, `dtp_stop_clks_o` and `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` sampled on `clk_smu_i`; bench pad inputs `xtrig_ctm_dst_req` (driven as the source of the routed trigger), `xtrig_ctm_src_ack`, `xtrig_clk_stop_req` (driven as the clock-stop request), `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_in_din` and `tb_xtrig_ctp_ack_in_din` (bit 1 driven as the acknowledge); the bench output `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_out_dout` bit 1; cross-trigger network registers CT_SRC[0], CT_SRC[1], CTP[0].CONFIG, CTP[0].STRETCH_MULT, CTP[1].CONFIG and CTP[1].STATUS at the addresses of the generated cross-trigger headers; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: the SMU cold reset returns the programmed cross-trigger network registers to their reset values, drops a point-to-point request in flight on CTP 1, and holds `dtp_stop_clks_o` low while the DTP reset is asserted even with a clock-stop request held.

*Test Coverage*:

* With every cross-trigger and CTP pad input idle, CT_SRC[0], CTP[0].CONFIG and CTP[0].STRETCH_MULT are written with `0x10`, `0x3` and `0x55`, values the leaf chooses, and read back as written.
* After the cold reset the three registers read their RDL resets: CT_DST_SELECT 0 in `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/regs/cross_trigger_matrix.rdl`, and MODE, INVERT, RESET and STRETCH_MULT 0 in `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_port/regs/cross_trigger_port.rdl`; `hw/sys/dtp/doc/clk_rst.adoc` ("Reset Architecture") says `rst_n_i` resets all CTN and CSR state.
* With CTP 1 in point-to-point mode and CT_SRC[1] routing the DTP internal source lane that `xtrig_ctm_dst_req` drives, a trigger raises bit 1 of the request output within a bound of 64 `clk_smu_i` edges; the bound is the value the leaf sets, and a cold reset applied with the request out brings that bit low within 16 `clk_smu_i` edges of the DTP reset assertion and keeps it low through the release. `hw/sys/dtp/doc/clk_rst.adoc` ("Reset Architecture") says `rst_n_i` resets all CTN and CSR state; the 16-edge bound is the value the leaf sets.
* The same cold reset applied in the state the sender reaches after the acknowledge input is held, where the sender drives the request low, ends with the request low, and CTP[1].STATUS reads its reset value 0 after the reset in both cases (`cross_trigger_port.rdl`, STATUS fields reset 0); in the held-acknowledge case the acknowledge is released and eight `clk_smu_i` cycles pass before that read.
* The STATUS value read while the acknowledge is still held after the reset is logged and never compared.
* With `xtrig_clk_stop_req` held and `dtp_stop_clks_o` observed high, a cold reset leaves `dtp_stop_clks_o` low on every `clk_smu_i` sample from the first with the DTP reset low through the first with it high again, as `clk_rst.adoc` says the clock-stop output resets to 0; the samples taken in the eight edges after that are logged and not compared.
* The reset of the JTAG logic is out of scope here: the leaf rebuilds the TAP after each cold reset.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for reset done, require every cross-trigger and CTP pad input idle, reset the TAP, read the IDCODE and require the JTAG2AXI gate open.
. Write CT_SRC[0], CTP[0].CONFIG and CTP[0].STRETCH_MULT and read each back.
. Pulse `rst_cold_ni` low, wait for the DTP reset and the primary reset to release, rebuild the TAP, and read the three registers for their reset values.
. For each of the request-out and held-acknowledge legs, set CTP[1] to point-to-point, route CT_SRC[1], pulse `xtrig_ctm_dst_req`, require bit 1 of the request output high, in the second leg hold the acknowledge and require the request low, pulse the cold reset while tracing the request output every `clk_smu_i` edge, rebuild the TAP, then read CTP[1].STATUS and sample the request output.
. Hold `xtrig_clk_stop_req`, wait for `dtp_stop_clks_o` high, pulse the cold reset while tracing `dtp_stop_clks_o`, release the request and rebuild the TAP.
. Log the last state of every bounded wait, require none expired and every declared wait kind exercised.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CTN-RESET-CLEARS-REGS` |CT_SRC[0], CTP[0].CONFIG and CTP[0].STRETCH_MULT read their programmed values before the cold reset and their RDL reset value 0 after it
|`CHK-CTP-RESET-MID-REQUEST` |bit 1 of the request output is low within 16 `clk_smu_i` edges of the reset assertion and stays low, and CTP[1].STATUS reads 0 (only `ACK_IN` while the acknowledge is still held) and 0 after its release, from the request-out and held-acknowledge states
|`CHK-CLKSTOP-RESET-LOW` |`dtp_stop_clks_o` is 0 on every sample while the DTP reset is asserted with the clock-stop request held and at the release
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |none of the bounded JTAG2AXI, reset, request and clock-stop waits expires
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-147-smu_xtrig_ctm_select_walk_test]]
===== 147 `smu_xtrig_ctm_select_walk_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xtrig_ctm_select_walk_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_xtrig_ctm_select_walk_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` primary TAP and the SMC JTAG2AXI bridge to the DTP CSR window (with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); bench pad inputs `xtrig_ctm_dst_req`, `xtrig_ctm_src_ack`, `xtrig_clk_stop_req`, `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_in_din`, `tb_xtrig_ctp_ack_in_din` and `tb_xtrig_ctp_wire_ext_assert`, which the leaf requires idle at start; the cross-trigger matrix registers CT_SRC[0], CT_SRC[16] and CT_SRC[18] at the addresses of the generated cross-trigger headers; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+expected_sep=1`, `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: every bit of the CT_DST_SELECT field of the cross-trigger matrix source registers stores a written 1 and reads it back, the bits spread over a CT_SRC register of each of three port-index groups, and a written 0 reads back 0.

*Test Coverage*:

* For n from 0 to 25, the value `1 << n` is written to one CT_SRC register and read back unchanged; the field is `CT_DST_SELECT[NUM_CT_DST-1:0]` with `sw = rw` in `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/regs/cross_trigger_matrix.rdl`, 26 bits wide because `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/doc/architecture.adoc` gives N = 26.
* Bit n goes to CT_SRC[0], CT_SRC[16] or CT_SRC[18] as n modulo 3 is 0, 1 or 2; CT_SRC[0] is an external CTP index and CT_SRC[16] and CT_SRC[18] are internal CTP indices, since `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/doc/architecture.adoc` ("Port Indexing") places the internal CTPs after the external ones and gives the defaults of 16 external and 10 internal CTPs, so index 16 is the first internal CTP. The grouping is the leaf's choice; each register takes nine, nine and eight of the 26 bits.
* After the walk, a write of 0 to each of the three registers reads back 0; the RDL says an all-zero value disables that source output.
* The number of walked bits per register is a property of the stimulus; it is logged and not compared.
* No trigger is routed and no output is observed: the effect of a selected bit on the matrix outputs, and every CT_SRC register other than the three used, are out of scope here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for reset done, require every cross-trigger and CTP pad input idle, reset the TAP, read the IDCODE and require the JTAG2AXI gate open.
. For each n from 0 to 25, write `1 << n` to the CT_SRC register of its group and read it back.
. Write 0 to CT_SRC[0], CT_SRC[16] and CT_SRC[18], read each back, and compare the walked-bit readbacks of each register and its cleared value.
. Log the last state of the JTAG2AXI wait and require it did not expire.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CT-SRC-WALK` |CT_DST_SELECT bits 0 to 25 each store the walked bit and read it back exactly
|`CHK-CT-SRC-KINDS` |each of the three CT_SRC index kinds reads back its walked bits and reads 0 after the clear
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the bounded JTAG2AXI wait does not expire
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-148-smu_xtrig_ctn_csr_rw_test]]
===== 148 `smu_xtrig_ctn_csr_rw_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xtrig_ctn_csr_rw_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_xtrig_ctn_csr_rw_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` primary TAP and the SMC JTAG2AXI bridge to the DTP CSR window (with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); bench pad inputs `xtrig_ctm_dst_req`, `xtrig_ctm_src_ack`, `xtrig_clk_stop_req`, `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_in_din`, `tb_xtrig_ctp_ack_in_din` and `tb_xtrig_ctp_wire_ext_assert`, which the leaf requires idle at start; the CONFIG, STATUS and STRETCH_MULT registers of the sixteen cross-trigger ports and the CT_SRC registers of the matrix at the addresses of the generated cross-trigger headers; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+expected_sep=1`, `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: the cross-trigger port CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT registers and the matrix CT_SRC registers store what is written, a STATUS write is acknowledged and changes nothing, and the sixteen port register windows and the matrix registers do not disturb each other.

*Test Coverage*:

* On port 3, CONFIG reads back `0x7`, which sets MODE, INVERT and RESET together, and then `0x0`, and STRETCH_MULT reads back `0x0`, `0x1` and `0xFFFF`; `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_port/regs/cross_trigger_port.rdl` gives the three CONFIG fields and the 16-bit STRETCH_MULT field as `sw = rw`.
* A write of `0xFFFF_FFFF` to port 3 STATUS completes with a SUCCESS bridge status and the STATUS read after it equals the read before it; the RDL says STATUS writes complete with an OKAY response and leave the register unchanged. The STATUS value is read and logged and is not compared with a fixed value.
* The value `0x1000 + i` written to STRETCH_MULT of each port i from 0 to 15 reads back from its own port, port 0 CONFIG reads back `0x1` and port 15 CONFIG reads back `0x6`, and ports 0 and 15 still read their own STRETCH_MULT; the address stride of the port windows follows `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_network/regs/cross_trigger_network.rdl` and its address map in `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_network/doc/architecture.adoc`.
* A write of `0x1` to CT_SRC[0] followed by a read, then a write of `0x2A5A` to port 0 STRETCH_MULT followed by a read, all interleaved over JTAG2AXI with each access completing before the next is issued, return the written values; four alternating reads of CT_SRC[25], port 15 STRETCH_MULT, CT_SRC[0] and port 0 STRETCH_MULT return 0, `0x100F`, `0x1` and `0x2A5A`; the leaf writes the last three and never writes CT_SRC[25], whose reset value 0 the RDL gives.
* The CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT values are the values the leaf programs; the effect of a CONFIG mode on the pads and of a STRETCH_MULT value on a pulse width are out of scope here.
* The leaf clears CT_SRC[0], CT_SRC[25] and CONFIG of ports 0 and 15 at the end and does not restore the other STRETCH_MULT values.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for reset done, require every cross-trigger and CTP pad input idle, reset the TAP, read the IDCODE and require the JTAG2AXI gate open.
. Write and read back CONFIG `0x7` and `0x0` and STRETCH_MULT `0x0`, `0x1` and `0xFFFF` on port 3.
. Read port 3 STATUS, write `0xFFFF_FFFF` to it, and read it again.
. Write `0x1000 + i` to STRETCH_MULT of ports 0 to 15, read each back, write CONFIG of ports 0 and 15, and read both CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT of those two ports.
. Write and read back CT_SRC[0] and port 0 STRETCH_MULT, read CT_SRC[25], port 15 STRETCH_MULT, CT_SRC[0] and port 0 STRETCH_MULT in turn, and clear CT_SRC[0], CT_SRC[25] and the CONFIG of ports 0 and 15.
. Log the last state of the JTAG2AXI wait and require it did not expire.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CTP-CFG-STRETCH-RW` |port 3 CONFIG reads back `0x7` and `0x0` and STRETCH_MULT reads back `0x0`, `0x1` and `0xFFFF` as written
|`CHK-CTP-STATUS-WRITE-IGNORED` |a STATUS write completes with success and the next read equals the read before it
|`CHK-CTP-WINDOW-INDEPENDENCE` |each of the 16 ports reads back its own STRETCH_MULT and ports 0 and 15 their own CONFIG
|`CHK-CTN-SERIALISED-ACCESS` |interleaved CT_SRC and CTP accesses over JTAG2AXI return the values last written to each register
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the bounded JTAG2AXI wait does not expire
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-149-smu_xtrig_ctn_reset_values_test]]
===== 149 `smu_xtrig_ctn_reset_values_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xtrig_ctn_reset_values_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_xtrig_ctn_reset_values_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` primary TAP and the SMC JTAG2AXI bridge to the DTP CSR window (with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); bench pad inputs `xtrig_ctm_dst_req`, `xtrig_ctm_src_ack`, `xtrig_clk_stop_req`, `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_in_din`, `tb_xtrig_ctp_ack_in_din` and `tb_xtrig_ctp_wire_ext_assert`, which the leaf requires idle before the first read; the 26 CT_SRC registers of the cross-trigger matrix and CONFIG, STATUS and STRETCH_MULT of the 16 cross-trigger ports at the addresses of the generated cross-trigger headers; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+expected_sep=1`, `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: after reset, with every cross-trigger and CTP pad input idle, all 26 matrix source registers and the three registers of each of the 16 cross-trigger ports read 0 over the SMC bridge.

*Test Coverage*:

* Each CT_SRC[n].CONFIG_0 register for n from 0 to 25 is read with a SUCCESS bridge status and returns 0; `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/regs/cross_trigger_matrix.rdl` gives CT_DST_SELECT a reset of 0.
* CONFIG, STATUS and STRETCH_MULT of each of the 16 ports read 0, as `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_port/regs/cross_trigger_port.rdl` gives for the fields of all three registers; the STATUS fields are live readouts of the port handshake, so the idle pad inputs are a precondition of the STATUS reads.
* The register counts and strides come from the generated cross-trigger network headers (`hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_network/regs/cross_trigger_network.rdl`), and the leaf requires the JTAG2AXI access count to equal the 74 reads it issues.
* The reads are the only accesses: nothing is written, and the behaviour of the registers after a write is out of scope here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for reset done, require every cross-trigger and CTP pad input idle, reset the TAP, read the IDCODE and require the JTAG2AXI gate open.
. Read CT_SRC[0] to CT_SRC[25] and compare each with 0.
. Read CONFIG, STATUS and STRETCH_MULT of ports 0 to 15 and compare each with 0.
. Log the last state of the JTAG2AXI wait, require it did not expire, and require the access count to match.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-CTN-CTSRC-RESET` |all 26 CT_SRC CONFIG_0 reads succeed and return 0
|`CHK-CTN-CTP-RESET` |CONFIG, STATUS and STRETCH_MULT of all 16 ports read 0
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the bounded JTAG2AXI wait does not expire
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-150-smu_xtrig_ctp_status_reset_test]]
===== 150 `smu_xtrig_ctp_status_reset_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xtrig_ctp_status_reset_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_xtrig_ctp_status_reset_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` primary TAP and the SMC JTAG2AXI bridge to the DTP CSR window (with `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` read as open); bench pad inputs `tb_xtrig_ctp_wire_ext_assert` (bit 4 pulled as the trigger, bit 3 pulled on the wire-OR port), `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_in_din` and `tb_xtrig_ctp_ack_in_din` (bit 2 driven as the far end of port 2), and `xtrig_ctm_dst_req`, `xtrig_ctm_src_ack` and `xtrig_clk_stop_req`, which the leaf requires idle at start; bench pad outputs `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_out_dout`, `tb_xtrig_ctp_ack_out_dout` and `tb_xtrig_ctp_req_out_din` sampled on `clk_smu_i`; CONFIG and STATUS of cross-trigger ports 2 and 3 and CT_SRC[2] at the addresses of the generated cross-trigger headers; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+expected_sep=1`, `+rom_hex=smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: the STATUS register of a cross-trigger port reports the handshake and pad levels in point-to-point mode at both polarities and REQ_OUT and ACK_OUT in wire-OR mode, and CONFIG.RESET drops a stuck outgoing request, discards triggers while set, and lets the first trigger after it clears start a new handshake.

*Test Coverage*:

* Port 2 is set to point-to-point and CT_SRC[2] selects port 4, so that a pad pull on the wire-OR port 4 raises a request on port 2; CONFIG and CT_SRC[2] read back what the leaf wrote, and the select bit follows the matrix rule that bit k of CT_SRC[n] routes port k into port n (`hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/regs/cross_trigger_matrix.rdl`).
* STATUS.BUSY reads 0 before the trigger and 1 while the request is outstanding, and reads 0 within four STATUS reads after the acknowledge completes the handshake; `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_port/regs/cross_trigger_port.rdl` gives BUSY as a pulse or handshake in progress. `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_port/doc/architecture.adoc` states that STATUS.BUSY rises two `clk_i` cycles after a state machine leaves IDLE and falls two cycles after both return to IDLE; the leaf does not compare that delay, and reads BUSY only after settle waits and within its four-read poll.
* At INVERT 0 and at INVERT 1, with the asserted pad level high and low respectively, the request output pad rests at its idle level before the trigger, STATUS.REQ_OUT reads 1 with the request outstanding, STATUS.ACK_IN reads 1 while the acknowledge pad is held asserted, the acknowledge output pad rests at its idle level before a request arrives, and STATUS.REQ_IN and STATUS.ACK_OUT both read 1 while the request pad is held asserted and the port acknowledges; the RDL says these fields read the levels with INVERT undone.
* On wire-OR port 3, with its wire pulled, STATUS.REQ_OUT and STATUS.ACK_OUT read 0, as the RDL gives for wire-OR mode; STATUS.ACK_IN and STATUS.REQ_IN on that port are logged as measured and not compared.
* With a request stuck out on port 2 and STATUS.REQ_OUT at 1, setting CONFIG.RESET lowers the request output pad and clears STATUS.REQ_OUT; the RDL says RESET holds the outgoing handshake idle with the request deasserted.
* A trigger arriving while CONFIG.RESET is set raises no request on any of 64 `clk_smu_i` samples, the RDL saying such a trigger is discarded. After RESET is cleared, with the wire released, no request rises on any of 64 `clk_smu_i` samples, so the discarded trigger is not held over; the first trigger after that raises a new request and an acknowledge retires it.
* With CONFIG.RESET set, a request held at the request-in pad makes the acknowledge-out pad rise and STATUS.BUSY read 1, and releasing the request makes the acknowledge fall and BUSY clear within four reads; the RDL and the architecture document say the incoming handshake keeps running while RESET is set.
* The wire-OR pad polarity at INVERT 1 and the cross-trigger destination output that a received request produces are out of scope here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for reset done, require every cross-trigger and CTP pad input idle, reset the TAP, read the IDCODE and require the JTAG2AXI gate open.
. Set port 2 CONFIG to point-to-point, write CT_SRC[2] to select port 4, and read both back.
. Read STATUS, pull the port 4 wire, wait for the request output pad, read STATUS, hold the acknowledge until the request drops, release the wire and poll STATUS until BUSY clears.
. At INVERT 0 run the trigger, acknowledge and request-in sequence reading STATUS at each held level, then pull the wire of port 3 and read its STATUS; set port 2 to wire-OR, preset its request-in and acknowledge-in pads high, set point-to-point with INVERT, read CONFIG back, and run the same sequence at INVERT 1.
. Trigger a request, read STATUS, set CONFIG.RESET and read the pad and STATUS, pull the wire for 64 `clk_smu_i` cycles with RESET set, release the wire, clear RESET, sample the request output pad for 64 `clk_smu_i` cycles, trigger again and acknowledge.
. Set CONFIG.RESET, hold the request-in pad, read the acknowledge pad and STATUS, release the request-in pad, and poll STATUS until BUSY clears; restore wire-OR mode and clear CT_SRC[2].
. Log the last state of every bounded wait, require none expired and both declared wait kinds exercised.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-STATUS-BUSY` |STATUS.BUSY reads 1 while the request is outstanding and 0 after the handshake completes
|`CHK-STATUS-LEVELS` |REQ_OUT, ACK_IN, REQ_IN and ACK_OUT read 1 at INVERT 0 and at INVERT 1, and REQ_OUT and ACK_OUT read 0 in wire-OR mode
|`CHK-HS-RESET-RECOVER` |RESET drops a stuck request and clears REQ_OUT, and the first trigger after RESET clears raises the request again
|`CHK-HS-RESET-DISCARD` |the request output pad stays low for 64 `clk_smu_i` cycles while a trigger arrives with RESET set, and for 64 cycles after RESET clears with the wire released
|`CHK-HS-RESET-RX` |with RESET set a request in is acknowledged and BUSY holds until the request is released
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |none of the bounded JTAG2AXI, pad settle, discard window and BUSY poll waits expires
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
==== JTAG

[[sec-044-smu_dtp_bsr_extest_loopback_test]]
===== 44 `smu_dtp_bsr_extest_loopback_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_bsr_extest_loopback_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: EXTEST DR loopback + instruction decode.

*Test Coverage*:

* EXTEST BSR scan loopback.
* Loads IR=EXTEST, shifts compact 8-bit patterns through the TB scan_in<-scan_out loopback, and checks TDO returns the pattern retimed by one TCK (`(pattern << 1) & mask`): the zero-length chain feeds TDI through the DUT's IEEE 1149.1 falling-edge TDO retimer, so observed bit i is pattern bit i-1 and observed bit 0 is the retimer's scan-entry content (0, TDI held low during TAP navigation).
* Also checks one-hot EXTEST decode.
* STUB:DECLARED `BSR_TB_SCAN_LOOPBACK`: tb_top ties `jtag_bsr_host_scan_in_i` <- `jtag_bsr_host_scan_out_o` over `DTP_BSR_MODEL_LEN` cells (compact 8-bit model); the stand-in proves the TB EXTEST DR path only, not a pad BSR or a SEP STAP (record: *Scan-chain loopbacks* in `SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`). Patterns whose retimed expectation is all-zero are forbidden: the JTAG driver maps an X/Z TDO to 0, which would make an all-zero expectation unable to fail.
* Nonzero expectations remain sensitive to stuck-0 / unresolved TDO.
* All TAP driving goes through the VIP sequence API (OcahJtagMasterSequence); the raw driver built by make_smu_jtag_tap is wrapped, never called directly here.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run the test body `cocotb_wrapper/tests/smu_dtp_bsr_extest_loopback_test.py` over the wrapper's product interfaces; it drives the TAP and pins directly and has no separate sequence file.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-BSR-EXTEST` |Evidence token `BSR_EXTEST_TDO_MATCH`: TB BSR scan loopback TDO (declared stub; pad BSR out of scope)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `smc_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-045-smu_dtp_bsr_ijtag_scan_test]]
===== 45 `smu_dtp_bsr_ijtag_scan_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_bsr_ijtag_scan_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: EXTEST/SAMPLE_PRELOAD BSR select; no Force / no sep_in.

*Test Coverage*:

* DTP BSR EXTEST/SAMPLE_PRELOAD instruction-gated .select.
* S1: Idle RTI `tb_bsr_select` low, then IDCODE IR+DR with live TCK while BSR select-high TCK count stays 0 (instruction gate, not TCK fanout).
* S2: EXTEST IR+DR: BSR select-high TCK count equals TAP DR-state width.
* S3: SAMPLE_PRELOAD IR+DR after TLR: same BSR select bar.
* TDO bits are not compared.
* `jtag_bsr_host_scan_ctrl_o.select` is TAP-state AND instruction-gated and is low in RTI, so samples must run *during* the scan, not after `back_to_rti`.
* Distinct from `smu_dtp_bsr_extest_loopback_test` (TDO loopback).
* Not claimed: pad BSR I/O, DFD/iJTAG SIB open, TDO payload match.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive `smu_dtp_bsr_ijtag_scan_test_seq` over the wrapper's product interfaces.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DTP-BSR-IJTAG-JTAG-READY` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-BSR-IDLE-SELECT` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-BSR-UNSEL-IDCODE` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-BSR-EXTEST-SELECT` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-BSR-SAMPLE-PRELOAD-SELECT` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|assertion |bsr_ijtag incomplete s1=<value> s2=<value> s3=<value>
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-049-smu_dtp_jtag2axi_smc_error_path_test]]
===== 49 `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_smc_error_path_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_smc_error_path_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric J2A unmapped DECERR + VERSION_LO recovery; gate tied open.

*Test Coverage*:

[[sec-053-smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test]]
===== 53 `smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: SMU_ALL_005: PTAP IDCODE + BYPASS + TRST/POR → Test-Logic-Reset.

*Test Coverage*:

* Scenarios (PTAP only): DTP-JTAG-PTAP.S1 — IDCODE returns the configured identification fields; S2 — BYPASS places a single-bit register between TDI and TDO; S3 — TRST or power-on returns the TAP to Test-Logic-Reset, and the leaf logs the cells rst=TRST, rst=POR and state=Test-Logic-Reset. No Force or deposit.
* The TRST leg starts in Shift-DR and holds TMS low through the TCK cycles of the assert, so the TMS path is the Shift-DR self-loop and the exported TAP state can read Test-Logic-Reset only through the reset; the state is sampled while TRST is still asserted. The POR leg clocks no TCK. In both shapes a TRST that does not reach the TAP expires the `s4_trst_tlr` wait with the last state logged.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive `smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test_seq` over the wrapper's product interfaces.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

The scenario has two implementations that share this claim: the cocotb
`smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test_seq.py` and the SV-UVM `smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test_seq.svh`
(`--framework uvm`). The SV-UVM shape drives the same S1..S5 through the shared
JTAG VIP on the primary TAP, samples the DUT's one-hot TAP state through
`dtp_tb_if`, and runs the scenario at least 16 passes in one simulation, the
power-on reset leg restoring the released-reset baseline before the next pass.
Independently of the sequence, the embedded DTP's reference models predict the
decoded instruction of every IR load, the IDCODE capture from the documented default
identification fields (all zero over the marker bit) and the BYPASS capture from the
shifted pattern, and the always-on scoreboard pairs
them with the reconstructed scans.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DTP-JTAG-PTAP-S1` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-JTAG-PTAP-S2` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-JTAG-PTAP-S3` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-NONVAC` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): nine bounded waits (TAP-state confirmations, the TRST and POR returns to Test-Logic-Reset) each name a finite bound and their last state; the site count and the expired count are compared
|`CHK-SB-MIN-ACTIVITY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the `idcode` and `bypass` features' comparison counts, from the always-on scoreboard, are at or above the floor the pass implies (one IDCODE scan; one directed plus `random_count` BYPASS scans)
|`CHK-SB-IR-DECODE`, `CHK-SB-IDCODE`, `CHK-SB-BYPASS` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): always-on scoreboard, the embedded DTP's `ir_decode`, `idcode` and `bypass` features reused unchanged (`dtp_ir_decode_ref_model`, `dtp_idcode_ref_model`, `dtp_bypass_ref_model` on the JTAG event and scan streams). A required feature with zero comparisons fails the run
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `smoke`, `hosted`. Under `--framework uvm` the SV-UVM rows above govern instead, and `+SMU_PTAP_IDCODE_NEGATIVE` must fail the run.

'''''

[[sec-055-smu_dtp_otp_smc_map_rw_test]]

[[sec-056-smu_dtp_otp_smc_series_error_test]]
===== 56 `smu_dtp_otp_smc_series_error_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_otp_smc_series_error_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: OTP J2A series NO_INCR + MAP-CTRL hole SLVERR; gate open on the SEP=1 wrapper.

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP JTAG2AXI series NO_INCR R/W + MAP-CTRL hole SLVERR (SEP=1, no Force).
* S1: After TCK sync, `tb_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable` reads 0 -- the OTP JTAG2AXI gate is open in this configuration.
* `lc_state_o` equals the no-LCC word of `seq_lib/smu_lifecycle_table.py`, so the eFuse JTAG demux is not err_slv.
* S2: Series NO_INCR write PATTERN_A to MAP SPARE[0], then series NO_INCR readback OKAY + data match.
* S3: SINGLE_OP read of SPARE[0] (same IR as the hole) returns SUCCESS + PATTERN_A; SINGLE_OP read of MAP_BASE+MAP_SIZE (below CTRL) returns SLVERR + `0xbadcab1e`; SINGLE_OP re-read of SPARE[0] still SUCCESS + PATTERN_A.
* SPARE[0] from `smc_addr.h` `SMC_TOP_SMC_EFUSE_MAP_SPARE_BASE_ADDR(0)`.
* Hole from `SMC_TOP_SMC_EFUSE_MAP_BASE_ADDR + SMC_TOP_SMC_EFUSE_MAP_SIZE`.
* Not claimed: Force-closed gate; SEP OTP; INCR across MAP fields; SHIM-unmapped DECERR at 0xC000D000 (needs a real eFuse SHIM or TB err_slv).
