[[sec-058-smu_dtp_smc_stap_smoke_test]]
===== 58 `smu_dtp_smc_stap_smoke_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_smc_stap_smoke_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: SMC STAP TRST + TAP_3DCR via tdo_oen; no Force / no sep_in.

*Test Coverage*:

* DTP-SMC-STAP: TRST + TAP_3DCR select via select-gated host TMS.
* S1: PTAP TRST (active-low) is visible on `tb_stap_smc_trst_n`.
* S2: Same IDCODE while SMC STAP is *not* selected: `tb_stap_smc_tms` stays at tms_hold and must *not* track `jtag_tms`.
* After TAP_3DCR selects SMC, the same IDCODE must make host TMS follow PTAP TMS (stap_sel mux).
* S3: TRST restores the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR. Before any re-select, the same IDCODE scan as the unselected control runs and `tb_stap_smc_tms` and `tb_stap_smc_tdo_oen` must stay quiet while the PTAP TMS edges show the scan ran; then re-select, IDCODE with non-zero DR.
* TMS is held low through every TRST assert, so the TAP has no path to Test-Logic-Reset other than the reset, and the 3DCR (held across Test-Logic-Reset by `config_hold`) can be cleared only by the reset.
* TDI/TCK fan out without `stap_sel`; they are not the select proof.
* `tb_stap_smc_tdo_oen` is logged when live.
* Not claimed: SMC DTM abstract commands, STAP slave BFM IDCODE, real LCC.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive `smu_dtp_smc_stap_smoke_test_seq` over the wrapper's product interfaces.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-JTAG-READY` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-TRST` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-UNSEL` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-UNSEL-OEN` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-IDCODE` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-IDCODE-OEN` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-IDCODE-TMS-MATCH` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-TRST-DESEL` |after the S3 TRST and before any re-select, an IDCODE scan leaves SMC STAP TMS and tdo_oen quiet (edges 0, oen TCKs 0) while the PTAP TMS edges show the scan ran
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-IDCODE-BFM` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-PAYLOAD-OEN` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-DTP-SMC-STAP-PAYLOAD-TMS-MATCH` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|assertion |smc_stap incomplete s1=<value> s2=<value> s3=<value>
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-059-smu_jtag_chain_enhanced_test]]
===== 59 `smu_jtag_chain_enhanced_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_jtag_chain_enhanced_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: PTAP IDCODE + BYPASS after VIP TCK period reprogram; no Force.

*Test Coverage*:

* PTAP IDCODE + BYPASS at 1/5/10/20 MHz TCK (SEP=1, no Force).
* S1: TAP ready at the default period (IDCODE).
* S2: For each programmed VIP TCK period, IDCODE matches the IEEE packing.
* S3: At each period, BYPASS is a one-bit register (8-bit TDI, TDO is capture-0 then TDI delayed one bit).
* S4: Completeness vs the independent required set (labels + programmed VIP `tck_period_ns`).
* A missing `FREQ_NS` leg or a plusarg that pins every TAP to one period fails here.
* Does not claim analog setup/hold vs silicon; FAIL-ON is wrong IDCODE, BYPASS delay, missing required frequency, or VIP period not matching the required nanosecond set.
* Not claimed: Force feat_ctrl; JTAG2AXI.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive `smu_jtag_chain_enhanced_test_seq` over the wrapper's product interfaces.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-JTAG-CHAIN-READY` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-JTAG-CHAIN-FREQ` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-JTAG-IDCODE` |Evidence token `JTAG_IDCODE_OK`: IDCODE reads the default DTP IDCODE at every swept TCK period
|`CHK-JTAG-BYPASS` |Evidence token `JTAG_BYPASS_OK`: BYPASS returns the expected shifted pattern at every swept TCK period
|`CHK-JTAG-CHAIN-ENHANCED` |Evidence token `JTAG_CHAIN_ENHANCED_OK`: all required TCK labels ran at their programmed periods
|assertion |jtag_chain_enhanced incomplete s1=<value> s2=<value>
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-060-smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_smoke_test]]
===== 60 `smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_smoke_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_smoke_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric J2A SCRATCH_15 + SPM + series INCR; gate open on the SEP=1 wrapper.

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric JTAG2AXI smoke: gate open, CAPS, SCRATCH_15, SPM, series INCR.
* S1: After TCK sync, `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` is 0 and `SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS` matches the RTL 14-bit packing.
* S2: SINGLE_OP 32b write/readback of CPU_CTRL SCRATCH_15 (not SCRATCH_0 — that mailbox is the live boot ROM PASS magic).
* S3: SINGLE_OP 64b write/readback of SPM base.
* S4: Series DATA_INCR write then readback at SPM+0x40 — data match.
* Addresses from `smc_addr.h`.
* Not claimed: Force-closed gate; OTP J2A; unmapped DECERR; NO_INCR / WITH_ERROR_STATUS series modes.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive `smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_smoke_test_seq` over the wrapper's product interfaces.
. Compare every access against its stated expectation in the scoreboard.
. Emit the evidence tokens listed under Checkers.

The scenario has two implementations that share this claim: the cocotb
`smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_smoke_test_seq.py` and the SV-UVM
`smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_smoke_test_seq.svh` (`--framework uvm`). The SV-UVM shape packs
and decodes every bridge register through the `dtp_env_pkg` codec the DTP bench
uses for this bridge, takes the expected CAPS word from the integrator guide's
default parameters and the addresses from the generated `smc_top_addrmap_pkg`, runs
the directed patterns
on pass 0 and seeded patterns on the later passes, and raises the TCK period to at
least four SMU clocks (the cocotb `min_jtag_smu_ratio` clamp). Independently of the
sequence, the embedded DTP's `dtp_jtag2axi_req_ref_model` pairs every transaction a
passive monitor sees on the DTP's SMC debug port with the request the scan encoded,
and `dtp_jtag2axi_status_ref_model` pairs every SINGLE_OP and SERIES_CTRL capture
with the completion the bus returned.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-JTAG-READY` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-GATE-OPEN` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-SCRATCH` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-SPM` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-SERIES-INCR` |Evidence token, emitted only after the compares it names have held
|assertion |jtag2axi_smoke incomplete s1=<value> s2=<value> s3=<value> s4=<value>
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-JTAG-READY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): IDCODE equals the SMU-configured value after the TAP reset
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-GATE-OPEN` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the bridge's lifecycle gate reads 0 and SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS equals the geometry the integrator guide's default parameters state (rd/wr pipeline depth 3, 8-byte data, 56-bit address, AXI4)
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-SCRATCH` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): SINGLE_OP 32-bit write and readback of CPU_CTRL SCRATCH_15, both SUCCESS, data equal
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-SPM` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): SINGLE_OP 64-bit write and readback of the SPM base word, both SUCCESS, data equal
|`CHK-JTAG2AXI-SMOKE-SERIES-INCR` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): SERIES_CTRL + SERIES_DATA_INCR write then readback at SPM+0x40, both SUCCESS, data equal
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): eight bounded waits (the TAP-state confirmation and seven status polls, the last draining the pipelined series read) each name a finite bound and their last status; none expired
|`CHK-SB-MIN-ACTIVITY` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): the `jtag2axi_req` and `jtag2axi_status` features' comparison counts, from the always-on scoreboard, are at or above the seven bridge transactions the pass launches
|`CHK-SB-JTAG2AXI-REQ`, `CHK-SB-JTAG2AXI-STATUS` |SV-UVM shape only (`--framework uvm`): always-on scoreboard, the embedded DTP's features reused unchanged: every bridge transaction observed by the passive monitor on `smu.dtp_axi_smc_dbg_req/resp` matches the direction, address, size, strobes and strobed data of the request the scan encoded, and every status capture carries the completion's status and, after a read, its data. A required feature with zero comparisons fails the run
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`. Under `--framework uvm` the SV-UVM rows above govern instead, and `+SMU_J2A_SCOREBOARD_NEGATIVE` must fail the run.

'''''

[[sec-061-smu_dtp_scan_chain_boundary_test]]
===== 61 `smu_dtp_scan_chain_boundary_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_scan_chain_boundary_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` on the primary TAP; TB returns each iJTAG host scan_out to its scan_in through one bench scan cell (a one-bit `prim_jtag_scan_reg` on the host's scan control, capturing 0) and each STAP host TDO to its TDI on a bare net, at the wrapper pins; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The iJTAG SIB hosts and the secondary TAPs leave and re-enter `smu_wrapper` at the pads, and the shift path runs through those boundary pins.

*Test Coverage*:

* The leaf gates the three iJTAG hosts by instruction (all three select under SELECT_IJTAG with their SIBs open, none under IDCODE with the SIBs still open). It measures the iJTAG DR with every SIB open, six cells because each open SIB puts its host's bench cell in the path, and with every SIB closed, three cells, and reads each host's scan-out pin on every Shift-DR TCK of the open pass. It round-trips the SIB enables. With the extra STAP and then the I/O STAP selected it reads a network IDCODE scan of the documented length and the bits leaving at the STAP's pad TDO. Every checked DR shift requires the PTAP TDO resolved on each TCK `jtag_tdo_oen` drives, since the JTAG VIP maps an X or Z TDO to 0.
* The loops are a bench stand-in: they prove the DTP's host-side decode, gating and shift ordering against a one-cell iJTAG segment and a bare STAP return, not any pad or adopter chain. They are declared in the *Bench stand-ins and exceptions* section of `SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc` (Scan-chain loopbacks).
* Expected behaviour comes from the JTAG specification pages, not the RTL. iJTAG: `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_intf_unit/doc/architecture.adoc` *iJTAG Network* (three SIBs from TDI, DFT secure, DFT non-secure, DFD; each one bit; an open SIB inserts its segment on the TDO side of its bit). TDR selection by instruction, SELECT_IJTAG selecting the network, the falling-edge TDO retimer and STAP selection replacing the TDR mux output: `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc` *TDR Multiplexer*, *iJTAG Support*, *TDO Retiming*. STAP host TMS parked at TMS-Hold (reset value 0) when unselected and following the PTAP when selected, `host_tdo_oen_o` from `stap_sel` and the TAP controls, the falling-edge TDO lockup on every STAP and the TDI lockup that adds one cycle: `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_stap/doc/architecture.adoc` *STAP Module*, *3DCR Register*, *Lockup Latches*; the JIU STAP chain hierarchy table enables TDI lockup on the I/O STAP only. SEP=1 STAP chain order I/O, SMC debug, SEP debug, extra, one SIB each: `hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc` *STAP Secondary Scan Path* and the Integrator Guide's *STAP Scan Chain Topology* (`JTAG_STAP_IO_ENABLE=1`, `JTAG_SMC_DBG_ENABLE=1`, `JTAG_NUM_EXTRA_STAPS=1`). IEEE 1149.1 gives a DR of N cells N TCKs of shift latency; the falling-edge retimer is no cell.
* Spec gaps carried as DV-owned rules: no page defines `jtag_scan_ctrl_t.select`, so the host select is expected to follow IEEE 1687 SIB semantics -- asserted only while the network is the active scan path and that SIB is open; the STAP page does not list the TAP states that assert `host_tdo_oen_o`, so the enable is expected for exactly the Shift-IR and Shift-DR TCKs (IEEE 1149.1 Section 4.5.1, with IEEE 1838 running the STAP through both scans). The value the I/O STAP's lockup pair holds at Capture-DR is undefined, so that one bit of its network scan is masked.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Under SELECT_IJTAG open all three SIBs and require every host select to assert during a scan with them open and the scan to return the open-chain word; load IDCODE and, from its Update-IR, require no select over an IDCODE DR scan and a second IDCODE IR scan, counted on `jtag_tck`; back under SELECT_IJTAG require the open-chain word again, and close the SIBs.
. Open all three SIBs and shift five directed and three seeded-random nonzero 9-bit payloads with the SIB bits held set; require each back six bits late after the captured 0b101010, and each host scan-out pin to carry the modelled SIB stream on all 15 Shift-DR TCKs. Close the SIBs and shift the same payloads; require each back three bits late after 0b000.
. Update-DR each SIB enable pattern (each alone, all three, none) and require the next scan to return the modelled word (each open SIB reading 1 with its bench cell after it); require the matching host select only while that SIB is open.
. Read the unselected extra STAP quiet, select it through TAP_3DCR with Config-Hold clear, and require a 44-bit network IDCODE scan (36 cells and an 8-bit nonzero tail) to return four SIB bits of 0, the IDCODE and the tail 36 bits late, `tb_stap_extra0_tdo` to carry three SIB captures, the IDCODE LSB first, the tail and the zero fill behind it on its 44 Shift-DR TCKs, its TDO enable to cover exactly the 6 + 44 shift TCKs, host TMS to match on every TCK, and the I/O STAP to drive no TDO enable.
. After a Test-Logic-Reset, which clears that selection, do the same for the I/O STAP: a 45-bit scan (37 cells, one of them the masked lockup bit, and the tail), `tb_stap_io_tdo` carrying the IDCODE, the tail and the zero fill behind it, the enable for exactly 6 + 45 TCKs, and the extra STAP driving no TDO enable.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-IJTAG-GATE` |under SELECT_IJTAG with all three SIBs open every host select is high on at least as many TCKs as the scan shifts and the scan returns the open-chain word; from the Update-IR that loads IDCODE, none of the three selects asserts over an IDCODE DR scan and the IR scan of a second IDCODE load, counted on jtag_tck, and the open-chain word returns again afterwards
|`CHK-SMU-IJTAG-CHAIN` |open pass: with all three SIBs held open the DR is six cells (three SIB bits and the three bench loop cells), each payload returns six bits late after the captured 0b101010, and the dfd, dft and dft_secure host scan-out pins each carry the modelled SIB stream on every Shift-DR TCK, count-gated; closed pass, the control: with every SIB shut the DR is three cells and each payload returns three bits late after 0b000; five directed and three seeded-random nonzero payloads per pass
|`CHK-SMU-IJTAG-SIB` |Update-DR latches the SIB enables and the next scan returns them, each open SIB followed by its bench cell, for each SIB alone, all three, and none; the matching host select asserts only while that SIB is open
|`CHK-SMU-STAP-IO-SELECT` |an unselected I/O STAP drives no TDO enable and its host TMS holds the TMS-Hold reset value 0 on every TCK of an IDCODE scan; selected over TAP_3DCR, a network IDCODE scan of 37 cells (the PTAP register, the I/O STAP's TDO and TDI lockup pair over the bare bench return, one TCK, and four SIBs) and an 8-bit tail returns four SIB bits of 0, the masked lockup bit, the IDCODE and the tail 37 bits late, `tb_stap_io_tdo` carries the IDCODE LSB first then the tail and the zero fill behind it on all 45 Shift-DR TCKs, the enable is high on every Shift-IR and Shift-DR TCK and low on every other TCK, read against the PTAP state, and covers exactly the 6 + 45 shift TCKs, host TMS matches the primary TAP on every TCK, and the extra STAP drives no TDO enable
|`CHK-SMU-STAP-EXTRA-SELECT` |an unselected extra STAP drives no TDO enable and its host TMS holds the TMS-Hold reset value 0 on every TCK of an IDCODE scan; selected over TAP_3DCR without Config-Hold, a network IDCODE scan of 36 cells (the PTAP register, three SIBs ahead, the bare bench return and its own SIB) and an 8-bit tail returns four SIB bits of 0, the IDCODE and the tail 36 bits late, `tb_stap_extra0_tdo` carries three SIB captures, the IDCODE LSB first, the tail and the zero fill behind it on all 44 Shift-DR TCKs, the enable is high on every Shift-IR and Shift-DR TCK and low on every other TCK, read against the PTAP state, and covers exactly the 6 + 44 shift TCKs, host TMS matches the primary TAP on every TCK, and the I/O STAP drives no TDO enable
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-062-smu_otp_bridges_under_dbg_disable_test]]
===== 62 `smu_otp_bridges_under_dbg_disable_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_otp_bridges_under_dbg_disable_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC fabric, SMC OTP and SEP OTP JTAG2AXI bridges); SEP eFuse shadow preload naming a lifecycle state whose posture blocks the fabric bridge; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The SMC and SEP OTP JTAG2AXI bridges complete while the lifecycle posture has closed the SMC fabric bridge.

*Test Coverage*:

* The posture is read, not assumed: `lc_state` must carry the preloaded state and `dbg_disable.smc_jtag2axi` must be asserted before and after all OTP traffic, so the completions are attributable to the blocked-fabric state.
* A SINGLE_OP on the fabric bridge launches neither AW nor AR on the DTP-to-SMC debug AXI port while that posture holds; the OTP bridges on both subsystems write and read back a spare word with SUCCESS in the same state, the SEP OTP bridge returns the {~raw, raw} LC_STATE word the image programmed, and the SEP shim window's EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME takes and returns a written value.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Read `lc_state` and `dbg_disable.smc_jtag2axi` and require the blocking posture.
. Issue a fabric SINGLE_OP and count AW and AR on the DTP-to-SMC debug AXI port; require none.
. Write and read back SMC eFuse MAP SPARE[0] and SEP eFuse MAP SPARE0 over their OTP bridges; read SEP MAP LC_STATE and compare against the image.
. Read, write and read back EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME through the SEP OTP bridge's shim window.
. Re-read `dbg_disable.smc_jtag2axi` and require it still asserted.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-OTP-DBG-POSTURE` |the SEP shadow image puts the chiplet in a lifecycle state whose posture disables dbg_disable.smc_jtag2axi, and lc_state carries that state
|`CHK-OTP-FABRIC-BLOCKED` |a SINGLE_OP on the primary TAP launches neither AW nor AR on the DTP -> SMC debug AXI port while that posture holds
|`CHK-OTP-SMC-RW-WHILE-BLOCKED` |SMC OTP JTAG2AXI write and readback of SMC eFuse MAP SPARE[0] both complete with SUCCESS in the same blocked-fabric state
|`CHK-OTP-SEP-LC-READ-WHILE-BLOCKED` |SEP OTP JTAG2AXI read of SEP eFuse MAP LC_STATE returns the {~raw, raw} word the shadow image programmed
|`CHK-OTP-SEP-RW-WHILE-BLOCKED` |SEP OTP JTAG2AXI write and readback of SEP eFuse MAP SPARE0 both complete with SUCCESS in the same blocked-fabric state
|`CHK-OTP-SEP-BANK-CTRL-READ` |a SEP OTP JTAG2AXI read of the shim window leaves the SEP on the eFuse bank-control port and returns EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME's RDL reset
|`CHK-OTP-SEP-BANK-CTRL-WRITE` |the same port takes a written EFUSE_BANK_INIT_TIME value and reads it back
|`CHK-OTP-DBG-POSTURE-HELD` |dbg_disable.smc_jtag2axi is still asserted after all OTP traffic, so the completions above are attributable to the blocked-fabric state
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-063-smu_xtrig_ctp_pad_test]]
===== 63 `smu_xtrig_ctp_pad_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xtrig_ctp_pad_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (SMC fabric JTAG2AXI into the SMC peripheral window that programs the cross-trigger network); every CT_Req_out pad sits on an `ocah_open_drain_bus` shared wire (private, or the group wire the pads in `tb_xtrig_ctp_wire_group` share), resting at the board pull of the port's INVERT sense, with a bench chiplet driver pulling it -- the other three CTP pad groups keep TB drive of the pad; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The four CTP pad groups of `smu_wrapper` follow the cross-trigger network's mode, handshake and matrix routing at the pads, and a wire-OR port receives cycle-exactly on its shared wire's assertion edge. Every expected level, address and count on the proof path comes from the generated cross-trigger headers or from a DV-owned table transcribed from the cross-trigger specification; the leaf cites no RTL.

*Test Coverage*:

* Register offsets, bitmasks and the CONFIG.MODE reset value come from `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_port/regs/gen/c/` and `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/regs/gen/c/`; the CTP count, the CTM port count and every per-index CTP and CT_SRC address come from `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_network/regs/gen/c/cross_trigger_network_addr.h`. The pad vectors are required to be `CROSS_TRIGGER_NETWORK_CTP_NUM` wide before any compare.
* The pad-control level per mode is a DV-owned table (`CTP_PAD_LEVELS`) transcribed from the CTP signal interface table in `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_port/doc/interface.adoc`: an enable the table calls "enabled" reads 1, one it calls "disabled" or "enabled by a cross trigger pulse" reads 0 with no trigger in flight, and a data output it calls "static-low" reads 0. That table also pins MODE to 0 (wire-OR) at reset, and the leaf raises if the RDL reset value disagrees. Wire-OR reset and one-lane point-to-point are both checked from the same table, so the P2P leg proves the written lane moved and the other lanes did not.
* The five pad-ring controls checked at 0 (`UNDRIVEN_PAD_CONTROLS`) are the `hw/sys/dtp/doc/port_table.adoc` CTP rows with no counterpart in the CTP signal interface table: CT_Req_in and CT_Ack_in are input pads (CTN architecture, `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_network/doc/architecture.adoc`) and carry no output data or output enable; CT_Ack_out is an output pad and carries no input-buffer enable. Spec gap: no specification sentence states the level these controls are driven to. The DV rule is that a control with no driver reads 0, and the checker is graded against that rule, not against a spec statement.
* Spec gap: the signal interface table calls `ct_ack_out_dout` "unused" in wire-OR mode and describes the two point-to-point data outputs only in terms of the request or acknowledge that drives them. The DV rule reads every data output as 0 while no trigger is in flight.
* The CTM port of the internal lane follows the port-indexing table (external CTPs at 0..N-1, internal lanes after; `hw/ip/cross_trigger/cross_trigger_matrix/doc/architecture.adoc`) and the SMU integrator statement that the low DTP internal-CT lanes are reserved for the SMC and the SMU exposes the lanes above them, with the SMU default of 8 exposed lanes (`doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc`, DTP configuration table; `hw/sys/smu/doc/port_table.adoc`). The DTP internal-lane count is the CTM port count less the CTP count from the header, and the `xtrig_ctm_src_req` width is required to equal the exposed count before the port is walked.
* The four-phase handshake runs in both directions at the pads, and a trigger is routed through the cross-trigger matrix to a second port and to an internal cross-trigger lane at `xtrig_ctm_src_req_o`, so the routing is observed at a different pin from the one that sourced it.
* S8 and S9 repeat the point-to-point handshake with matrix routing, and the wire-OR receive, on every one of the `CROSS_TRIGGER_NETWORK_CTP_NUM` lanes, so every lane of every pad group toggles at the pads; S10 routes a trigger into each of the DTP's internal lanes.
* A wire-OR port's `ct_dst` is CT_DST_LATENCY (3 clk_smu: the CTP synchronizer's one 2-FF synchronizer, i.e. two flop stages, plus the registered `ct_dst` output; the same constant as `DTP_CT_DST_LATENCY`) after its shared wire is first seen asserted, never at the release, and never through a reset with the wire resting. A per-clk_smu-cycle activity sampler records the cycle each observable first went high and how many times it rose, so the latency and the one-pulse-per-assertion bound are both cycle-exact.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. With every CT_Req_out wire resting at its pull after reset, observe `tb_xtrig_ctp_ct_dst` and `tb_xtrig_ctp_wire_mismatch` for a window and require no rises and no mismatch.
. Read CONFIG.MODE at reset and every CTP pad control; require the RDL reset value and the wire-OR column of the CTP table on every lane, and 0 on the five undriven controls.
. Write CONFIG.MODE=1 on one lane and require the point-to-point column on that lane, the wire-OR column elsewhere, and 0 on the undriven controls.
. Drive a point-to-point request in, require the acknowledge out and STATUS, release and require it retired.
. Point CT_SRC[1].CT_DST_SELECT at lane 0 and require the trigger on lane 1's request out; route lane 0 into the CT_SRC port of the first SMU-exposed internal lane and require `xtrig_ctm_src_req_o[0]`, then the same from a chiplet pull of lane 2's shared wire, held and released, requiring the receive latency, one pulse, and the routed request following the pull rather than the release.
. Put three wire-OR lanes on the group wire; require one chiplet pull reaches every member's `ct_dst` once at the same latency and leaves every non-member lane quiet, and that two members pulled in overlapping windows still reach every member exactly once and leave every non-member lane quiet, with the mismatch flag clear throughout both windows.
. For every lane in turn, with its successor as the sink, put both in point-to-point, require the pad controls of the point-to-point column on those two lanes alone, route the lane's destination to the sink, drive a request in, require the lane's acknowledge out and the sink's request out, retire it with the sink's acknowledge in, and return the lane to wire-OR.
. With every lane back at wire-OR, pull each lane's private wire in turn and require that lane's `ct_dst` once at CT_DST_LATENCY and no other lane's.
. With lane 0 in point-to-point, route its destination into the CT_SRC port of each DTP internal lane in turn, drive a request, and require that internal lane to rise once and no other, acknowledging it when its mode bit selects the handshake; then pulse each exposed lane's acknowledge input with no request in flight, sampling every cycle of the pulse and of the settle after it, and require no lane to move.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-DEFAULT` |CONFIG.MODE reads its RDL reset value (wire-OR) and every CTP pad control sits at the wire-OR level of the CTP signal interface table on all `CROSS_TRIGGER_NETWORK_CTP_NUM` lanes
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-TIEOFF` |the five pad-ring controls the CTP signal interface defines no driver for read 0 in both modes (DV rule; see the spec gap above)
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-P2P-MODE` |CONFIG.MODE=1 reads back, moves the written lane's pad controls to the point-to-point level of the CTP signal interface table and leaves every other lane at its wire-OR level
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-P2P-RX` |a point-to-point request in raises the acknowledge out and shows in STATUS, and releasing the request retires it
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-ROUTE` |with CT_SRC[1].CT_DST_SELECT pointing at lane 0, the trigger appears as lane 1's request out and the acknowledge in retires it
|`CHK-SMU-CTM-SRC` |routing lane 0's destination into the CTM port of the first SMU-exposed internal lane raises xtrig_ctm_src_req_o[0], and a chiplet pull of lane 2's shared wire routes the same way, the pulse following the pull rather than the release
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-WIRE-IDLE` |with every CT_Req_out wire resting at its pull after reset, no wire-OR lane's `ct_dst` rises and the wire-mismatch flag is 0
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-WIRE-RX` |a chiplet pull of lane 2's private wire (held >= PULSE_BOUND clk_smu) raises `tb_xtrig_ctp_ct_dst[2]` exactly CT_DST_LATENCY clocks after the wire is first seen asserted, once, and the release raises nothing further
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-WIRE-SHARED` |three wire-OR lanes on one group wire: one chiplet pull reaches every member's `ct_dst` once at the same latency and leaves every non-member lane quiet; two members pulled in overlapping windows still reach every member exactly once and leave every non-member lane quiet
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-EVERY-LANE` |every lane in point-to-point moves its own pad controls, acknowledges a request in, and routes it through the matrix to its successor's request out, which the successor's acknowledge in retires
|`CHK-SMU-CTP-WIRE-RX-EVERY-LANE` |a chiplet pull of each lane's private wire raises that lane's `ct_dst` once at CT_DST_LATENCY and no other lane's
|`CHK-SMU-CTM-EVERY-LANE` |routed into each of the ten DTP internal lanes, the two the SMC keeps and the eight the SMU exposes, a lane-0 request raises that internal lane once and no other, and an acknowledge on an idle exposed lane moves no request on any cycle of the pulse or the settle after it
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `dtp_under_smu`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-064-smu_xtrig_mode_composition_test]]
===== 64 `smu_xtrig_mode_composition_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_xtrig_mode_composition_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive of the external CTM lanes; `elaboration` firmware mode, `+xtrig_int_ct_mode=0x80`

*Objective*: The per-internal-CT mode vector reaches the DTP, and a handshake lane acknowledges where a pulse-sync lane does not.

*Test Coverage*:

* `doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc` ("Cross Trigger Parameters", "Debug & Test Ports (DTP) Integration") states the lane geometry: 8 SMU-exposed internal CT lanes, 10 at the DTP, the low 2 reserved for the SMC with their mode bits at zero ("SMC-reserved DTP mode bits are 0 (pulse-sync)"). Those counts are the golden of the S1 mask compare and the set of lanes the live leg walks.
* Reading the DTP's mode vector back against the `+xtrig_int_ct_mode` the bench elaborated the build with, and the DTP mode vector width, are plumbing compares and carry no evidence token. `CFG` is not decoded: its struct layout is unspecified.
* What the tokens rest on is the live leg: `port_table.adoc` states that the CTM ack ports are "Unused in pulse-sync mode (mode bit = 0)", and each of the 8 external CTM lanes is requested in turn so that a lane whose mode bit is set acknowledges and holds while a pulse-sync lane does not; the observed acknowledging-lane count must equal the mode popcount.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Read the mode vector at the DTP and require the 2 SMC-reserved bits zero.
. Request each of the 8 external CTM lanes in turn and record which acknowledge; require the pulse-sync lanes silent and the handshake lanes to acknowledge and hold.
. Compare the acknowledging-lane count against the mode popcount.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-XTRIG-MODE-S1` |the 2 SMC-reserved mode bits into DTP are zero and a pulse-sync lane never acknowledges
|`CHK-SMU-XTRIG-MODE-S2` |a handshake lane acknowledges and holds; the observed ack-lane count is the mode popcount
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-151-smu_jtag_ic_reset_ext_override_test]]
===== 151 `smu_jtag_ic_reset_ext_override_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_jtag_ic_reset_ext_override_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the scenario runs in the test module itself and there is no separate sequence)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` driving the DTP primary TAP (IC_RESET instruction, 155-bit data register, Test-Logic-Reset by TMS high, `TRST_n` pulse, `jtag_trst` read back released); DUT outputs `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ovrd` and `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ctrl_n` sampled 16 `clk_smu_i` cycles after each Update-DR, on 64 consecutive `clk_smu_i` edges from the Update-DR edge in the combined-change leg, and on every `jtag_tck` edge in the no-update leg; `jtag_ptap_state`, `jtag_tck`, `jtag_tdo` and `jtag_tdo_oen` observed; `powergood_i` dropped and restored for the power-on reset leg, with `obs_powergood_stable_o`, `rst_cold_n_o` and `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` waited high afterwards; `clk_ref_i` and `clk_smu_i`; the default external reset slice of one port; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The external IC_RESET output follows the written reset-enable and reset-control bits after Update-DR only, and `reset_hold` decides whether Test-Logic-Reset restores them while `TRST_n` and power-on reset always do.

*Test Coverage*:

* `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc` ("IC_RESET Support") and `doc/integrator/src/smu-composition.adoc` ("IC_RESET TDR Structure") are the source of the register shape: the external slice is port 0, with `reset_hold` at bit 0, `reset_enable` (active low) at bit 1 and `reset_control` at bit 2, every bit resetting to 1, the override output being the inverse of `reset_enable` and the value output being `reset_control`.
* With `reset_enable` 0 the override output must read 1 and the control output must follow `reset_control` for both values, and writing `reset_enable` 1 must clear the override; with `reset_enable` 1 the override output must read 0 and the control output must still follow `reset_control`.
* A single 0 and a single 1 walked over the external port's two bits must give the expected outputs while every other bit of the data register reads back as written; the default external slice has one port, so independence from other external ports is not exercised.
* Writing `reset_control` with `reset_enable` 1 must move only the control output, and a second scan writing `reset_enable` 0 must then move only the override output (`hw/sys/dtp/doc/clk_rst.adoc`, "IC_RESET Overrides"; `doc/programmer/src/dtp-programming.adoc`).
* Changing `reset_enable` and `reset_control` in one Update-DR must settle to the written values within a 64-cycle `clk_smu_i` window; the number of output transitions inside that window is measured and logged, and the transient pulse is not compared.
* Shifting a different image through Capture-DR, Shift-DR and Pause-DR, then shifting the held image back and updating it, must leave both outputs where they were on every `jtag_tck` edge.
* With `reset_hold` 1, Test-Logic-Reset must restore every bit to 1 and clear the override; with `reset_hold` 0 it must keep the enable and control bits and the override (`architecture.adoc`, IC_RESET fields table); `TRST_n` and the power-on reset must restore every bit including `reset_hold`, and Test-Logic-Reset must leave `reset_hold` itself unchanged, which is an inference: the document states only that it does not reset the enable and control bits when `reset_hold` is 0 and says nothing about `reset_hold` itself.
* The override output sampled when the TAP reads Test-Logic-Reset with `powergood_i` low must read 0; the settling of the combined output is not compared.
* How the upstream reset passes through the port when the override is off is not observable at the SMU boundary beyond the override reading 0.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, reach Run-Test/Idle, read IDCODE, and require the baseline output pair (override 0, control level 1).
. Write the image with `reset_enable` 0 and `reset_control` 0, then 1, then clear it with `reset_enable` 1, sampling after each Update-DR.
. Write `reset_enable` 1 with `reset_control` 0, then 1, sampling after each.
. Walk a single 0 and a single 1 over the two external-port bits, reading the data register back after each write.
. Write `reset_control` first and `reset_enable` second in separate scans, sampling after each Update-DR.
. Change `reset_enable` and `reset_control` together in one Update-DR in both directions and sample 64 `clk_smu_i` cycles from the Update-DR edge.
. Shift a different image through Capture-DR, Shift-DR and Pause-DR, shift the held image back and update it, and count output moves on every `jtag_tck` edge.
. With `reset_hold` 1 set the override, apply Test-Logic-Reset by TMS and read the data register and the outputs.
. With `reset_hold` 0 set the override, apply Test-Logic-Reset by TMS and read the data register and the outputs.
. Pulse `TRST_n`, wait for Test-Logic-Reset, and read the data register and the outputs.
. With `reset_hold` 0 and the override set, drop `powergood_i`, wait for Test-Logic-Reset, restore it, wait for the power-on resets to release, then read the data register, the outputs and IDCODE.
. With `reset_hold` 0 and then 1 apply Test-Logic-Reset and read `reset_hold` back.
. Read the list of bounded waits and require that none expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ICEXT-EXT-ON` |With `reset_enable` 0 the override output reads 1 and the control output follows `reset_control`, and `reset_enable` 1 clears the override.
|`CHK-ICEXT-EXT-OFF` |With `reset_enable` 1 the override output reads 0 and the control output follows `reset_control`.
|`CHK-ICEXT-EXT-INDEP` |A single 0 and a single 1 on the one external port give the expected outputs and the rest of the data register reads back unchanged.
|`CHK-ICEXT-EXT-2STEP` |Writing `reset_control` moves the control output first and writing `reset_enable` then moves the override output.
|`CHK-ICEXT-EXT-BOTH` |`reset_enable` and `reset_control` changed in one Update-DR settle to the written values.
|`CHK-ICEXT-EXT-UPDONLY` |Both outputs hold on every `jtag_tck` edge while a different image is shifted through Capture-DR, Shift-DR and Pause-DR and the held image is shifted back and updated.
|`CHK-ICEXT-HOLD-1` |With `reset_hold` 1, Test-Logic-Reset restores all bits to 1 and clears the override.
|`CHK-ICEXT-HOLD-0` |With `reset_hold` 0, Test-Logic-Reset keeps the enable, the control and the override.
|`CHK-ICEXT-HOLD-TRST` |`TRST_n` restores every bit including `reset_hold`.
|`CHK-ICEXT-HOLD-POR` |Power-on reset restores every bit including `reset_hold`.
|`CHK-ICEXT-HOLD-SELF` |Test-Logic-Reset leaves `reset_hold` unchanged at 0 and at 1, an expectation inferred from the `reset_hold` description in `architecture.adoc`.
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |Every bounded wait completes within its bound and records its last state; an expired wait raises in the wait helper.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-152-smu_jtag_ic_reset_layout_caps_test]]
===== 152 `smu_jtag_ic_reset_layout_caps_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_jtag_ic_reset_layout_caps_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the scenario runs in the test module itself and there is no separate sequence)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` driving the DTP primary TAP (IC_RESET and JTAG_CAPS instructions, `TRST_n` pulse, Test-Logic-Reset by TMS); hierarchical read-only probes of the SMC and SEP IC_RESET slice buses (`jtag_smc_reset_ctrl`, `jtag_sep_reset_ctrl`) and of the SMC reset-control value leaves `ss_warm_reset_n_val`, `ss_cold_reset_n_val`, `cold_reset_n_val`, `cool_reset_n_val`, `warm_reset_n_val` and `fuse_reset_n_val`; DUT outputs `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ovrd` and `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ctrl_n` sampled 16 `clk_smu_i` cycles after each Update-DR and on every `jtag_tck` edge during the recapture leg; `jtag_ptap_state`, `jtag_tck`, `jtag_tdo` and `jtag_tdo_oen`; `powergood_i` dropped and restored for the power-on reset leg, with `obs_powergood_stable_o`, `rst_cold_n_o` and `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` waited high afterwards; `clk_ref_i` and `clk_smu_i`; the default configuration (SEP present, one external port); the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: The IC_RESET data register has the documented length, slice order and port-pair order, a cleared control bit lands at its slice position, the register resets to all ones and recaptures unchanged, and JTAG_CAPS reads the default configuration and ignores shifted-in bits.

*Test Coverage*:

* `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc` ("IC_RESET Support", "JTAG Capabilities") and `doc/integrator/src/smu-composition.adoc` ("IC_RESET TDR Structure", "JTAG Configuration Parameters", "Cross Trigger Parameters") are the source of the length 2 * (68 + 8 + 1) + 1 = 155 bits, the slice order from TDI (SMC, SEP, external, then `reset_hold` nearest TDO), the pair order (`reset_enable` at bit 2n+1, `reset_control` at bit 2n+2) and the JTAG_CAPS field map.
* A 163-bit marker shifted through the register must emerge after exactly 155 TCKs and the default image must be restored afterwards.
* One cleared control bit for each of `ss_warm_reset_n[31]` (first SMC port), `fuse_reset_n` (last SMC port), `abr_jtag_rst_n` (a SEP port), `km_jtag_rst_n` (last SEP port), the external port and `reset_hold` must land in its own slice, observed on the slice buses and the external outputs, with every other bit unchanged.
* Writing external port 0 with `reset_control` 0 must drive the control output low with the override still 0, and `reset_enable` is bit 1.
* The ten named ports (`ss_warm_reset_n[31]`, `ss_warm_reset_n[0]`, `ss_cold_reset_n[31]`, `ss_cold_reset_n[0]`, `cold_reset_n`, `cool_reset_n`, `warm_reset_n`, `fuse_reset_n`, `abr_jtag_rst_n`, `km_jtag_rst_n`) are each cleared alone and read back at their TDR position; the numeric position of each port within a slice follows the declaration order of the two documents, which do not state the numeric mapping, so the leaf compares against the numbering it derives from that order.
* After power-on reset and after `TRST_n`, starting from an image that only a reset can restore, all 155 bits must read 1.
* Reading the register, writing the captured image back and reading again must return the same image on both reads and move no external output; whether Capture-DR loads the live register value is not stated by the documents and the compare relies on the returned image only.
* JTAG_CAPS must read the default configuration (16 cross-trigger ports, 1 extra STAP, the STAP I/O, SEP debug and SMC debug enables set, 68 SMC, 8 SEP and 1 external IC_RESET ports, every instruction enable set, major version 0); bits 59:54, `num_xtrig_int_ct` (the SMU-exposed 8 or 8 plus the two lanes reserved for the SMC), are accepted as 8 or 10, logged as measured and left out of the exact compare.
* JTAG_CAPS must ignore all-ones, all-zeros and 61-bit marker shift-ins and be exactly 60 bits long.
* Every JTAG_CAPS field value is logged as measured.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, reach Run-Test/Idle, read IDCODE, read the IC_RESET register and require the default image, the slice buses at all ones and the outputs at (override 0, control level 1).
. Shift a 163-bit marker through IC_RESET, find where the first 0 emerges and require 155 TCKs, then read back the default image.
. Clear one control bit at a time for those six positions, reading the register, the slice buses and the outputs after each write.
. Write external port 0 with `reset_control` 0, read back, restore, and compare the outputs and slice buses.
. Clear each named port's control bit alone, read back the register, the slice buses and the SMC value leaf where one exists, then restore.
. Write an image with `reset_hold` and one control bit cleared, drop `powergood_i` and restore it, read the register; repeat with the `TRST_n` pulse in place of the power-on reset.
. Read the register, write the captured image back, read again, and count output moves on every `jtag_tck` edge.
. Load JTAG_CAPS, read the 60-bit register, log every field and require the default configuration with bits 59:54 accepted as 8 or 10.
. Shift all ones, all zeros and two 61-bit markers into JTAG_CAPS and require the same 60 bits back each time with the marker bit beyond the 60th as shifted.
. Read the register a final time and require the default image, then require that no bounded wait expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-ICLAY-IC-LEN` |A 163-bit marker emerges from IC_RESET after exactly 155 TCKs.
|`CHK-ICLAY-IC-SLICE` |A cleared control bit lands in its own slice at each slice boundary position and at `reset_hold`.
|`CHK-ICLAY-IC-PAIR` |`reset_control` is bit 2n+2 and `reset_enable` bit 2n+1 of the external port pair.
|`CHK-ICLAY-IC-NAMED` |The ten named ports sit at their TDR positions and no other bit moves.
|`CHK-ICLAY-IC-IMG` |The register reads all ones after power-on reset and after `TRST_n`.
|`CHK-ICLAY-IC-RECAP` |A recapture returns the written image and no output moves across the rewriting Update-DR.
|`CHK-ICLAY-CAPS-DEF` |JTAG_CAPS equals the default-configuration field map outside bits 59:54, and those bits read 8 or 10.
|`CHK-ICLAY-CAPS-RO` |JTAG_CAPS ignores shifted-in bits and is exactly 60 bits long.
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |Every bounded wait completes within its bound and records its last state; an expired wait raises in the wait helper.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-153-smu_jtag_trst_por_reset_test]]
===== 153 `smu_jtag_trst_por_reset_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_jtag_trst_por_reset_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the scenario runs in the test module itself and there is no separate sequence)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` driving the DTP primary TAP (`TRST_n` assert and release with TCK running at TMS 0, TMS 1 and stopped, IR and DR scans); `powergood_i` dropped and restored with `obs_powergood_stable_o`, `rst_cold_n_o` and `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o` waited high afterwards; observation of `jtag_trst`, `jtag_ptap_state` (on `clk_ref_i` while a reset is asserted, and after TCK edges), `tb_ptap_inst_decoded`, `jtag_tck`, `jtag_tdo` and `jtag_tdo_oen`; `clk_ref_i`; the run mode `sep_rtl` plusargs `+rom_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/smc_rom_default.hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/smc_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_efuse_default.hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_boot_rom_default.hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_itcm_default.hex`, `+sep_dtcm_hex=hw/sys/smu/dv/build/firmware/preloads/sep_dtcm_default.hex`, `+skip_fuse_sense`, `+smc_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smc/dv/assets/default_efuse_shadow_reg.preload` and `+sep_shadow_reg_preload=hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/default_sep_efuse_shadow_reg.preload`; `elaboration` firmware mode

*Objective*: `TRST_n` and the power-on reset each hold the primary TAP in Test-Logic-Reset without TCK, release it to Run-Test/Idle on the first TMS-low TCK, and Test-Logic-Reset loads IDCODE.

*Test Coverage*:

* `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc` ("Reset Hierarchy", "TAP Controller State Machine", "ID Code"), `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/interface.adoc` (instruction encodings) and `hw/sys/dtp/doc/clk_rst.adoc` ("Reset Architecture") are the source of the expected values: Test-Logic-Reset reads 16'h0001, Run-Test/Idle 16'h0002, the combined reset is the power-on reset ANDed with `TRST_n`, and Test-Logic-Reset loads IDCODE into the instruction register.
* The IDCODE data register value 32'h0000_0001 follows from the marker bit of the IDCODE field table in `architecture.adoc` with the manufacturer, part number and revision defaults of `doc/integrator/src/smu-composition.adoc` all 0; the decoded instruction is the one-hot value for opcode 0x01.
* A power-on reset alone, from a state that is not Test-Logic-Reset and with no TCK, must bring `jtag_ptap_state` to 16'h0001 within 2000 `clk_ref_i` cycles, decode IDCODE, and return IDCODE on a DR scan.
* `TRST_n` alone must bring the state to 16'h0001 within the same bound with TCK running at TMS 0, with TCK running at TMS 1 and with TCK stopped.
* With both resets asserted, with `TRST_n` released first and with the power-on reset released first, the state must hold 16'h0001 across TMS-low TCKs while either reset is asserted, and the first TMS-low TCK after both are released must give 16'h0002.
* After release, eight TMS-high TCKs must keep 16'h0001 with IDCODE decoded after a DEBUG_CONTROL instruction was loaded, the first TMS-low TCK must give 16'h0002, and a DR scan before any IR scan must return IDCODE.
* For each of BYPASS, DEBUG_CONTROL, JTAG_CAPS, IC_RESET, TAP_3DCR, SELECT_IJTAG and TMP_STATUS loaded first, Test-Logic-Reset reached by TMS must decode IDCODE and a DR scan must return IDCODE.
* The reset values of `jtag_tdo` and `jtag_tdo_oen` are not documented; the leaf logs the pair before, during and after each reset and compares only that the pair after `TRST_n`, after power-on reset and after both are equal to each other.
* How `powergood_stable` is derived from `powergood_i` is not documented; the leaf drives `powergood_i` and observes the TAP only.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, reach Run-Test/Idle and read IDCODE.
. Park in Shift-DR under BYPASS, drop `powergood_i` with `TRST_n` released and no TCK, wait for Test-Logic-Reset, restore `powergood_i`, then read the decoded instruction and run a DR scan.
. Park in Shift-DR and assert `TRST_n` with TCK at TMS 0, with TCK at TMS 1 and with TCK stopped, waiting for Test-Logic-Reset each time.
. Assert `TRST_n` and drop `powergood_i` together, release `TRST_n` first, then assert both again and release `powergood_i` first, sampling the state and applying a TMS-low TCK while one reset is still asserted.
. Load DEBUG_CONTROL, assert `TRST_n`, release it, hold TMS high for 8 TCKs, apply one TMS-low TCK and run a DR scan before any IR scan.
. For each prior instruction load it, apply five TMS-high TCKs, sample the decoded instruction and run a DR scan.
. After `TRST_n`, after power-on reset and after both, sample `jtag_tdo` and `jtag_tdo_oen` while the reset is held and after the release.
. Read the list of bounded waits and require that none expired.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-RSTH-POR-ONLY` |A power-on reset alone holds the TAP in Test-Logic-Reset, IDCODE is decoded and a DR scan returns IDCODE.
|`CHK-RSTH-TRST-ONLY` |`TRST_n` low gives Test-Logic-Reset with TCK running at TMS 0, at TMS 1 and with TCK stopped.
|`CHK-RSTH-RST-COMB` |Either reset alone holds Test-Logic-Reset across TMS-low TCKs, and the first TMS-low TCK after both are released gives Run-Test/Idle.
|`CHK-RSTH-RST-RELEASE` |Test-Logic-Reset holds under TMS high after release, the first TMS-low TCK gives Run-Test/Idle and a DR scan returns IDCODE.
|`CHK-RSTH-TLR-IDCODE` |Test-Logic-Reset loads IDCODE for each of seven prior instructions.
|`CHK-RSTH-TDO-RST` |The (`jtag_tdo`, `jtag_tdo_oen`) pair read after `TRST_n` is the same pair read after power-on reset and after both.
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |Every bounded wait completes within its bound and records its last state; an expired wait raises in the wait helper.
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
==== SEP

[[sec-066-smu_sep_smoke_test]]
===== 66 `smu_sep_smoke_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sep_smoke_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive only (no external VIP); `sep_smoke` firmware mode -- the SMC runs the `smu_sep_smoke` arm image from ROM and the real SEP RTL boots under it; verdict through `SmuSepBootScoreboard` on the SEP trace probes

*Objective*: The real SEP RTL comes out of reset under the SMU wrapper, fetches from boot ROM, executes from ICCM and stores to DCCM, in the same run in which the SMC arm firmware boots from ROM and signals `TEST_PASS`. The SEP runs on its default run gate; the arm image (`fw/tests/smu_sep_arm/main.c`) writes its marker and does not release the SEP, so no arm-before-boot ordering is claimed.

*Test Coverage*:

* Profile pre-conditions are fail-capable: during cold reset `sep_reset_n_o` must read 0 and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` must read 0 (the real-SEP profile asserts SEP reset and starts with fuse-done inactive); a `no_sep` build fails both.
* Boot readiness is the conjunction the scoreboard's `boot_ready()` requires: SEP reset observed both asserted and released; fuse-done observed low then high *unless* `sep_fuse_sense_skipped_o` is 1, in which case the sense is replaced by the shadow-register preload and the fuse terms are logged as informational rather than counted; SMC arm firmware signalled `TEST_PASS`; boot-ROM fetch seen; ICCM execution seen; at least 256 retired instructions over at least 16 distinct PCs; and at least one DCCM write.
* The fuse-sense carve-out reads the DUT node that *selects* the substitute (`sim_skip_fuse_sense` inside the SEP eFuse shadow registers), not a plusarg scan, so a build without `SIMULATION` keeps the fuse terms required.
* After the SMC arm is seen, 100 000 cycles with `sep_reset_n_o` high and zero retired instructions is a failure with the run-gate state logged, so a released SEP that never fetches is diagnosed rather than timed out.
* Firmware console bytes (`fw_char_o`) are collected and printed; they are not a pass condition.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. In `smu_base_test`, sample `sep_reset_n_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` during cold reset and assert both are 0 for this profile.
. Release reset and, every `clk_smu_i` edge, feed the scoreboard the SEP reset and fuse-done levels, `smc_test_pass_o`, the boot-ROM / ICCM fetch-seen flags, the DCCM write count and any console byte; count retired instructions and distinct PCs from the SEP trace monitor.
. Return as soon as `boot_ready()` holds; fail if 100 000 post-arm cycles pass with no retire, or if the overall cycle budget (`SMU_SEP_BOOT_MAX_CYCLES`, default 3 000 000) expires.
. In `check_phase`, re-assert every readiness term individually so the log names which one was missing.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_profile_precondition` |`sep_reset_n_o == 0` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o == 0` during cold reset
|`sep_reset_low_high` |SEP reset observed asserted and then released
|`sep_fuse_sense` |fuse-done observed low then high, or `sep_fuse_sense_skipped_o == 1` with the terms demoted to informational
|`smc_arm_pass` |SMC arm firmware wrote `TEST_PASS` (`smc_test_pass_o`)
|`sep_boot_rom_iccm` |boot-ROM fetch seen and ICCM execution seen
|`sep_retire_floor` |>= 256 retired instructions over >= 16 distinct PCs
|`sep_dccm_write` |`sep_dccm_write_count_o` advanced above 0
|`post_arm_idle` |a SEP out of reset retired something within 100 000 cycles of the SMC arm marker being seen
|`CHK-NONVAC` |emitted by the scoreboard only after every term above held
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every readiness term holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Evidence tokens: `SEP_BOOT_ROM_OK`, `SEP_ICCM_OK`, `SEP_DCCM_WRITE_OK`. Enrolled in: `all`, `fw_boot`.

'''''

[[sec-067-smu_sep_fuse_sense_done_test]]
===== 67 `smu_sep_fuse_sense_done_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sep_fuse_sense_done_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: SEP eFuse bank model inside the wrapper composition (declared in *Bench stand-ins and exceptions*); `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl_fuse_sense` run mode, which leaves `+skip_fuse_sense` and the SEP shadow preload unset

*Objective*: SEP fuse sense completes on the composition and reports on `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` after the cold reset released and after eFuse read traffic appeared.

*Test Coverage*:

* Measured against the `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` row of `port_table.adoc`; the ordering the checks require is a property of the elaborated design, not of that row.
* `sep_fuse_sense_skipped_o` -- the bench's view of the SEP shadow registers' own skip flag -- must read 0, so the rising edge timed here is a completed sense and not the plusarg path's forced value.
* eFuse traffic is recognised on the SMU eFuse shim command port by the `valid` bit of `fuse_command_req_t` / `fuse_command_resp_t`, laid out as `hw/ip/efuse/doc/interface.adoc` ("Fuse Command Protocol") gives them; only the ordering is asserted, the sense duration is logged.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Arm the edge and eFuse-command observers ahead of bring-up.
. Require `sep_fuse_sense_skipped_o` low and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` low under cold reset.
. After release, require SEP eFuse read traffic on the shim command port and then exactly one rising edge of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, after both.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-FUSE-SENSE-S2` |sep_fuse_sense_done_o rises once, after reset release and after SEP eFuse traffic
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-068-smu_sep_secure_tm_test]]
===== 68 `smu_sep_secure_tm_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sep_secure_tm_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive of `secure_tm_req_i` across three cold resets; `elaboration` firmware mode, `+expected_sep=1`

*Objective*: `secure_tm_req_i` is latched into `secure_tm_o` once per cold reset from the value it holds at the SEP fuse-sense-done edge, not the value it held when the reset asserted, and the strap is ignored between sampling windows.

*Test Coverage*:

* The sampling instant is the specification's: TEST_EN "is latched when SEP fuse sensing is done if SEC_DIS is not asserted" and "on chiplet cold reset release if SEC_DIS is asserted", and the latched value "can be cleared only by chip reset" (`hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc`, Test Mode Entry; `hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc`, `secure_tm` and the Secure Scan Chain; `doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc`, the `secure_tm_req_i` bullet). The strap and done ports are the `secure_tm_req_i` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rows of `port_table.adoc`.
* In each of three cold resets the strap holds one value as the reset asserts and is changed to the value it is to hold at the edge only after the SEP reset (`obs_sep_rst_n_o`) and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` are both read low; `secure_tm_o` must read low under the reset, stay low across every sample from that change to the edge, and carry the changed value after it. A latch taken when the reset asserts, or a wire, fails these compares. The second reset enters with `secure_tm_o` at 1 from the first, so its low read under reset is an observed 1 -> 0 clear.
* After the edge the strap is changed again and the output must not follow it, in both directions, across a stated number of samples.
* Gap: on the `+skip_fuse_sense` path this leaf runs on (`SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`, "`+skip_fuse_sense` with the shadow preload"), `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises on the first clock after the SEP reset releases, so the fuse-sense-done edge and cold-reset release are one clock apart and the leaf does not tell the specification's two windows apart. It brackets the sampling instant to after the last strap change under reset and no later than the observed edge. SEC_DIS is not observable at the SMU boundary. Telling the two windows apart needs a run with the real SEP fuse sense, as <<sec-067-smu_sep_fuse_sense_done_test>> runs it, where the edge follows the release by the sense duration.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Drive the strap to one value and assert a cold reset; wait for the SEP reset and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to read low, require `secure_tm_o` low, then change the strap to the value it is to hold at the edge and keep the reset held.
. Release the reset; require `secure_tm_o` low on every sample until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises and equal to the changed value after it.
. Change the strap with no reset and require `secure_tm_o` unchanged.
. Repeat with the values swapped (1 as the reset asserts, 0 at the edge; the 1 left by the first reset must clear), then once more with the strap held at 1 throughout.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SMU-SECURE-TM` |across three cold resets secure_tm_o clears under the reset, stays low from a strap change made under reset until the SEP fuse-sense-done edge, carries the changed value after the edge rather than the value present when the reset asserted, and ignores the strap between those windows in both directions
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''
[[sec-154-smu_secure_tm_real_sense_window_test]]
===== 154 `smu_secure_tm_real_sense_window_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_secure_tm_real_sense_window_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_secure_tm_real_sense_window_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: bench strap `tb_secure_tm_req` (the leaf's idle-input override drives it to 1 before the first cold reset) and bench observation `tb_secure_tm` of `secure_tm_o` (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`); `rst_cold_ni`, `jtag_trst`, `jtag_tck`, `obs_sep_rst_n_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `clk_smu_i`, `clk_ref_i`; the fuse-sense probe (`seq_lib/smu_fuse_sense_probe.py`), which records the rising edges of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` and counts the `valid` strobes (bit 0) of `sep_efuse_shim_command_resp` under `u_dut`; SEP eFuse bank model inside the wrapper composition (declared in *Bench stand-ins and exceptions*); `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl_fuse_sense` run mode, which leaves `+skip_fuse_sense` unset (the leaf fails if it is set); `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images

*Objective*: `secure_tm_o` latches the strap at the SEP fuse-sense completion, clears under a second cold reset and stays 0 across the whole real sense that follows, whatever the strap does before the completion.

*Test Coverage*:

* The sampling rules are the specification's: the sampled value clears when cold reset asserts, is sampled when fuse sensing completes, and is retained when neither sampling event occurs (`hw/sys/sep/doc/test_mode.adoc`, Secure test-mode sampling table; `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc`, the `secure_tm_req_i` bullet). The strap and done ports are the `secure_tm_req_i` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rows of `hw/sys/smu/doc/port_table.adoc`; `secure_tm_o` has no row there and the leaf reads it on the bench observation `tb_secure_tm`.
* Leg 1: with the strap held at 1 from before the first cold reset, `secure_tm_o` reads 1 on the sample taken 64 `clk_smu_i` cycles after the first completion rise of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
* Leg 2 under reset: with the strap still at 1, a second cold reset is asserted; once `obs_sep_rst_n_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` both read low and the reset has been held for 64 `clk_ref_i` cycles, `secure_tm_o` reads 0 on every `clk_smu_i` sample of the hold, which is an observed 1 -> 0 clear, and the strap is then dropped to 0.
* Leg 2 after release: from the release until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises again, `secure_tm_o` reads 0 on every `clk_smu_i` sample, and the window holds at least 1000 samples, a floor the leaf picks so that the window is a real sense and not the skipped one.
* Strap pulse: inside that window the leaf raises the strap to 1 at the 100th response strobe it counts and returns it to 0 at the 180th; `secure_tm_o` reads 0 on every sample taken while the strap is high, because a strap change is not a sampling event. After the completion rise, with the strap at 0, `secure_tm_o` reads 0 on 512 further samples.
* The number of response strobes seen at the completion rise and the final strap and `secure_tm_o` values are logged and not compared.
* SEC_DIS is not observable at the SMU boundary, and the strap is 0 at the release and at the completion rise of leg 2, so this leaf does not tell the early capture point after release from the fuse-sense completion; the sampling of a 1 at the completion is proven by leg 1 only.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Require `+skip_fuse_sense` unset, wait for the bring-up reset to finish and require the strap `tb_secure_tm_req` at 1.
. Wait for the first rise of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, wait 64 `clk_smu_i` cycles and require `secure_tm_o` at 1.
. Assert `rst_cold_ni` and `jtag_trst`, wait for `obs_sep_rst_n_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` to read low, hold 64 `clk_ref_i` cycles while sampling `secure_tm_o` on every `clk_smu_i`, and require every sample 0.
. Drop the strap to 0, release `rst_cold_ni` and `jtag_trst`, run the 16-pulse TAP reset walk, and sample `secure_tm_o` on every `clk_smu_i` until `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` rises, requiring at least 1000 samples, all 0.
. In that window raise the strap at response strobe 100 and drop it at response strobe 180, and require every sample while the strap is high to read 0.
. After the completion rise take 512 samples with the strap at 0 and require every one to read 0.
. Check that every bounded wait recorded a finite bound and a last observed state and none expired, and that the step stamps increase in simulation time up to the pass stamp.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SEP-SECURE-TM-CLEAR-REAL` |`secure_tm_o` reads 1 after the first sense, reads 0 on every sample of the second cold-reset hold, and reads 0 on every sample from the release to the completion rise over at least 1000 samples
|`CHK-SEP-SECURE-TM-NO-EARLY-FOLLOW` |`secure_tm_o` reads 0 on every sample while the strap is at 1 between response strobes 100 and 180 and on every sample after the completion rise with the strap at 0
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |every bounded wait of the leaf, on the completion rises, the reset lows, the strobe counts and the TAP reset walk, records a finite bound and a last observed state and none expires
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the step stamps of the leaf are strictly increasing in simulation time from the first step to the pass stamp
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
[[sec-155-smu_sep_cold_reassert_during_sense_test]]
===== 155 `smu_sep_cold_reassert_during_sense_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sep_cold_reassert_during_sense_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_sep_cold_reassert_during_sense_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `rst_cold_ni`, `jtag_trst`, `jtag_tck`, `rst_cold_n_o`, `sep_reset_n_o`, `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, `clk_smu_i`, `clk_ref_i`; the fuse-sense probe (`seq_lib/smu_fuse_sense_probe.py`), which records the rising edges of `rst_cold_n_o`, `sep_reset_n_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, and counts the request rising edges and the response `valid` strobes (bit 0) of `sep_efuse_shim_command_req` and `sep_efuse_shim_command_resp` under `u_dut`; SEP eFuse bank model inside the wrapper composition (declared in *Bench stand-ins and exceptions*); `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl_fuse_sense` run mode, which leaves `+skip_fuse_sense` unset (the leaf fails if it is set); `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images

*Objective*: re-asserting the cold reset after the SEP fuse sense and at chosen points inside a later sense resets the SEP, clears `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, aborts the partial sense and lets a full sense restart.

*Test Coverage*:

* The SEP reset is the boot-qualified reset that "holds every IP in reset until eFuse sensing completes" (`hw/sys/sep/doc/reset_controller.adoc`, sequenced resets paragraph; `hw/sys/sep/doc/clk_rst.adoc`, reset table), and the fuse controller senses the bank "at cold reset" (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, introduction). The sense issues one read for 256 shadow words, returns one response `valid` strobe per word, and `fuse_sense_done_o` rises after the strobe that arrives while the word counter is 255 (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, sensing run paragraph). The done flag resets to 0 (`hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl/sep_cpu_ctrl.rdl`, `sep_fuse_sense_done`).
* Leg 1: with `sep_reset_n_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` both reading 1 just before, `rst_cold_ni` is driven low; from the fall of `sep_reset_n_o` to the end of a 64-cycle `clk_ref_i` hold, `sep_reset_n_o` reads 0 on every `clk_smu_i` sample, and from the fall of `rst_cold_n_o` over the same hold `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 0 on every sample.
* Leg 1 restart: after the release, a second sense completes with exactly one rising edge of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` since the release and at least 256 response strobes counted before that rise.
* Abort legs: the leaf releases the reset, counts response strobes, and re-asserts `rst_cold_ni` when the count reaches 1 (word 0 has arrived) and 128 (words 0 to 127 have arrived; the leaf labels this leg word 128); in each leg the strobe count at the trigger equals the target, `sep_reset_n_o` reads 0 and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 0 on every sample of the hold, and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` has no rising edge between the release and the end of the hold.
* Near word 255: the leaf takes the trigger as 254 minus the largest number of strobes that elapsed between a trigger and the fall of `rst_cold_n_o` in the two earlier abort legs, and compares as in the other abort legs only when the fall lands before the 256th strobe; otherwise it logs that the window was missed and the leg is not claimed. The latency from the pin to each fall is logged and not compared.
* Final leg: after the last release the sense restarts with a new request rising edge before the first sample of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` at 1, delivers at least 256 response strobes counted from 0 before that sample, and produces exactly one rising edge.
* No document states how many strobes may precede an abort or what the SEP reset latency is, so the trigger strobe counts 1 and 128 and the computed near-255 count are values the leaf programs.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Require `+skip_fuse_sense` unset, wait for the bring-up reset to finish and for the first rise of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`.
. Wait for `sep_reset_n_o` to read 1 and require `sep_reset_n_o` and `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` both 1, then drive `rst_cold_ni` and `jtag_trst` low, wait for `rst_cold_n_o` and `sep_reset_n_o` to fall, hold 64 `clk_ref_i` cycles and compare the samples.
. Release, run the 16-pulse TAP reset walk, wait for the second rise of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o`, and compare its rise count and strobe count.
. Re-assert the reset, hold, release without the TAP reset walk, wait for response strobe 1, re-assert at once, hold and compare.
. Release, wait for response strobe 128, re-assert at once, hold and compare.
. Release, wait for the computed near-255 strobe count, re-assert at once, hold, and compare when the fall landed before the 256th strobe.
. Release and wait for the first sample of `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` at 1; compare the strobe count, the rise count and the new request edge.
. Check that every bounded wait recorded a finite bound and a last observed state and none expired, and that the step stamps increase in simulation time up to the pass stamp.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-SEP-RESET-REASSERT` |`sep_reset_n_o` reads 0 on every sample while the cold reset is re-asserted after the done flag is set and after response words 0 and 128, and near 255 when that fall lands before the 256th strobe
|`CHK-SEP-DONE-COLD-CLEAR` |`sep_fuse_sense_done_o` reads 0 under the re-asserted cold reset and the second sense produces exactly one rise after at least 256 response strobes
|`CHK-SEP-SENSE-ABORT-RESTART` |an aborted sense leaves `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` at 0 with no rise, and the final sense restarts with a new request and delivers at least 256 strobes before its single rise
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |every bounded wait of the leaf, on the sense completions, the strobe counts, the cold-reset edges and the TAP reset walk, records a finite bound and a last observed state and none expires
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the step stamps of the leaf are strictly increasing in simulation time from the first step to the pass stamp
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''
==== SMC

[[sec-069-smu_smc_mailbox_int_test]]

* Mailbox 0 and mailbox 31 raise their outbound write-threshold IRQ over the JTAG2AXI frontdoor, and the boundary vector must equal `baseline | 1<<idx` and return to baseline when the enable is cleared.

[[sec-072-smu_smc_smoke_test]]
===== 72 `smu_smc_smoke_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_smc_smoke_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`)

*BFM / Model*: TB pin drive only (no external VIP); `smc_smoke` firmware mode -- the SMC runs the `smu_smc_smoke` image from ROM under the SEP=1 wrapper build (`compile_smu_chiplet`, the wrapper's one profile); verdict through `SmuSmcBootScoreboard`

*Objective*: SMC firmware boots from ROM and reaches its architectural PASS under the production SMU wrapper, with instruction-memory and scratch activity observed.

*Test Coverage*:

* The profile pre-condition is fail-capable: during cold reset `sep_reset_n_o` must read 0, because the SEP=1 wrapper holds the real SEP reset tree in reset (the same check the elaboration leaf makes); a composition that tied the SEP reset off fails it. `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` after release is logged, not asserted: it is a SEP-side observation this SMC boot claim does not own.
* Firmware boot is one reset-to-completion transaction; reset timing is randomised by `smu_base_test`, and every piece of evidence is a DUT counter or an architectural pass/fail signal, not a TB-side inference.
* Both firmware outcomes are wired: `smc_test_fail_o` asserting fails the run at that cycle; `smc_test_pass_o` ends it.
* Activity floors make a silent PASS impossible: the scoreboard requires `smc_rom_read_count_o > 0` and `smc_scratch_write_count_o > 0` at check time, and the final sample is taken two edges after PASS because the TB write counter updates one edge after the combinational pass flag.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. In `smu_smc_smoke_seq`, assert `sep_reset_n_o == 0` during cold reset and log `sep_fuse_sense_done_o` after release.
. Log the bring-up state (`smc_fuse_sense_done_o`, `smc_fuse_reset_n_delayed_o`, `rst_primary_smc_clk_n_o`, `smc_init_mem_done_o`, `rst_cold_n_o`, `powergood_o`).
. Every `clk_smu_i` edge, sample `smc_test_pass_o`, `smc_test_fail_o`, `smc_rom_read_count_o` and `smc_scratch_write_count_o` into the scoreboard; raise on FAIL.
. On PASS, wait two edges, re-sample the counters, and return; fail on the cycle budget (`SMU_SMC_BOOT_MAX_CYCLES`, default 2 000 000).
. In `check_phase`, require PASS seen, FAIL not seen, and both activity counters above 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_reset_precondition` |`sep_reset_n_o == 0` during cold reset on the SEP=1 wrapper
|`smc_test_fail` |`smc_test_fail_o` never asserted (a FAIL ends the run at that cycle)
|`smc_test_pass` |`smc_test_pass_o` asserted before the cycle budget expired
|`smc_rom_read_floor` |`smc_rom_read_count_o > 0`
|`smc_scratch_write_floor` |`smc_scratch_write_count_o > 0`
|`CHK-NONVAC` |emitted by the scoreboard only after every term above held
|===

*Pass Criteria*: PASS observed, FAIL never observed, both activity counters above 0, scoreboard reports zero errors. Evidence tokens: `SMC_ROM_READ_OK`, `SMC_SCRATCH_WRITE_OK`, `SMC_TEST_PASS_OK`. Enrolled in: `all`, `fw_boot`.

'''''

[[sec-074-smu_smc_wdt_timeout_irq_test]]

*Source Module*: `smu_smc_wdt_timeout_irq_test`
