. Check that both waits finished inside their bounds, each recorded once with a finite bound and a last observed state, and that the steps ran in order.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-LCG-RMACHIP1-LC-EXPORT` |with the RMA_CHIPLET image `lc_state_o`, `obs_sep_lc_state_o` and `smc_lc_state_in_o` each read `0x87` on one sampled cycle
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the lifecycle settle wait and the debug settle wait each finish inside their bound with a recorded last state and no wait record shows an expiry
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the three steps and the pass point have strictly increasing simulation-time stamps
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-166-smu_lc_rma_sip_1_export_test]]
===== 166 `smu_lc_rma_sip_1_export_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_lc_rma_sip_1_export_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_lc_rma_sip_1_export_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: boundary outputs `lc_state_o`, `smc_lc_state_in_o`, `lc_sigint_err_o` and `lcc_dbg_disable_o` and the bench observation `obs_sep_lc_state_o`, all read X-strict on `clk_smu_i`; eFuse shadow preload `hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/sep_efuse_shadow_lc_rma_sip_1.preload` supplied as `+sep_shadow_reg_preload` under `+skip_fuse_sense` (the leaf fails if another image is named); `+lc_sigint_inject` absent (the leaf fails if it is set); `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images and the default SMC shadow preload

*Objective*: with the eFuse shadow image holding the RMA_SIP state, the SMU exports the LC_STATE pair `0xC3` at the wrapper boundary, at the SEP observation point and at the SMC's LC_STATE input.

*Test Coverage*:

* The SEP shadow encodes the logical state as `{~raw[3:0], raw[3:0]}` in bits [7:0] (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`, LC_STATE row), and RMA_SIP is raw `4'b001X` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/lifecycle_controller.adoc`, lifecycle state encodings table). The image programs raw `4'b0011`, so the exported word is `0xC3`. `lc_state_o` is the lifecycle state output driven by the SEP (`hw/sys/smu/doc/port_table.adoc`, `lc_state_o` row).
* The leaf waits until `smc_lc_state_in_o` reads `0xC3`, within 60000 `clk_smu_i` cycles, then until `lcc_dbg_disable_o` is unchanged for 16 consecutive cycles, within 4096 cycles; both bounds are the leaf's own.
* On one `clk_smu_i` edge after that, `lc_state_o`, `obs_sep_lc_state_o` and `smc_lc_state_in_o` each read `0xC3`.
* Measured and logged only: `lc_sigint_err_o` on that edge, `lc_state_o` one edge later, and the cycle counts of the two waits.
* The values of `lcc_dbg_disable_o`, the other lifecycle states and the debug policy behind this state are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Require the shadow preload to be the RMA_SIP image and `+lc_sigint_inject` to be absent.
. Wait until `smc_lc_state_in_o` reads `0xC3`, then until `lcc_dbg_disable_o` holds for 16 cycles.
. Sample `lc_state_o`, `obs_sep_lc_state_o` and `smc_lc_state_in_o` on one edge and compare each with `0xC3`; log `lc_sigint_err_o`.
. Check that both waits finished inside their bounds, each recorded once with a finite bound and a last observed state, and that the steps ran in order.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-LCG-RMASIP1-LC-EXPORT` |with the RMA_SIP image `lc_state_o`, `obs_sep_lc_state_o` and `smc_lc_state_in_o` each read `0xC3` on one sampled cycle
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the lifecycle settle wait and the debug settle wait each finish inside their bound with a recorded last state and no wait record shows an expiry
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the three steps and the pass point have strictly increasing simulation-time stamps
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-167-smu_sep_inbound_burst_extension_test]]
===== 167 `smu_sep_inbound_burst_extension_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sep_inbound_burst_extension_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_sep_inbound_burst_extension_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`, which extends the `smu_dtp_sep_dm_sba_test` sequence and runs the `smu_dtp_sep_dm_dmi_test` setup)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP spliced to the SEP TAP, driving the SEP debug module DMI and its system bus); `ocah_axi_vip` master on the external AXI input of the SMU, with the primary reset `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` as its reset; request and response taps on `sep_external_req` / `sep_external_resp` (the 254-bit request and 110-bit response of the SEP extension port) and on `sep_efuse_bank_ctrl_req` / `sep_efuse_bank_ctrl_resp` (AXI-Lite with a 32-bit data bus), both under `u_dut`, decoding the accepted AW, W and AR handshakes on `clk_smu_i`; `smc_lc_state_in_o` and `sep_region_size_o`; eFuse shadow preload `hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/sep_efuse_shadow_lc_test_dev.preload` as `+sep_shadow_reg_preload` under `+skip_fuse_sense`, so the SEP debug module is reachable; `+pll_sys_period_ns=10`; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images and the default SMC shadow preload

*Objective*: an INCR burst from the external AXI input into the SEP inbound port is routed on its first address, to the extension port when it starts in the extension window and to the eFuse SHIM bank control port when it starts at the SHIM base.

*Test Coverage*:

* The SEP routes a burst on its first address only, and bursts are supported into the extension port (`hw/sys/sep/doc/memory_map.adoc`, Single-Beat Register Access); a burst that begins in the SHIM control window carries its later beats to the SHIM, and the SHIM control CSRs sit at the base of `sep_external` (`doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc`, SHIM paragraph). The inbound filter admits a burst only for an enabled entry with `allow_burst` set (`doc/programmer/src/sep-smc-common.adoc`, AXI Inbound/Outbound Filters). The extension port is `sep_external_req_o` / `sep_external_resp_i` (`hw/sys/smu/doc/port_table.adoc`).
* Setup through the SEP debug module system bus, in the programmer-guide order: inbound filter entry 1 is cleared, given start `0x2000_0000` and end `0x2000_FFFF`, then written with read allowed, write allowed, entry enabled and burst allowed (`ALLOW_NS` clear), and `SEP_REGION_SIZE` is written with `0x4000_0000`. The SHIM base from the generated `sep_addr.h` equals `0x2000_0000`, the entry config reads back with those bits set, `SEP_REGION_SIZE` reads back `0x4000_0000` and `sep_region_size_o` reaches it. `SEP_GLOBAL_BASE_ADDR` stays at its reset value 0 (`hw/sys/sep/regs/blocks/sep_cpu_ctrl/sep_cpu_ctrl.rdl`), so the global address of each burst equals its SEP-local address; the aperture values are the values the leaf programs.
* Extension window: a four-beat INCR write of 8-byte beats at SEP-local `0x2000_1000` shows exactly one AW on the extension port with that address, AxLEN 3 and AxSIZE 3, four W beats and no AR, and nothing on the SHIM bank control port; the same burst read shows one AR with that address, AxLEN 3 and AxSIZE 3 and no AW or W beat on the extension port, and nothing on the SHIM port.
* SHIM base: the same burst shape at `0x2000_0000`, written and read, produces AXI-Lite transfers on the SHIM bank control port in ascending address order whose 4-byte spans together cover the 32 bytes of the burst, with as many W transfers as AW transfers on the write, and nothing on the extension port. No document states how an 8-byte beat maps onto the 32-bit SHIM port, so the 4-byte transfer width is the width of the port the leaf decodes, and the step between transfers is logged next to the beat size.
* Restore: `SEP_REGION_SIZE` is written back to its reset value and read back, `sep_region_size_o` follows it, and entry 1 reads back disabled.
* The AXI response code of each burst is logged and not compared.
* The burst length, beat size, payload bytes and wait bounds (256 `clk_smu_i` cycles between port events, 512 for a response, 64 to drain) are values the leaf programs; bursts into a register region, the crypto region and non-incrementing burst types are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Run the debug-module setup of <<sec-108-smu_dtp_sep_dm_dmi_test>>: wait for `smc_lc_state_in_o` to leave its pre-sense value and bring the SEP debug module system bus up over the primary TAP.
. Program inbound filter entry 1 and `SEP_REGION_SIZE` over the system bus, read both back, create the AXI master and measure the `clk_smu_i` period.
. Issue the four-beat write and the four-beat read at SEP-local `0x2000_1000`, each with the two port taps sampling, and compare the events on both ports.
. Issue the same two bursts at `0x2000_0000` and compare the events on both ports.
. Restore `SEP_REGION_SIZE`, disable entry 1 and read both back.
. Check that every bounded wait recorded a finite bound and a last observed state and none expired, with the expected number of wait sites of each kind, and that the step stamps increase in simulation time up to the pass stamp.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-EXT-BURST` |a four-beat INCR burst at SEP-local `0x2000_1000` reaches the extension port as one AW or AR with that address, AxLEN 3, AxSIZE 3 and four W beats on the write, and nothing reaches the SHIM bank control port
|`CHK-DBG-SHIM-BURST` |a four-beat INCR burst from the SHIM base carries every beat to the eFuse bank control port at ascending addresses covering the burst, and nothing reaches the extension port
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |every bounded wait of the leaf, on the posture, the system-bus polls, the port polls, the burst responses, the beat gaps and the drains, records a finite bound and a last observed state and none expires
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the step stamps of the leaf are strictly increasing in simulation time and the access, burst and final region-size counts match the leaf's steps
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-168-smu_sep_stap_hold_reset_test]]
===== 168 `smu_sep_stap_hold_reset_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_sep_stap_hold_reset_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_sep_stap_hold_reset_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (`OcahJtagMasterSequence` on the primary TAP, scans composed over the secondary scan string); `jtag_tms`, `jtag_trst` and `jtag_tck`; bench observation `tb_stap_sep_tms` (`u_dut.u_smu.dtp_sep_stap_tap_ctrl.tms`, the TMS of the SEP debug STAP host interface, `tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`) sampled on `clk_ref_i`; `smc_lc_state_in_o` and `lcc_dbg_disable_o`; eFuse shadow preload `hw/sys/smu/dv/assets/sep_efuse_shadow_lc_test_dev.preload` as `+sep_shadow_reg_preload` under `+skip_fuse_sense`, the TEST_DEV image, which leaves every SEP debug path open; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images and the default SMC shadow preload

*Objective*: a deselected SEP debug STAP parks its host TMS at its TMS-Hold value and adds only its SIB bit to the scan string, and its 3DCR resets on TRST and, unless Config-Hold is set, on Test-Logic-Reset.

*Test Coverage*:

* The rules are `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_stap/doc/architecture.adoc`: a STAP adds one bit to the scan chain while its SIB is closed and four while it is open; when not selected the STAP host TMS is tied to the TMS-Hold bit of its 3DCR; the 3DCR bits are TMS-Hold (bit 2), Select (bit 1) and Config-Hold (bit 0), each resetting to 0; Config-Hold preserves the 3DCR across Test-Logic-Reset, and TRST assertion always resets the register. The scan string carries the PTAP, the I/O, SMC, SEP and extra STAP SIBs in that order (`doc/integrator/src/smu-dtp.adoc`, STAP Scan Chain Topology).
* Setup: after `smc_lc_state_in_o` leaves its pre-sense value, the exported lifecycle word is the TEST_DEV word and `lcc_dbg_disable_o` reads 0, so the SEP STAP is ungated; the primary TAP is taken to Run-Test/Idle and the PTAP 3DCR is written with Select and Config-Hold set.
* TMS-Hold, for each value 0 and 1: with the SEP SIB open, the SEP 3DCR is written with Select 0, that TMS-Hold value and Config-Hold 1 and the SIB closed again; during an IDCODE scan with a 24-bit flush marker, `tb_stap_sep_tms` reads that value on every `clk_ref_i` sample of a 2000-cycle window with no edge, while `jtag_tms` shows at least as many edges as the PTAP IR is wide.
* Scan path: the flush marker reappears on TDO after the 32-bit IDCODE register plus the four SIB bits, so the deselected SEP TAP is not in the path.
* TRST: with the SEP STAP selected through the network, the 3DCR written as `3'b111` reads back `3'b111`; after a TRST pulse of 4 TCKs and a re-selection the 3DCR reads `3'b000`.
* Test-Logic-Reset with Config-Hold set: a 3DCR written as `3'b101` reads back `3'b101` and reads `3'b101` again after five TMS highs; with Config-Hold clear, a 3DCR written as `3'b100` reads back `3'b100` and reads `3'b000` after five TMS highs.
* Measured and logged only: the level of `tb_stap_sep_tms` before and after the TRST pulse, the cycle in which each TMS window's scan ends, and the final level of `tb_stap_sep_tms`. The level of `tb_stap_sep_tms` after a reset is logged and not compared.
* The PTAP, the other STAPs and the SEP debug module behind a selected SEP STAP are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Wait for the reset-done event and for `smc_lc_state_in_o` to leave its pre-sense value, then require the TEST_DEV lifecycle word and `lcc_dbg_disable_o` at 0.
. Take the TAP to Run-Test/Idle, load the PTAP IR with TAP_3DCR and write the PTAP 3DCR with Select and Config-Hold set.
. For TMS-Hold 0 and then 1, open the SEP SIB, write the SEP 3DCR deselected, scan IDCODE with the flush marker while sampling `tb_stap_sep_tms` and `jtag_tms`, and compare the parked level, the SEP TMS edge count, the PTAP edge count and the chain length.
. Write the SEP 3DCR with `3'b111`, select the SEP STAP through the network, capture the 3DCR, pulse TRST for 4 TCKs, re-select and capture again, and compare both captures.
. Write `3'b101`, capture, apply five TMS highs and a TMS low, re-select, capture and compare; repeat with `3'b100`.
. Check that every bounded wait recorded a finite bound and a last observed state and none expired, with the expected site kinds, and that the step stamps and the per-step scan counts match.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-STAP-TMS-HOLD` |with the SEP STAP deselected its host TMS holds the TMS-Hold value 0 and 1 with no edge while the PTAP TMS toggles, and the scan path is the PTAP register plus the four SIB bits
|`CHK-DBG-STAP-3DCR-RESET` |the SEP STAP 3DCR reads `3'b111` before a TRST pulse and `3'b000` after it, keeps `3'b101` across Test-Logic-Reset with Config-Hold set and reads `3'b000` after it with Config-Hold clear
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |every bounded wait of the leaf, on the posture, the reset-done poll, the two TMS windows and the TRST hold, records a finite bound and a last observed state and none expires
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the step stamps are strictly increasing in simulation time and the number of network scans in each step matches the leaf's steps
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-169-smu_smc_gpio_access_filter_test]]
===== 169 `smu_smc_gpio_access_filter_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_smc_gpio_access_filter_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_smc_gpio_access_filter_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP and the SMC fabric JTAG2AXI bridge, 4-byte reads and writes, used for setup); `ocah_axi_vip` master on the external AXI input of the SMU, with the primary reset `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` as its reset, issuing 4-byte reads and writes with AxPROT 0..7 and recording the response code and read data; the bench pad vector `gpio_pad_io` (bit 11 sampled on `clk_smu_i`) and the bench pad drive enable `tb_gpio_drive_en`, held at 0 so the bench does not drive the pad; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+expected_sep=1`, `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC and SEP shadow preloads, and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the register addresses and bit positions the leaf uses come from the generated `smc_addr.h` and `gpio_intf.h` of the register collateral

*Objective*: the GPIO pad access filter admits an access over the external AXI input only when its AxPROT equals the stored requirement, once the matching filter is enabled, and admits AxPROT 0 and 7 while both filters are disabled.

*Test Coverage*:

* Pad 11 is entry 11 of `gpio_intf[65]` at `BASE_ADDR + 0x000_3000` with a 0x10 stride (`hw/sys/smc/regs/smc.rdl`); within it DATA_CTRL is at +0x0 and ACCESS_FILTER at +0x8 (`hw/ip/gpio/regs/gpio_intf.rdl`).
* The ACCESS_FILTER rules are the RDL's: with write_filter_enable set, writes require an exact match between AWPROT and awprot_requirement; with read_filter_enable set, reads require an exact match between ARPROT and arprot_requirement; a clear enable leaves that direction unfiltered, and the register is to be read back after a write. ACCESS_FILTER resets to `0x00010100`, both enables clear and both requirements `3'b001` (`gpio_intf.rdl`; `hw/ip/gpio/doc/memmap.adoc`, Reset Values).
* Setup over JTAG2AXI: SMC GLOBAL_BASE is written with the LOCAL_BASE value and read back, and SMC inbound filter entries 0 (secure) and 1 (non-secure) are cleared, given the range from DATA_CTRL to ACCESS_FILTER + 4, enabled with read and write allowed and read back, so that an AxPROT[1] of either value reaches the GPIO block (`hw/ip/axi_filter/regs/filter_ctrl.rdl`, `allow_ns`; `doc/programmer/src/sep-smc-common.adoc`, AXI Inbound/Outbound Filters). The entry range, the aperture value and the pad number are values the leaf programs.
* Reset and unfiltered: ACCESS_FILTER of pad 11 reads `0x00010100` with response OKAY; with both filters clear, a DATA_CTRL write with AWPROT 0 sets `core2pad` and the pad reads 1, a write with AWPROT 7 clears it and the pad reads 0, and DATA_CTRL reads with ARPROT 0 and 7 return the written word, each with response OKAY.
* Write filter: after ACCESS_FILTER is written with write_filter_enable set and read back, a DATA_CTRL write toggling `core2pad` is made for each AWPROT 0..7; only AWPROT 1 completes with OKAY and moves the pad, and the other seven answer with a response other than OKAY and leave the pad unchanged. ACCESS_FILTER is then written back to its reset value with AWPROT 1 and read back.
* Read filter: after ACCESS_FILTER is written with read_filter_enable set and read back, and a known DATA_CTRL word is written, a DATA_CTRL read is made for each ARPROT 0..7; only ARPROT 1 completes with OKAY and returns the known word with the read-only status bits masked, and the other seven answer with a response other than OKAY. ACCESS_FILTER reads the written value before it is restored to its reset value.
* The response code and read data of each refused access are logged; the leaf requires only that the code differs from OKAY.
* The filter's behaviour for an initiator identity, the other pads and the AxPROT bits the filter does not name are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, write the SMC GLOBAL_BASE over JTAG2AXI, program inbound filter entries 0 and 1 and read each configuration back, then create the AXI master and hold `tb_gpio_drive_en` at 0.
. Read ACCESS_FILTER of pad 11, write DATA_CTRL with AWPROT 0 and 7, wait for the pad to follow, and read DATA_CTRL with ARPROT 0 and 7.
. Write ACCESS_FILTER with the write filter enabled and read it back, then write DATA_CTRL with each AWPROT 0..7, recording the response and the pad level, and write ACCESS_FILTER back to its reset value.
. Write ACCESS_FILTER with the read filter enabled and read it back, write a known DATA_CTRL word, read DATA_CTRL with each ARPROT 0..7, re-read ACCESS_FILTER and restore its reset value.
. Check that every JTAG2AXI poll, AXI response and pad settle finished inside its bound with a recorded last state, and that the steps and the access counts match the leaf's steps.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-GPIO-FILTER-RESET` |ACCESS_FILTER of pad 11 reads `0x00010100` and AxPROT 0 and 7 are admitted with both filters disabled
|`CHK-DBG-GPIO-FILTER-WRITE` |with write_filter_enable set only an AWPROT equal to awprot_requirement is admitted and the other seven values are refused without changing the pad
|`CHK-DBG-GPIO-FILTER-READ` |with read_filter_enable set only an ARPROT equal to arprot_requirement returns the DATA_CTRL value and the other seven values are refused
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |every JTAG2AXI poll, AXI response and pad settle wait of the leaf finishes inside its bound with a recorded last state and no wait record shows an expiry
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the five steps and the pass point have strictly increasing simulation-time stamps and the JTAG2AXI and AXI access counts match the leaf's steps
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-170-smu_smc_gpio_spi_pad_ownership_test]]
===== 170 `smu_smc_gpio_spi_pad_ownership_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_smc_gpio_spi_pad_ownership_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_smc_gpio_spi_pad_ownership_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP and the SMC fabric JTAG2AXI bridge, 4-byte writes and one read, used for setup); `ocah_axi_vip` master on the external AXI input of the SMU, with the primary reset `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` as its reset, issuing 4-byte DATA_CTRL reads and writes; the bench pad vector `gpio_pad_io` (sampled on `clk_smu_i`), the bench pad drive enable and drive value `tb_gpio_drive_en` and `tb_gpio_drive_val`, and the bench GPIO interrupt vector `tb_gpio_interrupt` (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`); `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+expected_sep=1`, `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC and SEP shadow preloads, and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the register addresses and bit positions the leaf uses come from the generated `smc_addr.h` and `gpio_intf.h` of the register collateral

*Objective*: with the SMU holding the SEP, GPIO pads 0-3, 8 and 9 follow the SEP SPI host and ignore their DATA_CTRL register until `lsio_disable` is set, and pads 4-7, 10 and 54 are not driven by the SPI host and answer to the register only once `lsio_disable` is set.

*Test Coverage*:

* The ownership rules are `doc/integrator/src/smu-sep.adoc`, SPI host pad table: pads 0-3, 8 and 9 carry the SEP SPI host signals, pads 4-7, 10 and 54 are "Not driven. Output disabled, input disabled", a pad follows the SEP SPI host while its `DATA_CTRL.lsio_disable` is clear, and setting `lsio_disable` returns the pad to register control. The DATA_CTRL fields are `hw/ip/gpio/regs/gpio_intf.rdl`: `core2pad`, `enable_rx_tx` (`2'b01` transmit, `2'b10` receive, `2'b11` both), `interface_enable`, `interrupt_enable`, `lsio_disable`, and the read-only `lsio_enable`, which is set while the hardware LSIO request is asserted and `lsio_disable` is clear. DATA_CTRL of pad N is at `gpio_intf[N] @ BASE_ADDR + 0x000_3000 + N * 0x10` (`hw/sys/smc/regs/smc.rdl`).
* Setup over JTAG2AXI: SMC GLOBAL_BASE is written with the LOCAL_BASE value and read back, and SMC inbound filter entry 0 is given the range from DATA_CTRL of pad 0 to DATA_CTRL of the last pad + 4 with read and write allowed, so the external AXI master reaches DATA_CTRL; `interrupt_enable` is then written on pads 4-7, 10 and 54. The entry range and the aperture value are values the leaf programs.
* SPI-host pads: on each of pads 0-3, 8 and 9 DATA_CTRL reads `lsio_enable` 1, and after writes of `interface_enable` with transmit and receive enabled and `core2pad` 1 and then 0, the pad level equals the level sampled before the writes on every `clk_smu_i` sample for 64 cycles after each write.
* `lsio_disable`: on each of pads 0, 8 and 9, a write of `lsio_disable`, `interface_enable` and receive and transmit enabled makes DATA_CTRL read `lsio_enable` 0; the pad then reads 1 within 64 cycles of a write of `core2pad` 1 and 0 within 64 cycles of a write of `core2pad` 0, reads 1 with transmit only and `core2pad` 1, and, with the bench driving the pad high, its `tb_gpio_interrupt` bit reads 1 within 64 cycles with receive and `interrupt_enable` set and stays 0 for 64 cycles with receive disabled.
* Undriven pads: on each of pads 4-7, 10 and 54 with `lsio_disable` clear, a transmit-only write with `core2pad` 1 leaves the pad level at the value sampled before it for 64 cycles, and with the bench driving the pad high a receive write with `interrupt_enable` leaves the `tb_gpio_interrupt` bit at 0 for 64 cycles; with `lsio_disable` set, the same writes make the pad read 1 and the interrupt bit read 1 within 64 cycles.
* Priority: the register writes the leaf makes with the SPI request present change none of pads 0, 8 and 9, and after `lsio_disable` the same register values change all three.
* Measured and logged only: `lsio_enable` of the undriven pads and the pad level before each leg. The active level of the boundary enables `core2pad_en_o` and `pad2core_en_o` is not observed, because the bench has no tap on them, so no claim is made about the output enable and input enable.
* Pads other than the twelve named, the SPI protocol on the pads and the pad shim `CONTROL.hw2_ovrd` path are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, write the SMC GLOBAL_BASE and read it back, program inbound filter entry 0 over JTAG2AXI, create the AXI master and write `interrupt_enable` on pads 4-7, 10 and 54.
. For pads 0-3, 8 and 9, read DATA_CTRL, write `core2pad` 1 and 0 with `interface_enable` and receive and transmit enabled, and hold-sample the pad level after each write.
. For pads 0, 8 and 9, write `lsio_disable`, read DATA_CTRL, write `core2pad` 1 and 0 and wait for the pad level, write transmit only and wait for the pad level, drive the pad high and write receive with `interrupt_enable` and wait for the interrupt bit, then write receive disabled and hold-sample the interrupt bit.
. For pads 4-7, 10 and 54, run the transmit leg and the receive leg with `lsio_disable` clear, hold-sampling the pad level and the interrupt bit, then with `lsio_disable` set, waiting for both, and read DATA_CTRL.
. Derive the priority table from the records of the second and third steps and compare it.
. Check that every JTAG2AXI poll, AXI response, settle wait and hold window finished inside its bound with a recorded last state, with the expected number of sites of each kind, and that the steps ran in order.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-GPIO-SPI-OWNED` |pads 0-3, 8 and 9 read `lsio_enable` 1 and keep their level under `core2pad` 1 and 0
|`CHK-DBG-GPIO-SPI-DISABLE` |`lsio_disable` clears `lsio_enable` on pads 0, 8 and 9 and the pad follows `core2pad`, the transmit-only write and the receive interrupt
|`CHK-DBG-GPIO-SPI-UNDRIVEN` |pads 4-7, 10 and 54 are not driven by the SPI host in either direction until `lsio_disable` is set
|`CHK-DBG-GPIO-SPI-PRIORITY` |the same register write is ignored with `lsio_disable` clear and obeyed with it set
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |every JTAG2AXI poll, AXI response, settle wait and hold window of the leaf finishes inside its bound with a recorded last state, with the expected number of sites of each kind, and no wait record shows an expiry
|`CHK-NONVAC` |the six steps and the pass point have strictly increasing simulation-time stamps and the JTAG2AXI and AXI access counts match the leaf's steps
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`.

'''''

[[sec-171-smu_dtp_jtag2axi_series_contention_test]]
===== 171 `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_series_contention_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_series_contention_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_series_contention_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP, driven at the TCK period the bench draws for the seed and logs, with `min_jtag_smu_ratio = 4.0` so TCK is at least four `clk_smu_i` periods; instructions `SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP`, `SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL`, `SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR`, `SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR` and `SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS`); the bench taps `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` and the launch counters `dtp_smc_dbg_aw_count_o`, `dtp_smc_dbg_ar_count_o` and `dtp_smc_dbg_b_count_o` (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`), sampled on `clk_smu_i`; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC and SEP shadow preloads, and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the scratch and outbound mailbox 0 addresses (`READ_DATA`, `STATUS`) come from the generated `smc_addr.h` and the `STATUS.EMPTY` bit mask from the generated mailbox header, both through `seq_lib.smu_addr_map`

*Objective*: the SMC fabric JTAG2AXI bridge moves series data to and from the SMC scratch words at the right address, lane and width, reports the first series failure until reset without letting `SINGLE_OP` change or read that code, and completes a `SINGLE_OP` retry that is given Run-Test/Idle TCKs.

*Test Coverage*:

* Series geometry is `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc`, `*_AXI_SERIES_CTRL` (63 scan bits for the 56-bit address of the SMC fabric row of the bridge table in `hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc`), `*_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR` (the address advances `2**E`, the data register is `8 * 2**E` bits) and `*_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR` (the address does not change, the payload rides the lanes the address selects). The scratch words are eight 32-bit words at `0xC0002880` (`SMC_TOP_SMC_MISC_WRAP_SCRATCH_COLD_WARM_SCRATCH_BASE_ADDR` in `smc_addr.h`) and CPU_CTRL `SCRATCH(8)` at `0xC00390C0` implements `data[31:0]` only.
* INCR: an eight-beat 32-bit write raises the AW and B counters at the SMU boundary by 8 and leaves `SERIES_CTRL.op` 0, `SINGLE_OP` reads of words 0, 1, 6 and 7 return W0, W1, W6 and W7, one `SERIES_CTRL` read load with two data scans returns W1 on capture 2, and a 64-bit leg raises the counters by 2 and reads back `0x5A5A0008` and `0x5A5A0009` at CPU_CTRL `SCRATCH(8)` and the next 8 bytes. The eight-scan read stream and its AR counter delta are measured and logged only.
* NO_INCR: two NO_INCR writes at word 5 (address bit 2 set) raise the AW and B counters by 2 and leave the second word there, `SINGLE_OP` reads return it and leave the neighbour word 4 of the same 8-byte beat at its earlier value, and a single-load NO_INCR read returns it on capture 2 before a `SINGLE_OP` rewrite and the rewritten word after it.
* WITH_ERROR_STATUS: the data register is 33 bits (`*_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS`); the increment bits 1, 0, 1, 1 steer the write so P0, P2 and P3 land on words 0 to 2 (counters up by 4), the status bit shifted out of every write scan reads 0, and a single-load read with increment bits 1 and 0 returns P0 with status 0 on capture 2. As the positive control of the status-0 compares, a `SERIES_CTRL` read load at `LOCAL_BASE + 0x001_7000` (a DECERR gap, `hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc`) followed by two WITH_ERROR_STATUS scans with increment 0 shifts out status 1 on the second scan (`architecture.adoc`); `SERIES_CTRL.reset` follows.
* Pipeline: for `pl_depth` 0 and 3 (the two ends of the range of the bridge capability fields, `*_JTAG2AXI_CAPS`) two write beats shifted back to back raise the AW and B counters by 2, `SINGLE_OP` reads return them in order and `SERIES_CTRL.op` reads 0 after the leg. The `pl_depth` field of every capture is measured and logged only; no read pipeline is scanned.
* Sticky status: `SERIES_CTRL.op` is 1 after an SLVERR and 2 after a DECERR, reports the first failure since `SERIES_CTRL.reset`, is not replaced by a later failure or an OKAY beat, and `SINGLE_OP` status neither changes nor reads it (`*_AXI_SERIES_CTRL` op field). The SLVERR source is a read of the outbound mailbox 0 `READ_DATA` register (`SMC_TOP_SMC_MAILBOX_OUTBOUND_MAILBOX_0_READ_DATA_BASE_ADDR` in `smc_addr.h`) while the mailbox is empty, which answers SLVERR with data `0xFEEDDEAD` (`hw/ip/axi_lite_mailbox_unit/doc/interface.adoc`, Access Results), after a `SINGLE_OP` read of `STATUS` shows `EMPTY` set. A read at `LOCAL_BASE + 0x001_7000` answers DECERR with `0xBADCAB1E` (`hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc`). Both orders run with a positive control: an OKAY beat and a clean single-load read follow each failure, and a reset followed by an OKAY beat shows op 0 for each latched code. The response of a write at `0x1_7000`, which the chapter states as SLVERR, is measured and logged only. Every poll waits for `SERIES_CTRL.op` to leave 3 and, after writes, for the B counter.
* `SINGLE_OP`: a read reports op 0 with the written word and a read of the empty mailbox `READ_DATA` reports op 1 with `0xFEEDDEAD` while code 2 stays latched in `SERIES_CTRL`.
* Run-Test/Idle spacing (`*_AXI_SINGLE_OP` op 3): the op field and the AR counter delta of each unspaced `SINGLE_OP` pair are measured and logged only; the retry after 64 Run-Test/Idle TCKs and the spaced control pairs are compared.
* Measured and logged only: the eight-scan read stream and its AR counter delta, the first-capture beats and marker payloads, the `pl_depth` field of every `SERIES_CTRL` capture, the address field captured after a series, the op field of a `SINGLE_OP` write at `0x1_7000`, the TCK count per operation, and the unspaced op field and AR delta.
* The SERIES_CTRL behaviour below the four-to-one ratio, the E=0 and E=1 sizes, write strobes and the OTP bridges are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, read IDCODE, wait for `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` to read 0, sample the three counters, scan SERIES_CTRL once with a marker to measure its width, and write `SERIES_CTRL.reset`.
. Write eight distinct words with INCR at `0xC0002880`, poll `SERIES_CTRL.op` and the B counter, and compare the counters.
. Read words 0, 1, 6 and 7 back with `SINGLE_OP`, log the eight-scan read stream, then compare capture 2 of a single-load read of word 1.
. Write two 64-bit words at CPU_CTRL `SCRATCH(8)` and read them back with `SINGLE_OP`.
. Write two words with NO_INCR at word 5, read words 5 and 4 back with `SINGLE_OP`, read word 5 with a single-load NO_INCR read, rewrite it with `SINGLE_OP` and read it again.
. Measure the 33-bit width, write four beats with WITH_ERROR_STATUS and the increment bits, read words 0 to 2 back with `SINGLE_OP`, compare a single-load read, then load a read at `0xC0017000`, scan twice with increment 0 and compare the status bit of the second scan, and write `SERIES_CTRL.reset`.
. For `pl_depth` 0 and 3, shift two write beats back to back, wait for `SERIES_CTRL.op` and the B counter, and read both words back with `SINGLE_OP`.
. Reset, read mailbox `STATUS` for `EMPTY`, read the empty `READ_DATA` with a single-load read, add an OKAY beat, read the gap and word 6 with single-load reads, log a `SINGLE_OP` write at `0x1_7000`, and reset again with an OKAY beat after it.
. Read the gap first with a single-load read, read word 6 with a single-load read and read the empty mailbox `READ_DATA` with a single-load read.
. With code 2 latched, run a `SINGLE_OP` read of word 6 and of the empty mailbox `READ_DATA`, scan SERIES_CTRL after each, then reset and add an OKAY beat.
. Issue SINGLE_OP pairs with no Run-Test/Idle TCK between them (up to eight attempts), complete the retry after 64 idle TCKs, and run spaced control pairs.
. Print the checker lines in step order, check that every wait finished inside its bound with the stated site count per kind, and stamp the pass point after the last step.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SERIES-SETUP` |the gate tap reads 0 before the first scan, SERIES_CTRL scans 63 bits wide and reads op 0 after `SERIES_CTRL.reset`, and the counter baselines are sampled
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SERIES-INCR` |an eight-beat 32-bit INCR write raises the AW and B counters by 8 and leaves op 0, SINGLE_OP reads return W0, W1, W6 and W7, a single-load series read returns W1 on capture 2, the 64-bit leg raises the counters by 2 and reads back both words, and the eight-scan read stream is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SERIES-NOINCR` |a two-beat NO_INCR write leaves the last word at word 5 with the AW and B counters up by 2, word 4 of the same 8-byte beat is unchanged, and a single-load NO_INCR read returns X2 before a SINGLE_OP rewrite and X3 after it
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SERIES-ERRSTATUS` |WITH_ERROR_STATUS scans are 33 bits wide, report status 0 on every OKAY beat, land P0, P2, P3 on words 0 to 2 by the increment bits with the counters up by 4, a single-load read returns P0 with status 0, a read of 0xC0017000 followed by two scans with increment 0 shifts out status 1 on the second, and `SERIES_CTRL.reset` returns op from 2 to 0
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SERIES-PIPELINE` |for `pl_depth` 0 and 3 two back-to-back write beats raise the AW and B counters by 2 and land in order, and op reads 0 after the leg; the pending indication is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-STICKY-SLVERR-FIRST` |with the mailbox STATUS read showing EMPTY, a series read of the empty mailbox READ_DATA returns `0xFEEDDEAD` and `SERIES_CTRL.op` reads 1, stays 1 after an OKAY beat and after a DECERR gap read (`0xBADCAB1E`), and a single-load read of word 6 is clean; the `0x1_7000` window write response is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-STICKY-DECERR-FIRST` |`SERIES_CTRL.op` reads 2 after a DECERR gap read and stays 2 after a clean single-load read and an empty mailbox READ_DATA series read that returns `0xFEEDDEAD`
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-STICKY-RESET` |`SERIES_CTRL.reset` returns op from 1 and from 2 to 0 and a following OKAY beat keeps it 0
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SINGLE-INDEP` |with series code 2 latched a SINGLE_OP OKAY read reports op 0 and a SINGLE_OP read of the empty mailbox READ_DATA reports op 1 with `0xFEEDDEAD`, and `SERIES_CTRL.op` stays 2 after each
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-BUSY-OP3` |the op field and AR delta of unspaced SINGLE_OP pairs are measured and logged only, the retry after 64 Run-Test/Idle TCKs reads op 0 with word 7, and spaced pairs read op 0
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the gate wait, the `SERIES_CTRL.op` polls, the B counter waits, the SINGLE_OP capture polls, the unspaced-attempt loop and its single retry capture site finish inside their bounds with a recorded last state and the stated site count per kind
|`CHK-NONVAC` |ordered sim-time fence S1<...<S12<PASS, the pass point is stamped after the last step and every step has a nonzero scan count
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-172-smu_dtp_jtag2axi_caps_single_op_test]]
===== 172 `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_caps_single_op_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_caps_single_op_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_caps_single_op_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP, driven at the TCK period the bench draws for the seed and logs; instructions `SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS`, `SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS`, `SEP_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS`, `IDCODE`, BYPASS and `SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP`); the bench taps `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` and the launch counters `dtp_smc_dbg_aw_count_o`, `dtp_smc_dbg_ar_count_o` and `dtp_smc_dbg_b_count_o` (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`), sampled on `clk_smu_i`; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC and SEP shadow preloads, and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the CPU_CTRL `SCRATCH(13)` and outbound mailbox 0 (`READ_DATA`, `STATUS`) addresses come from the generated `smc_addr.h` and the `STATUS.EMPTY` bit mask from the generated mailbox header, both through `seq_lib.smu_addr_map`

*Objective*: the three JTAG2AXI bridges report the capability words of the bridge table, the words are read-only and stable, and the SMC fabric `SINGLE_OP` op field reports SLVERR, DECERR and OKAY as the status of the previous operation without leaving a sticky status or sending anything for op 0.

*Test Coverage*:

* Capability words: the field positions and encodings are the PTAP JTAG2AXI capability fields table of `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc` (`*_JTAG2AXI_CAPS`), the values the DTP JTAG2AXI bridges table of `hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc`. The SMC fabric word is AXI4 (`bus_type` 0), `addr_size` 56, `data_size` 3 (8 bytes), `wr_pl_depth` 3 and `rd_pl_depth` 3, 0x3DF0. The SMC OTP and SEP OTP words are AXI4-Lite (`bus_type` 1), 32, 2 (4 bytes), 3 and 3, 0x3D41 each. Every word is built with `pack_jtag2axi_caps` from those tables and each field is compared separately.
* Read-only and stable: the register is a read-only TDR (`*_JTAG2AXI_CAPS` in `architecture.adoc`). All zeros, all ones and 0x2AAA are shifted into each 14-bit register and updated, and each recapture equals the word above, as does the recapture after an IDCODE scan and after a BYPASS load.
* `SINGLE_OP` geometry: the register is 132 bits (op 2, size 2, wstrb 8, data 64, address 56; `*_AXI_SINGLE_OP` fields with the SMC fabric widths of `hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc`), measured by shifting a marker through a longer scan.
* SLVERR status: a `SINGLE_OP` read of `STATUS` of the outbound mailbox 0 reads `EMPTY` 1 with op 0, then a read of `READ_DATA` of the empty mailbox captures op 1 with `0xFEEDDEAD` (`hw/ip/axi_lite_mailbox_unit/doc/interface.adoc`, Access Results) and raises the AR counter by 1. The capture before it reads op 0, so op 1 is the first error status of the run. The op field of the `SINGLE_OP` write at `LOCAL_BASE + 0x001_7000`, which `hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc` states as SLVERR, is measured and logged only.
* DECERR status: a read at `LOCAL_BASE + 0x001_7000`, an address between unit apertures, captures op 2 with `0xBADCAB1E` (`hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc`) and raises the AR counter by 1.
* OKAY status: the capture before the write reads op 2; a write of `0x0A11CE5E` (non-zero, differing from the reset value 0 of `cpu_ctrl.rdl`) to CPU_CTRL `SCRATCH(13)` at `0xC00390E8` reads op 0 with the AW and B counters up by 1, and a read of it, issued right after a second gap read whose capture holds `0xBADCAB1E` and so differs from the written word, returns op 0, the written word and an AR counter up by 1.
* Recovery: a second empty-mailbox read captures op 1 with `0xFEEDDEAD`, a clean read of `SCRATCH(13)` then captures op 0 with `0x0A11CE5E` (no sticky status and no alias onto the live register), and the write that followed the DECERR read read op 0.
* Nop: two op 0 scans with a non-zero address, data and strobe leave the AR counter flat and, sampled after the op 1 scan of the same fields completes, the AW counter flat (`*_AXI_SINGLE_OP` op 0 sends nothing over AXI); the op 1 scan raises the AR counter by 1.
* Measured and logged only: the op field and counter deltas of the `SINGLE_OP` write at `0x1_7000`.
* Per-size and per-strobe `SINGLE_OP` matrices, the reserved op 3 and the series instructions are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, read IDCODE, wait for `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` to read 0, sample the three counters and measure the `SINGLE_OP` scan width.
. Scan `SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS` and compare the word and its fields.
. Scan `SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS` and `SEP_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS` and compare each word and its fields.
. For each of the three registers shift in zeros, ones and 0x2AAA and recapture after each, then recapture after an IDCODE scan and after a BYPASS load.
. Read mailbox 0 `STATUS` for `EMPTY`, capture, read the empty `READ_DATA`, capture, and write `0xDEAD0003` at `0xC0017000` once (logged only).
. Read `0xC0017000` and poll the capture until the op field leaves 3.
. Capture, write `0x0A11CE5E` to `SCRATCH(13)` and poll the capture.
. Read `0xC0017000` again, then read `SCRATCH(13)` and poll the capture.
. Read the empty mailbox `READ_DATA` again, then read `SCRATCH(13)` cleanly.
. Scan two op 0 words with a non-zero address, data and strobe, then scan the same fields with op 1.
. Print the checker lines in step order, check that every wait finished inside its bound with the stated site count per kind, and stamp the pass point after the last step.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-CAPS-SETUP` |the SMC fabric gate tap reads 0 before the first scan, IDCODE reads the documented value and the AW, AR and B counter baselines are sampled
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-CAPS-SMC` |SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS reads 0x3DF0 with bus_type 0, addr_size 56, data_size 3, wr_pl_depth 3 and rd_pl_depth 3, each field compared separately
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-CAPS-OTP` |SMC_OTP and SEP_OTP JTAG2AXI_CAPS both read 0x3D41 with bus_type 1, addr_size 32, data_size 2, wr_pl_depth 3 and rd_pl_depth 3, each field compared separately
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-CAPS-STABLE` |each CAPS word recaptures as 0x3DF0, 0x3D41 and 0x3D41 after zeros, ones and 0x2AAA shift-ins and after an intervening IDCODE scan and BYPASS load
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SINGLE-SLVERR` |STATUS reads EMPTY with op 0, the empty-mailbox READ_DATA read captures op 1 with 0xFEEDDEAD and raises the AR counter by 1 after a capture of op 0, the SINGLE_OP scan is 132 bits wide, and the response of the 0xC0017000 window write is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SINGLE-DECERR` |a read at 0xC0017000 captures op 2 with 0xBADCAB1E and raises the AR counter by 1
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SINGLE-OKAY` |the capture before a write of SCRATCH[13] reads op 2, the write reads op 0 with the AW and B counters up by 1, a gap read right before the read of SCRATCH[13] captures op 2 with 0xBADCAB1E, and the read returns 0x0A11CE5E with op 0 and the AR counter up by 1
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SINGLE-RECOVERY` |a second empty-mailbox READ_DATA read captures op 1 with 0xFEEDDEAD and a clean read of SCRATCH[13] captures op 0 with 0x0A11CE5E
|`CHK-DBG-J2A-SINGLE-NOP` |two op 0 scans with a non-zero address, data and strobe leave the AR counter flat and, after the op 1 scan of the same fields completes, the AW counter flat and the AR counter up by 1
|`CHK-NONVAC` |ordered sim-time fence S1<...<S11<PASS, the pass point is stamped after the last step and every step has a nonzero scan count
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the gate wait and the ten SINGLE_OP capture polls finish inside their bounds with a recorded last state and the stated site count per kind
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-173-smu_dtp_otp_sep_series_error_test]]
===== 173 `smu_dtp_otp_sep_series_error_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_otp_sep_series_error_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_dtp_otp_sep_series_error_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP, driven at the TCK period the bench draws for the seed and logs; instructions `IDCODE`, `SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP`, `SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL`, `SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR`, `SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR` and `SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP`); the bench taps `lc_state_o` and `tb_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable` and the launch counters `dtp_smc_dbg_aw_count_o`, `dtp_smc_dbg_ar_count_o` and `dtp_smc_dbg_b_count_o` (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`), sampled on `clk_smu_i`; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC and SEP shadow preloads, and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the SPARE0 and interface-control window addresses come from the generated `sep_addr.h` (a `c_header_u32` read through `seq_lib.smu_addr_map`)

*Objective*: the SEP OTP JTAG2AXIL bridge writes and reads the SEP shadow-map SPARE0 words through its series instructions, answers the first in-span address that owns no register with SLVERR as a `SINGLE_OP` op and as a latched series code, completes the next beat with the correct data, clears the code with `SERIES_CTRL.reset`, and leaves the DTP-to-SMC debug AXI port untouched.

*Test Coverage*:

* Series write and read: the SPARE0 words are at `0x109302E0` and `0x109302E4` (`SEP_TOP_SEP_EFUSE_MAP_SPARE0_BASE_ADDR`, `hw/sys/sep/regs/gen/c/sep_addr.h`). `SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR` writes the non-zero, distinct words `0x13579BDF` and `0x2468ACE0` (`hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc`: `*_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR` leaves the address unchanged), `SERIES_CTRL.op` reads 0 after each, and a single-load NO_INCR read (one `SERIES_CTRL` read load, two data scans) and single-load INCR reads of both words return capture 2 equal to the word written.
* `SINGLE_OP` geometry: the register is 72 bits (`2 + S + D/8 + D + A` with D 32, A 32 and S 2; `*_AXI_SINGLE_OP` in `architecture.adoc` and the SEP OTP row of the bridges table in `hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc`), measured by shifting a marker through a longer scan.
* Hole, `SINGLE_OP`: the first in-span offset that owns no register is `0x1093041C`, the word after `SEP_TOP_EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_BASE_ADDR + SEP_TOP_EFUSE_INTERFACE_CTRL_SIZE` and below `SEP_TOP_EFUSE_MMR_BASE_ADDR` (`sep_addr.h`); the APB decode answers any address in the span outside the register windows with SLVERR (`hw/sys/sep/doc/otp_fuse_controller.adoc`). A read there captures op 1, and a `SINGLE_OP` read of SPARE0 word 0 right after it captures op 0 with `0x13579BDF`.
* Hole, series: `SERIES_CTRL.op` reads 0 before a series beat at `0x1093041C` and 1 after it, the code being sticky until `SERIES_CTRL.reset` and reporting the first failure (`*_AXI_SERIES_CTRL` op in `architecture.adoc`); the single-load read of SPARE0 word 0 after the hole returns `0x13579BDF` and op stays 1; a `SINGLE_OP` read beside the latched code captures op 0 with `0x13579BDF` and op stays 1 (the completion status of `SINGLE_OP` does not change the series code).
* Isolation: the AW, AR and B counters at the SMU boundary equal their baselines after all SEP OTP traffic (`hw/sys/dtp/doc/jtag.adoc`: the three JTAG2AXI bridges are separate instruments); an SMC fabric `SINGLE_OP` read at `LOCAL_BASE + 0x001_7000` = `0xC0017000` (between unit apertures, `hw/sys/smc/doc/memmap.adoc`) captures op 2 and a read of CPU_CTRL `SCRATCH(13)` at `0xC00390E8` (`smc_addr.h`, `SMC_TOP_SMC_CPU_CTRL_SCRATCH_BASE_ADDR(13)`) captures op 0, with the AR counter up by exactly 2 and the AW and B counters unchanged.
* Reset: a `SERIES_CTRL` write with bit 38 set (the reset field of the 39-bit control register, `architecture.adoc`) captures op 1 and the next capture reads op 0; the single-load series read of word 0 after it returns `0x13579BDF` with op 0.
* Gate and sensing: `lc_state_o` leaves its pre-sense value inside 2000 polls of 50 `clk_smu_i` cycles and `tb_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable` reads 0 before any SEP OTP access.
* Measured and logged only: the three-scan INCR stream from one load, the marker beats and the first capture of each series read, the `pl_depth` field of `SERIES_CTRL` captures, the data word of the `SINGLE_OP` hole read, the first capture of the SPARE0 load that follows the series hole beat, and the data word of the fabric gap read.
* The `LOCKS` registers, writes at the hole and addresses outside `0x10930000..0x10930573` are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, read IDCODE, wait for `lc_state_o` to leave its pre-sense value, read `tb_otp_jtag2axi_security_disable`, sample the three counters and write `SERIES_CTRL.reset`.
. Write `0x13579BDF` and `0x2468ACE0` to the SPARE0 words through `SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR`, polling `SERIES_CTRL.op` after each, then read word 0 back with one load and two NO_INCR scans.
. Read each SPARE0 word with one `SERIES_CTRL` load and two INCR scans, then read one further three-scan INCR stream (logged only).
. Measure the `SINGLE_OP` width, read `0x1093041C` and poll the capture, then read SPARE0 word 0 and poll the capture.
. Capture `SERIES_CTRL.op`, load a read at `0x1093041C` with one NO_INCR scan and poll `SERIES_CTRL.op`, then read SPARE0 word 0 with one load and two NO_INCR scans and poll `SERIES_CTRL.op`.
. Read SPARE0 word 0 with `SINGLE_OP` and capture `SERIES_CTRL.op`, then read the fabric gap at `0xC0017000` and `SCRATCH(13)` with the SMC `SINGLE_OP` and compare the counters.
. Write `SERIES_CTRL.reset`, capture `SERIES_CTRL.op`, then read SPARE0 word 0 with one load and two NO_INCR scans and poll `SERIES_CTRL.op`.
. Print the checker lines in step order, check that every wait finished inside its bound with the stated site count per kind, and stamp the pass point after the last step.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-SEP-OTP-SETUP` |lc_state_o leaves its pre-sense value inside its bound, the SEP OTP gate tap reads 0, IDCODE reads the documented value, the AW, AR and B counter baselines are sampled and SERIES_CTRL.reset leaves op 0
|`CHK-DBG-SEP-OTP-POSCTRL` |NO_INCR writes of 0x13579BDF and 0x2468ACE0 to the SPARE0 words leave op 0, the single-load NO_INCR read returns the first word and the single-load INCR reads return both words with op 0, and the 72-bit SINGLE_OP read of word 0 captures op 0 with the first word; the three-scan INCR stream is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-SEP-OTP-HOLE-SINGLE` |a SINGLE_OP read at 0x1093041C captures op 1 and the SINGLE_OP read of SPARE0 word 0 right after it captures op 0 with 0x13579BDF; the data word of the SLVERR read is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-SEP-OTP-HOLE-SERIES` |SERIES_CTRL.op reads 0 before a series beat at 0x1093041C and 1 after it, the single-load read of SPARE0 word 0 after it returns 0x13579BDF with op 1, and op stays 1 beside a SINGLE_OP read that captures op 0; the first capture of the SPARE0 load after the series hole beat is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-SEP-OTP-ISOLATION` |the AW, AR and B counters equal their baselines after all SEP OTP traffic, then the SMC fabric SINGLE_OP read at 0xC0017000 captures op 2 and the read of 0xC00390E8 captures op 0 with the AR counter up by 2 and the AW and B counters unchanged
|`CHK-DBG-SEP-OTP-RESET` |the SERIES_CTRL write with bit 38 set captures op 1 and the next capture reads op 0, and the single-load series read of SPARE0 word 0 after it returns 0x13579BDF with op 0
|`CHK-NONVAC` |ordered sim-time fence S1<...<S8<PASS, the pass point is stamped after the last step and every step has a nonzero scan count
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the sensing wait, the SERIES_CTRL.op polls and the SINGLE_OP capture polls finish inside their bounds with a recorded last state and the stated site count per kind
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-174-smu_dtp_sep_ic_reset_effect_test]]
===== 174 `smu_dtp_sep_ic_reset_effect_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_sep_ic_reset_effect_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_dtp_sep_ic_reset_effect_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP, driven at the TCK period the bench draws for the seed and logs; instructions `IDCODE`, `JTAG_CAPS`, `IC_RESET`, `TAP_3DCR` and `SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP`; the SEP STAP selected through the PTAP `TAP_3DCR`, SIB and STAP 3DCR scans with its debug TAP `dmi` register and the debug module system bus `sbcs`, `sbaddress0` and `sbdata0`); the bench taps `sep_reset_n_o` (the SEP reset after the JTAG override), `obs_sep_rst_n_o` (the reset at the SEP input, logged only), `obs_sep_clk_o`, `sep_trace_valid_o`, `obs_smc_rst_n_o` (`rst_primary_smc_clk_no`), `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ovrd` and `jtag_ic_reset_ext_ctrl_n` (the two members of `jtag_ic_reset_ext_o`), `lc_state_o` and `lcc_dbg_disable_o` (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`), sampled on `clk_smu_i`; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC shadow preload and the TEST_DEV SEP shadow preload `sep_efuse_shadow_lc_test_dev.preload`, which opens the SEP debug STAP, and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the SEP cold scratch word address comes from the generated `sep_addr.h` and the SMC `SCRATCH[11]` address from `smc_addr.h`

*Objective*: the `sep_reset_n` pair of the IC_RESET SEP slice, programmed in the documented order, holds the SEP main reset low and clears SEP cold scratch word 0, Test-Logic-Reset keeps or releases the override according to `reset_hold`, the documented release order restores the SEP, and the SMC slice, the external slice and the SMC fabric stay untouched while the override holds.

*Test Coverage*:

* Layout: with `num_ext_ic_rst` 1 in `JTAG_CAPS` (bits 24:17, `hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc`), 68 SMC and 8 SEP ports, the `IC_RESET` TDR is 155 bits (`doc/integrator/src/smu-composition.adoc`), measured by shifting the reset image ahead of 32 extra bits. Port _n_ counted from TDO holds `reset_enable` at bit 2n+1 and `reset_control` at bit 2n+2 (`architecture.adoc`, PTAP IC_RESET fields); the SEP slice runs `km`, `otbn`, `aes`, `hmac`, `kmac`, `sep_reset_n`, `trng`, `abr` from the external slice inward (`smu-composition.adoc`), so `sep_reset_n` is port 6 with `reset_enable` at bit 13 and `reset_control` at bit 14, derived in the sequence from the helper port constants.
* Positive control: SEP cold scratch word 0 (`SEP_TOP_SEP_SCRATCH_COLD_SCRATCH_BASE_ADDR(0)`, `0x10802000`, `sep_addr.h`) is written with `0x5EC01D5E` over the SEP debug system bus and read back; `sep_reset_n_o` reads 1 and `obs_sep_clk_o` shows both levels. Scratch set 0 is cleared by `sep_reset_n` (`hw/sys/sep/doc/cpu.adoc`, Scratch-bank reset behavior); the word reads 0 after each override where the non-zero word stood before it.
* Attribution control: with no override engaged, five TMS-high TCKs (Test-Logic-Reset), a STAP re-select and a `dmactive` re-activation leave cold scratch word 0 equal to `0x5EC01D5E` over the system bus.
* Engage: reset_control first (bit 14 = 0 with bit 13 = 1) leaves `sep_reset_n_o` at 1 and the scratch word at `0x5EC01D5E`; reset_enable second (bits 13 and 14 = 0) takes `sep_reset_n_o` to 0 and holds it on every one of 2000 `clk_smu_i` cycles, with `obs_sep_clk_o` at both levels (`hw/sys/dtp/doc/clk_rst.adoc`, IC_RESET overrides). The capture of the held image clears exactly bits 13 and 14.
* Isolation: while the override holds, `jtag_ic_reset_ext_o` reads ovrd 0 and val 1, `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` reads 1 on every sample of a 500-sample window and the SMC fabric read of `SCRATCH[11]` (`0xC00390D8`, `smc_addr.h`) captures op 0 with the `0x5C5C0B01` written before. The external slice reads ovrd 1 with val 0 while the control leg drives it (bit 2 = 0, then bit 1 = 0) and ovrd 0 after release; `sep_reset_n_o` reads 1 throughout.
* Test-Logic-Reset: with `reset_hold` 1 five TMS-high TCKs return `sep_reset_n_o` to 1 and the capture to all ones, the scratch word reads 0 and a new word `0x5EC02D5E` reads back; with `reset_hold` 0 (`architecture.adoc`, `clk_rst.adoc`) the override survives, `sep_reset_n_o` reads 0 on 400 samples and the capture clears bits 0, 13 and 14 only.
* Release: reset_enable first (bit 13 = 1) returns `sep_reset_n_o` to 1, it stays 1 on 200 samples after reset_control (bit 14 = 1), the capture clears only bit 0, the scratch word reads 0 and `0x5EC03D5E` reads back.
* Gate and sensing: `lc_state_o` leaves its pre-sense value and `lcc_dbg_disable_o` reads 0 inside 2000 polls of 50 `clk_smu_i` cycles before the SEP STAP is selected.
* Measured and logged only: the `sep_trace_valid_o` 1-sample counts before and during the hold, the fall and rise latencies of `sep_reset_n_o`, the 1-sample count of `obs_sep_rst_n_o` over the hold window, and the TCK period.
* The SEP-internal sibling reset ports and the warm scratch bank are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, read IDCODE, wait for the lifecycle posture, read `JTAG_CAPS`, scan `IC_RESET` with the reset image ahead of 32 extra bits and write `SCRATCH[11]` through the SMC fabric `SINGLE_OP` with `0x5C5C0B01`, polling the capture.
. Select the SEP STAP, set `dmcontrol.dmactive`, write `0x5EC01D5E` to SEP cold scratch word 0 over the system bus and read it back, then sample `sep_reset_n_o`, `obs_sep_clk_o` and `sep_trace_valid_o` over 2000 cycles.
. With no override engaged drive five TMS-high TCKs, re-select the SEP STAP, set `dmactive` and read the scratch word over the system bus.
. Update `IC_RESET` with bit 14 = 0 and bit 13 = 1, sample `sep_reset_n_o` over 200 cycles and read the scratch word over the system bus.
. Update `IC_RESET` with bits 13 and 14 = 0, wait for `sep_reset_n_o` to read 0 and sample the hold window of 2000 cycles.
. Scan `IC_RESET` with the held image, sample `jtag_ic_reset_ext_o`, `rst_primary_smc_clk_no` over 500 cycles and read `SCRATCH[11]` through the SMC fabric `SINGLE_OP`, polling the capture.
. Drive five TMS-high TCKs with `reset_hold` 1, wait for `sep_reset_n_o` to read 1, scan `IC_RESET`, re-select the SEP STAP, set `dmactive`, read the scratch word, write `0x5EC02D5E` and read it back.
. Update `IC_RESET` with `reset_hold` 0, then bit 14 = 0, then bit 13 = 0, wait for `sep_reset_n_o` to read 0, drive five TMS-high TCKs, sample 400 cycles and scan `IC_RESET`.
. Release with bit 13 = 1 and wait for `sep_reset_n_o` to read 1, write bit 14 = 1, sample 200 cycles, scan `IC_RESET`, re-select the SEP STAP, set `dmactive`, read the scratch word, write `0x5EC03D5E` and read it back.
. Stage the external control bit, apply the external override, wait for `jtag_ic_reset_ext_o` ovrd to read 1, release with the enable bit then the control bit, wait for ovrd to read 0 and write `reset_hold` 1 back.
. Print the checker lines in step order, check that every wait finished inside its bound with the stated site count per kind, and stamp the pass point after the last step.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-SETUP` |IDCODE reads the documented value, JTAG_CAPS reports 68, 8 and 1 IC_RESET ports, the IC_RESET scan returns a 155-bit all-ones image, the SCRATCH[11] write of 0x5C5C0B01 captures op 0 and dmcontrol.dmactive reads 1 through the SEP STAP
|`CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-POSITION` |update 1 (bit 14 = 0, bit 13 = 1) leaves sep_reset_n_o at 1 and SEP cold scratch word 0 at 0x5EC01D5E; update 2 (bits 13 and 14 = 0) takes sep_reset_n_o to 0; the capture has exactly bits 13 and 14 clear
|`CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-QUIESCE` |cold scratch word 0 reads 0x5EC01D5E over the system bus with sep_reset_n_o at 1 and obs_sep_clk_o at both levels before the override and again after Test-Logic-Reset and a STAP re-select with no override engaged; after the override sep_reset_n_o reads 0 on every one of 2000 hold samples with obs_sep_clk_o at both levels; sep_trace_valid_o counts are measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-ISOLATION` |during the SEP override jtag_ic_reset_ext_o reads ovrd 0 and val 1, rst_primary_smc_clk_no reads 1 on every sample of its window and the SMC fabric read of 0xC00390D8 captures op 0 with 0x5C5C0B01; the external control leg drives ovrd 1 and val 0 and releases to ovrd 0
|`CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-HOLD-TLR` |with reset_hold 1 Test-Logic-Reset returns sep_reset_n_o to 1, the capture is all ones, cold scratch word 0 reads 0 and 0x5EC02D5E reads back; with reset_hold 0 sep_reset_n_o stays 0 on 400 samples and the capture clears bits 0, 13 and 14
|`CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-RELEASE` |reset_enable written 1 first returns sep_reset_n_o to 1, it stays 1 on 200 samples after reset_control is written 1, the capture clears only bit 0, cold scratch word 0 reads 0 and 0x5EC03D5E reads back
|`CHK-NONVAC` |ordered sim-time fence S1<...<S11<PASS, the pass point is stamped after the last step and every step S1 to S10 has a nonzero scan count
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the sensing wait, the capture polls, the reset and external-output waits, the system-bus completion polls and the dmi retry loops finish inside their bounds with a recorded last state and the stated site count per kind
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-175-smu_dtp_jtag2axi_ratio_floor_test]]
===== 175 `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_ratio_floor_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_ratio_floor_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_ratio_floor_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP, a fresh master per leg at TCK periods of 40, 48, 32, 20 and 40 ns; instructions `IDCODE`, `SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL` and `SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR`); the bench taps `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` (the gate), `dtp_smc_dbg_aw_count_o`, `dtp_smc_dbg_ar_count_o` and `dtp_smc_dbg_b_count_o` (the launch counters at the SMU boundary) and the `clk_smu_i` edges that measure the system clock period (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`); `+pll_sys_period_ns=10`; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC and SEP shadow preloads and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the SMC scratch word addresses come from the generated `smc_addr.h`

*Objective*: the SMC fabric JTAG2AXI bridge completes a series write and single-load series reads with correct data and status at TCK to `clk_smu_i` ratios 4.0 and 4.8 and again at 4.0 after a `SERIES_CTRL.reset` that follows the 3.2 and 2.0 legs, and the behaviour at 3.2 and 2.0 is measured against the rule that `SERIES_CTRL.op` is 0 or 3.

*Test Coverage*:

* Setup: `clk_smu_i` measures 10 ns from two rising edges (`doc/integrator/src/smu-composition.adoc`, system clock settings) and no TCK to `clk_smu_i` floor is set because the test owns the ratio, both logged; `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` reads 0 inside 400 polls of 50 `clk_smu_i` cycles before the first scan, `SERIES_CTRL.reset` (bit 62) leaves `op` 0 (`hw/ip/jtag/jtag_ptap/doc/architecture.adoc`, `*_AXI_SERIES_CTRL`) and the counter baselines are sampled.
* Ratio 4.0 and 4.8: a write of four 32-bit words (`SERIES_DATA_INCR`, `architecture.adoc`) to `SCRATCH_COLD_WARM[0..3]` at `0xC0002880` (`smc_addr.h`) raises the AW and B counters by 4, `SERIES_CTRL.op`, captured with `nop` control scans, leaves 3 within 16 captures and the B counter shows four completions within 64 polls of 4 cycles; each word is then read with one `SERIES_CTRL` read load and two data scans and capture 2 equals the word written (`0x40400001` to `0x40400004` at TCK 40 ns, `0x48480001` to `0x48480004` at 48 ns), with `op` 0 after every load (`doc/integrator/src/smu-dtp.adoc`, TCK 10 MHz to 50 MHz against a 100 MHz system clock). The AR delta and the `pl_depth` field capture are measured and logged only.
* Ratio 3.2 and 2.0: the same series with words `0x32320001` and `0x20200001` onward is run at TCK 32 ns and 20 ns. `SERIES_CTRL.op` after every load, the read-back words, the counter deltas, the retry count (64 Run-Test/Idle TCKs per retry, at most 4, applied to every capture that reads 3) and the TCK count per operation are measured and logged only (no minimum ratio is documented in `doc/integrator/src/smu-dtp.adoc` or `hw/sys/dtp/doc/clk_rst.adoc`). The one compare is the op code rule: every capture is 0 or 3 and never 1 or 2, because 1 and 2 report a failed response and 3 reports an operation attempted before the previous completed (`architecture.adoc`); when no capture reads 3 the checker reports the 3 branch as NOT-EXERCISED.
* Recovery: `SERIES_CTRL.reset` at TCK 40 ns reads `op` 0 on the next capture (the reset clears the sticky code, `architecture.adoc`) and the series of words `0x4A4A0001` to `0x4A4A0004` completes with equal read-backs, `op` 0 and the AW and B counters up by 4.
* The OTP bridges, TCK ratios outside these five, other series kinds and pipeline depths are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Measure the `clk_smu_i` period, reset the TAP at TCK 40 ns, read IDCODE, wait for `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` to read 0, sample the counter baselines and write `SERIES_CTRL.reset`.
. At TCK 40 ns on a fresh master write the four-word series, wait for `op` to leave 3 with `nop` control scans and the B counter to show four completions, read the four words in the single-load form and capture `op`.
. Repeat step 2 at TCK 48 ns with the words `0x48480001` onward.
. Repeat step 2 at TCK 32 ns with the words `0x32320001` onward, capturing `op` after every word with up to four retries, as measured only.
. Repeat step 4 at TCK 20 ns with the words `0x20200001` onward.
. Compare that every `op` capture of the 3.2 and 2.0 legs is 0 or 3.
. Return TCK to 40 ns on a fresh master, write `SERIES_CTRL.reset`, capture `op` and repeat step 2 with the words `0x4A4A0001` onward.
. Print the checker lines in step order, check that every wait finished inside its bound with the stated site count per kind, and stamp the pass point after the last step.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-RATIO-SETUP` |the clk_smu_i period and the absent TCK floor are logged, the gate tap reads 0 before the first scan, SERIES_CTRL.reset leaves op 0 and the counter baselines are sampled
|`CHK-DBG-RATIO-COMPLETE-4P0` |at TCK 40 ns the four-word series write raises the AW and B counters by 4 and the four single-load series reads return the four words with op 0 after every load; the AR delta is measured and logged only
|`CHK-DBG-RATIO-COMPLETE-4P8` |at TCK 48 ns the four-word series write raises the AW and B counters by 4 and the four single-load series reads return the four words with op 0 after every load
|`CHK-DBG-RATIO-OP3-RULE` |every SERIES_CTRL.op capture of the 3.2 and 2.0 legs is 0 or 3 and never 1 or 2, and the integration legs read 0; a leg that never reads 3 is reported as NOT-EXERCISED for that branch
|`CHK-DBG-RATIO-RECOVERY` |after SERIES_CTRL.reset at TCK 40 ns op reads 0 and a new four-word series write and four single-load reads complete with equal data, op 0 after every load and the AW and B counters up by 4
|`CHK-NONVAC` |ordered sim-time fence S1<...<S8<PASS, the pass point is stamped after the last step and every scanning step has a nonzero access count
|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the gate wait, the op polls, the B counter waits and the two retry loops finish inside their bounds with a recorded last state and the stated site count per kind
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

[[sec-176-smu_dtp_smc_dm_dmi_test]]
===== 176 `smu_dtp_smc_dm_dmi_test`

*Source Module*: `smu_dtp_smc_dm_dmi_test` (`cocotb_wrapper/tests/`; the sequence `smu_dtp_smc_dm_dmi_test_seq` in `cocotb_wrapper/seq_lib/`)

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (the primary TAP, driven at the TCK period the bench draws for the seed and logs; instructions `IDCODE`, `TAP_3DCR` and `SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP`; the SMC STAP selected through the PTAP `TAP_3DCR`, SIB and STAP 3DCR scans, with its debug transport module registers `dtmcs` (IR 0x10) and `dmi` (IR 0x11) and the debug module registers `dmcontrol`, `dmstatus`, `sbcs`, `sbaddress0`, `sbaddress1` and `sbdata0`); the bench taps `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` (the gate), `dtp_smc_dbg_aw_count_o`, `dtp_smc_dbg_ar_count_o` and `dtp_smc_dbg_b_count_o` (the launch counters at the SMU boundary), `tb_stap_smc_tms`, `tb_stap_smc_tck` and `tb_stap_smc_tdo_oen` (the SMC STAP host interface) and `jtag_tms` (`tb/tb_wrapper_top.sv`), sampled on `clk_smu_i` and `clk_ref_i`; `elaboration` firmware mode under the `sep_rtl` run mode; `+skip_fuse_sense` with the default SMC and SEP shadow preloads and the `+rom_hex`, `+smc_efuse_hex`, `+sep_efuse_hex`, `+sep_boot_rom_hex`, `+sep_itcm_hex` and `+sep_dtcm_hex` hex images; the `RESET_CTRL` and `SCRATCH` addresses come from the generated `smc_addr.h`

*Objective*: the SMC debug module, held in reset by `CPU_CTRL.RESET_CTRL`, is released by a fabric write that sets bit 24, answers `dmcontrol`, `dmstatus` and `sbcs` with the documented capability values and carries a system-bus write and read of `SCRATCH[15]` that the fabric bridge reads back and that does not move the DTP-to-SMC launch counters.

*Test Coverage*:

* Setup: `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` reads 0 inside 400 polls of 50 `clk_smu_i` cycles, IDCODE reads the documented value and the counter baselines are sampled. While the SMC STAP is unselected `tb_stap_smc_tms` does not follow `jtag_tms` on a fabric scan; after the `TAP_3DCR` selection (`hw/ip/jtag/jtag_stap/doc/architecture.adoc`, the chain order io, smc, sep, extra0 of `doc/integrator/src/smu-composition.adoc`) it follows it on every TCK. The `tb_stap_smc_tdo_oen` TCK counts are measured and logged only.
* Held: the fabric read of `RESET_CTRL` (`0xC0039020`, `smc_addr.h`) reads bit 24 (`debug_reset_n`, active low) 0 (`hw/sys/smc/regs/blocks/cpu_ctrl/cpu_ctrl.rdl`; `hw/sys/smc/doc/cpu.adoc`, debug-module reset); the core `reset_n` bits 3:0 and `uncore_reset_n` bit 8 are logged. The dmi write of `dmcontrol` and the two dmi reads with the module held complete inside the retry bound and their status and data are measured and logged only; the IDCODE scanned after them equals the first.
* Release: the fabric write of the word read while held with bit 24 set captures op 0 with the AW and B counters each up by 1; the read back shows bit 24 = 1 and bits 8:0 equal to the held word, against the 0 read before (`cpu_ctrl.rdl`, writing 1 releases the debug module).
* Capabilities: `dtmcs` (5-bit IR, RISC-V External Debug Support 0.13, `cpu.adoc` Debug Module Configuration) gives `abits`, which sizes every dmi scan and is logged with `version` and `idle`. After the release the dmi write of `dmcontrol` = 1 reads back `dmactive` 1; `dmstatus` reads `version` 2, `authenticated` 1, `hasresethaltreq` 1, `impebreak` 1 and `confstrptrvalid` 0; `sbcs` reads `sbversion` 1, `sbasize` 56, `sbaccess8`, `sbaccess16`, `sbaccess32` and `sbaccess64` 1 and `sbaccess128` 0 (`cpu.adoc`, SMC Debug Module capability readbacks), each field compared separately. A busy dmi status clears with `dtmcs.dmireset` and more idle TCKs before the access retries.
* System bus: the fabric write of `0x0ECCA114` (size 2) to `SCRATCH[14]` (`0xC00390F0`) precedes the system-bus write of `0x5BA0F15E` to `SCRATCH[15]` (`0xC00390F8`), assembled from `sbaddress1` and `sbaddress0` with `sbaccess` 2 and started by the `sbdata0` write, `sbbusy` polled to 0 with `sberror` and `sbbusyerror` 0 (`cpu.adoc`, debug system bus through `fbus`). The fabric read of `0xC00390F8` returns op 0 and `0x5BA0F15E`; the system-bus reads (`sbreadonaddr` 1) of `0xC00390F0` first and `0xC00390F8` second return `0x0ECCA114` and `0x5BA0F15E` (`cpu_ctrl.rdl`, SCRATCH reset 0).
* Path: the AW, AR and B counters rise by exactly 1 for the fabric write (AW and B) and the fabric read (AR) and stay flat across the system-bus write and both system-bus reads (`doc/integrator/src/smu-composition.adoc`, the debug system bus is the cluster path and the fabric bridge is the DTP-to-SMC debug AXI port).
* Measured and logged only: the TCK period, the counter baselines, the `dtmcs` fields, the dmi status and data while the module is held, the first `sbbusy` poll of each system-bus access and the `tb_stap_smc_tdo_oen` TCK counts.
* The SMC cores, abstract commands, program buffer and the SEP debug module are out of scope.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Reset the TAP, read IDCODE, wait for `tb_smc_jtag2axi_security_disable` to read 0 and sample the AW, AR and B counters.
. Read `RESET_CTRL` through the SMC fabric `SINGLE_OP`, polling the capture, with the SMC STAP unselected and `tb_stap_smc_tms` observed.
. Select the SMC STAP through the three `TAP_3DCR` scans.
. Scan `dtmcs` through the network with `tb_stap_smc_tms` observed and keep `abits` and `idle`.
. Write `dmcontrol` = 1 and read `dmcontrol` and `dmstatus` through dmi with the module held, then scan IDCODE.
. Write the held `RESET_CTRL` word with bit 24 set through the fabric `SINGLE_OP` and read it back.
. Clear the dmi status with `dtmcs.dmireset`, write `dmcontrol` = 1 and read it back.
. Read `dmstatus` and `sbcs` through dmi.
. Write `SCRATCH[14]` through the fabric `SINGLE_OP`, sample the counters, set `sbcs`, `sbaddress1`, `sbaddress0` and `sbdata0` through dmi and poll `sbcs` until `sbbusy` reads 0.
. Read `SCRATCH[15]` through the fabric `SINGLE_OP`, sample the counters, set `sbcs` with `sbreadonaddr`, write `sbaddress0` with `0xC00390F0` and then `0xC00390F8`, poll `sbbusy` and read `sbdata0` after each, then sample the counters.
. Print the checker lines in step order, check that every wait finished inside its bound with the stated site count per kind, and stamp the pass point after the last step.

*Checkers*:

[cols="1,2",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SETUP` |the gate tap reads 0 before the first scan, IDCODE reads the documented value, the launch counter baselines are sampled, tb_stap_smc_tms stays parked while the SMC STAP is unselected and follows jtag_tms edge for edge after TAP_3DCR with no TCK mismatch
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-RESET-HELD` |RESET_CTRL reads bit 24 = 0 and its bits 3:0 and bit 8 are logged; the dmi write and two dmi reads while the debug module is held complete inside the retry bound with their values measured and logged only; the IDCODE after them equals the first
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-RELEASE` |the RESET_CTRL write with bit 24 set captures op 0 with the AW and B counters each up by 1 and the read back has bit 24 = 1 with bits 8:0 equal to the value read while held
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-DMACTIVE` |after the release the dmi write of dmcontrol = 1 reads back dmactive = 1 inside the retry bound
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-DMSTATUS` |the dtmcs scan completes and its abits size the dmi scans; dmstatus reads version 2, authenticated 1, hasresethaltreq 1, impebreak 1 and confstrptrvalid 0, each compared separately
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBCS` |sbcs reads sbversion 1, sbasize 56, sbaccess8, sbaccess16, sbaccess32 and sbaccess64 1 and sbaccess128 0, each compared separately
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBA-WRITE` |the 32-bit system-bus write of 0x5BA0F15E to SCRATCH[15] (0xC00390F8) completes with sbbusy 0, sberror 0 and sbbusyerror 0 inside the poll bound
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBA-READ` |the fabric read of 0xC00390F8 captures op 0 with 0x5BA0F15E, the system-bus read of SCRATCH[14] (0xC00390F0) returns 0x0ECCA114 written over the fabric first and the system-bus read of 0xC00390F8 then returns 0x5BA0F15E, with sbbusy, sberror and sbbusyerror 0 after each
|`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBA-PATH` |the DTP-to-SMC AW, AR and B counters stay flat across the system-bus write and both system-bus reads and rise by exactly 1 for each of the fabric write of 0x0ECCA114 and the fabric read of 0xC00390F8
|`CHK-NONVAC` |ordered sim-time fence S1<...<S11<PASS, the pass point is stamped after the last step and every step S1 to S10 has a nonzero scan count
