|`CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS` |the gate wait, the five fabric op capture polls, the sixteen dmi retry loops and the three sbbusy polls finish inside their bounds with a recorded last state and the stated site count per kind
|===

*Pass Criteria*: every checker above holds and the scoreboard reports zero errors. Enrolled in: `all`, `hosted`.

'''''

=== Formal Verification Plan

No formal target exists for SMU. There is no `smu_formal_cfg.toml`, no
`testlists/formal.toml` and no `formal/` directory under `hw/sys/smu/dv/`, and
`run_dv.py --validate-configs` lists no `smu (formal)` view. The only formal
target in the tree is the DTP primary-TAP controller
(`hw/sys/dtp/dv/docs/DTP_VPLAN.adoc`, "Formal Verification Plan"); it proves
`jtag_tap_ctrlr` with `dtp` as the formal top and is DTP block-level evidence,
not SMU integration evidence. Every row of this plan is simulation credit.

=== Regression Groups

`testlists/all.toml` (`--dut smu`, the wrapper):

[cols="2,1,4",options="header",]
|===
|Group |Leaves |Notes
|`fw_boot` |4 |The firmware boot smokes, the SMC firmware fabric leaf and the elaboration leaf; needs the RISC-V toolchain, so no workflow schedules it
|`elaboration` |1 |The elaboration leaf alone
|`fabric` |19 |The fabric and filter leaves; every member is in `hosted`
|`smc_under_smu` |23 |The SMC-facing leaves reached through the SMU; every member is in `hosted`
|`dtp_under_smu` |39 |The DTP-facing leaves reached through the SMU; every member is in `hosted`
|`smoke` |2 |The PR gate: `sim.yml` runs `--items smoke` on every hardware diff. Toolchain-free by construction
|`hosted` |86 |The toolchain-free nightly and weekly set (`regress.yml`); every leaf uses `elaboration` firmware. The name records the runner class the scheduled tiers run on, not a feature
|`all` |122 |Every test in `testlists/all.toml`; the regression, and the denominator of this plan's wrapper cards
|`sep_real_fw` |14 |Wrapper leaves that boot real SEP DV firmware images
|`sep_entropy` |1 |The firmware-driven entropy stack
|`sep_probe` |1 |The probe leaf that keeps SEP instruction retirement live while the TAP answers
|`sep_chain` |2 |Lifecycle-gated JTAG-to-SMC chain leaves
|`sep_lifecycle` |9 |Wrapper leaves that run one lifecycle state's eFuse image each
|===

`smoke` ⊂ `hosted` ⊂ `all` and `elaboration` ⊂ `fw_boot` ⊂ `all`; `hosted`
and `fw_boot` are disjoint. Every `all` member has a card above, and every
card names the groups its test is enrolled in.

=== Signoff Package

The SMU signoff package is this book plus the tracker: the feature list,
the scope and traceability matrix, the release matrix, the component scope
and the disposition record (`hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`)
hold across candidates, and the tracker's signoff item carries what belongs to
one candidate (the commit, the run trees it was qualified with, the open
issues and their release dispositions). Feature credit is the
<<smu-scope-traceability,Scope and Traceability>> matrix above; the feature
list it grades against follows.

[[smu-feature-list]]
==== SMU hosted feature list

This chapter defines the hosted feature subset F1–F7 and maps every enrolled
test to one feature and one `TEST_EVIDENCE` token. The F1–F7 numbering is
defined by the table below and nowhere else. The hardware facts the features describe are in
`doc/integrator/src/smu.adoc`.

The executable contract is `testlists/all.toml`. Feature credit is
the enrolled `hosted` group. Names in `hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`
and SEP=1 work are OUT.

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Field |Value
|Feature numbering |F1–7, defined by the table in this chapter
|Feature-credit group |`hosted` (`--dut smu`)
|PR smoke |`smoke` (`smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test`, `smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test`)
|Evidence map |`cocotb/env/smu_evidence_map.py` `TEST_EVIDENCE`
|OUT catalog |`hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`
|===

[[smu-feature-subset]]
===== Hosted subset

Features 1–7, scoped to `--dut smu` on its compile profile `compile_smu_chiplet`
(the wrapper with the real SEP). The SEP=0 composition is OUT: no target of
this package elaborates it, and its proofs are `not-runnable` in the
disposition record.

[cols="1,2,2,3,3",options="header",]
|===
|ID |SPEC feature |Scope |What is in |What stays OUT
|F1 |SMC boot and firmware execution |IN (observe) |Bring-up, reset release, mailbox CSR, WDT, GPIO strap, OCTS free-run |SMC CPU firmware boot, ROM regression
|F2 |Real SEP RTL under the SMU |OUT |— |SEP boot and firmware execution as credit: `smu_sep_smoke_test` retires SEP instructions on the wrapper, but it boots SEP firmware, so it needs the RISC-V toolchain and sits in `fw_boot` / `all`, outside `hosted`; observe-only
|F3 |DTP debug and test access |IN |PTAP IDCODE/BYPASS, JTAG2AXI, OTP MAP, STAP, BSR select, boot-stall, IC_RESET, SEP DMI `IR=5'h11` |Closed JTAG2AXI security gate (the default SEP eFuse preload leaves debug enabled), lifecycle policy, iJTAG model depth
|F4 |AXI crossbar fabric |IN |External-port ID conversion through the crossbar, filters, ATOP reject, alias remap, fabric BAR |The crossbar routing matrix beyond the SMC aperture; the SEP=0 shape (direct ID converters, `smu_axi_xbar` absent), which needs `smu #(.CFG(smu_pkg::NoSepCfg))`, an elaboration this package does not build
|F5 |SMC↔SEP interoperability |OUT |— |Mailbox challenge-response and SEP→SMC notify (`smu_sep_bidirect_test`, `smu_sep_smc_notify_test`): SEP DV firmware leaves, which need the RISC-V toolchain and sit in `sep_real_fw` / `all`, outside `hosted`; observe-only
|F6 |Cross-trigger and clock-stop |IN (pin-level) |Clock-stop coordination, product-pin CTM remap, illegal-phase corners |Four-phase CTM handshake / CLA loop (no product pin or frontdoor for CLA feedback or the CTM handshake)
|F7 |Lifecycle and security |OUT |— |The SEP=0 demote / `lc_state` observe (`smu_smc_security_demote_pm_test`, needs `smu #(.CFG(smu_pkg::NoSepCfg))`, which this package does not build); `feat_ctrl` matrices and lifecycle policy: the lifecycle stimulus is SEP firmware (`smu_sep_lcc_flow_test`, the `sep_lifecycle` and `sep_chain` groups), which needs the RISC-V toolchain and sits outside `hosted`
|===

|`smu_dtp_sep_ic_reset_effect_test` |F3 |hosted |`CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-SETUP`, `CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-POSITION`, `CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-QUIESCE`, `CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-ISOLATION`, `CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-HOLD-TLR`, `CHK-DBG-IC-SEP-RELEASE`, `CHK-NONVAC`, `CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS`
|`smu_dtp_jtag2axi_ratio_floor_test` |F3 |hosted |`CHK-DBG-RATIO-SETUP`, `CHK-DBG-RATIO-COMPLETE-4P0`, `CHK-DBG-RATIO-COMPLETE-4P8`, `CHK-DBG-RATIO-OP3-RULE`, `CHK-DBG-RATIO-RECOVERY`, `CHK-NONVAC`, `CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS`
|`smu_dtp_smc_dm_dmi_test` |F3 |hosted |`CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SETUP`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-RESET-HELD`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-RELEASE`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-DMACTIVE`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-DMSTATUS`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBCS`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBA-WRITE`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBA-READ`, `CHK-DBG-SMC-DM-SBA-PATH`, `CHK-NONVAC`, `CHK-TIMEOUT-PATHS`
|===

[[smu-out-and-wrapper]]
===== OUT names and the wrapper

* Every name in `hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc` is OUT for this subset,
  and none of them has a `TEST_EVIDENCE` row: the map holds rows only for
  names with a test body in this tree.
* `--dut smu` is the wrapper bench and carries `hosted` itself; PASSes are
  labelled `DUT_TAG=WRAPPER`.
* Wrapper elaboration / reset is `smu_wrapper_elaboration_test`
  (wrapper `fw_boot` and `elaboration`).

[[smu-release-matrix]]
=== Release Qualification

This section defines the mandatory qualification set: the CI matrix in
`.github/workflows/sim.yml` and `regress.yml`. `--dut smu` is the wrapper
bench and its `hosted` is the mandatory job.

[[smu-release-combinations]]
==== Mandatory combinations

[cols="2,2,1,3",options="header",]
|===
|Tier |Command |Seeds |Blocks
|PR |`--dut smu --tool verilator --items smoke` |1 |PR merge (`sim.yml`)
|Nightly |`--dut smu --tool verilator --items hosted` |1 |release qualification (`regress.yml`)
|Weekly |`--dut smu --tool verilator --items hosted` |1 |Dashboard (`dashboard.yml` publishes the weekly); collects no coverage (`coverage: false`); same group as nightly
|===

Nightly and weekly each run one seed (`reseed: 1`). A red nightly is not a PR merge gate
(`regress.yml` says so) but it blocks release qualification.

`smu` `smoke` members: `smu_dtp_jtag_smoke_test`, `smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test`,
both toolchain-free. The firmware smokes (`smu_wrapper_elaboration_test`,
`smu_smc_smoke_test`, `smu_sep_smoke_test`) are `fw_boot`, which needs the
toolchain container and is not a CI tier.

[[smu-release-decisions]]
==== Decisions

* `smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test` is in `hosted` and `smoke`.
  It is not a member of `fw_boot`.
* Feature credit is the enrolled `hosted` set on `--dut smu`. The SEP=1
  firmware groups and the names in `hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc` are OUT.

[[smu-hosted-component-signoff]]
=== Component Signoff

This section defines the hosted component scope. Feature credit is the
enrolled `hosted` set, which runs on `--dut smu` — the wrapper, whose
PASSes carry `DUT_TAG=WRAPPER`. The names in
`hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc` are OUT.

[[smu-signoff-jtag2axi-otp-filter]]
==== JTAG2AXI / OTP / filter depth

The JTAG2AXI, OTP and filter depth tests are enrolled in `hosted`:

* JTAG2AXI: `smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_smoke_test`,
  `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_smc_rw_matrix_test`,
  `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_smc_error_path_test`,
  `smu_dtp_jtag_smc_cpu_register_test`,
  `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_wstrb_partial_sticky_test`,
  `smu_dtp_jtag2axi_back_to_back_error_ok_test`,
  `smu_jtag2axi_vs_smn_same_csr_race_test`
* OTP: `smu_dtp_otp_smc_map_rw_test`,
  `smu_dtp_otp_smc_series_error_test`,
  `smu_dtp_otp_smc_complete_rw_test`,
  `smu_otp_vs_fabric_map_race_test`,
  `smu_lc_sigint_fail_closed_test`
* Filter: `smu_sys_in_filter_program_jtag_test`,
  `smu_sys_in_filter_window_edge_test`,
  `smu_sys_in_filter_reprogram_shrink_test`,
  `smu_axi_filter_allow_ns_test`,
  `smu_axi_filter_in_instance_matrix_test`,
  `smu_axi_filter_out_instance_matrix_test`

`smu_smc_dtp_jtag2axi_security_test`,
`smu_hier_ctn_vs_jtag2axi_concurrent_test`,
`smu_feat_ctrl_flip_mid_jtag2axi_test`,
`smu_dtp_otp_debug_access_test`,
`smu_dtp_otp_sep0_err_slv_test`, and the other `feat_ctrl` / OTP names
that need the SEP=1 lifecycle controller have OUT rows in
`hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`, as do
`smu_dtp_jtag2axi_abort_mid_op_test` (needs an OTP interface that holds
SINGLE_OP in BUSY) and `smu_dtp_ptap_otp_instr_scan_test` (needs the SEP=0
OTP error slave); both need `smu #(.CFG(smu_pkg::NoSepCfg))`, which this package does not
build.

[[smu-signoff-atop-reject-filter-allow]]
==== ATOP reject and filter-allow

Filter-allow positive control is enrolled:
`smu_axi_filter_allow_ns_test` emits `CHK-SMU-ALLOW-NS-S1/S2/S3`;
the SYS_IN program / window / shrink leaves emit `AXI_FILTER_OKAY`
via `CHK-AXI-FILTER-OKAY`.

`smu_axi_atomic_operation_test` proves the supported class only
(`AXI_NON_ATOP_OK`, RID==ARID). The TB ties
`smu_axi_in_req.aw.atop = 0`; there is no legal ATOP driver on this
DUT. ATOP reject / err_slv poison is **OUT** for the hosted set: a negative check
needs a positive control, and no legal ATOP stimulus exists on this DUT.
A green atomic test is not ATOP non-support credit.

[[smu-signoff-smc-under-smu]]
==== SMC-under-SMU

Release-group (`hosted`) SMC-under-SMU names:

`smu_smc_reset_ctrl_test`,
`smu_system_timer_octs_test`, `smu_i3c_mem_port_connectivity_test`,
`smu_smc_wdt_sanity_test`, `smu_smc_wdt_timeout_irq_test`,
`smu_smc_mailbox_sanity_test`. The SEP=0 composition and demote observes
(`smu_no_sep_configuration_test`, `smu_smc_security_demote_pm_test`) are OUT:
they need `smu #(.CFG(smu_pkg::NoSepCfg))`, which this package does not build, and are
`not-runnable` in `hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`.

`smu_smc_smoke_test` -- the SMC ROM boot to PASS on the wrapper -- is not in
`hosted`. It is enrolled in `fw_boot` and `all`, which need the SMC
firmware compile; neither `sim.yml` nor `regress.yml` runs either group, so it
carries no release credit until one of them is scheduled.

`smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test` and `smu_smc_mailbox_int_test` are in
`hosted` (the former also in `smoke`).

eFuse / burn under SMU is OUT (SEP=1 / child-TB).
The GPIO strap feature has no testcase in this package (`smc_wrapper` declares
no `captured_straps_i`); it is not release credit.

[[smu-signoff-dtp-under-smu]]
==== DTP-under-SMU

JTAG smoke, boot-stall, IC_RESET, clock-stop, STAP, BSR, illegal-phase CTM
and product-pin CTM remap (`smu_xtrig_ctm_remap_test`) are the enrolled
DTP-under-SMU set in `hosted`. Block-level DTP signoff is the DTP package.

`feat_ctrl` scenarios that need the SEP=1 lifecycle controller are OUT in
`hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc`.

[[smu-signoff-hosted-fabric]]
==== Hosted fabric

ID-width, crossbar error, prot decode, alias remap, and fabric BAR are
checked through `ocah_axi_vip`
(`cocotb/seq_lib/smu_axi_helpers.py`). Filter-allow and ATOP reject are
covered above. SEP=1 3×3 xbar receives no release credit.

[[smu-known-limitations]]
=== Known Limitations

* *`all` is not a scheduled group.* `.github/workflows/regress.yml` runs
  `hosted` nightly and weekly: the rest of `all` is the SEP firmware set,
  whose `c_build` stages need the RISC-V toolchain container, which the
  hosted runners do not have. Those leaves run only where a developer or a
  runner with the container runs `--items all`; the sign-off run of record
  is such a run, not a workflow run.
* *The wrapper is the surface.* `--dut smu` is the wrapper bench and carries
  `hosted` itself; its PASSes are labelled `DUT_TAG=WRAPPER` and are the
  density evidence. The SEP=0 composition (`smu #(.CFG(smu_pkg::NoSepCfg))`) has no target in
  this package, so nothing here verifies it; its proofs are `not-runnable` in
  the disposition record.
* *Deferred scenarios are not cards.* Names classified deferred or OUT --
  including the SEP-driven SMC bring-up images that poll SRAM no sys-inbound
  master can reach, the JTAG2AXI abort scenario, the SEP=0 composition
  proofs, and the
  asynchronous cold-reset assertion the RTL does not implement -- are
  catalogued in `hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc` with the
  condition each needs, and have no entry in this plan or in any testlist.
* *Cross-trigger and iJTAG have no OCAH-local model*, so the coverage those
  interfaces get is what the DTP-under-SMU tests reach through JTAG and the
  bench's pad loopbacks.
* *Functional coverage closes at 100 percent of its Phase 1 population
  and no CI gate enforces it.* The phases, the collectors and what this plan
  may claim from a database are in <<smu-dv-functional-coverage-plan>>.
* *Verilator replaces the `ocah_prim` `prim_sync3`
  cell with the behavioural stand-in in
  `hw/sys/smc/dv/tb/verilator_stubs/`.* VCS drops it. Xcelium receives
  both files; which of the pair it elaborates is not established. The
  leaves that observe a crossing are named in <<smu-vplan-bench-standins>>.

[[smu-vplan-bench-standins]]
==== Bench stand-ins

The `prim_sync3` file listed in `smu_sim_cfg.toml`
`[build].stubs` is a product-module stand-in, not a tooling shim. It
elaborates ahead of the Bender filelist so Verilator's `-Wno-MODDUP`
first-definition-wins selects it; when `tool == "vcs"` the
runner drops any stub whose basename the Bender graph supplies, and the
product cell elaborates. Xcelium receives both files like Verilator does, and
this bench does not pin which of the pair it elaborates; a comparison against
the product cell belongs to a VCS run, where the runner drops the stub. The
behaviour the stand-in
adds (`initial '0` on
each stage), the leaves that observe a crossing
(`obs_powergood_stable_o`, `obs_powergood_stable_low_seen_o`, the
reset-release observes of `smu_smc_reset_ctrl_test` and
`smu_ext_boot_seq_gate_test`, and the boot-stall gate of the five
boot-stall leaves), and the condition that closes the entry are the
`prim_sync3` stand-in row of
`hw/sys/smu/dv/docs/SMU_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION.adoc` (*Bench stand-ins and
exceptions*).
